(() => {
  "use strict";

  const g = globalThis;

  // Prevent double-definition if AI Dungeon re-runs the Library tab.
  if (g.AIDRPG && g.AIDRPG.__shellReady === true) {
    return;
  }

  g.AIDRPG = {
    __shellReady: true,
    VERSION: "0.25.0-chunk25",
    BUILD_STAGE: "chunk25-major-actor-memory",

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------
    // CORE SHELL HANDLERS
    // These are intentionally minimal for Chunk 1.
    // Later chunks will expand them module-by-module.
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------
    onInput(text) {
  const runtime = this.RuntimeGate.validate("input", text);
  if (!runtime.ok) {
    return this.RuntimeGate.failSoft("input", runtime, text);
  }

  this.CoreState.init();
  this.PlayerSystem.init();
  this.BuildIdentitySystem.init();
  this.ProgressionSystem.init();
  this.InventorySystem.init();
  this.SceneStateSystem.init();
  this.WorldEntitySystem.init();
  this.TimeTravelSystem.init();
  this.ReputationSystem.init();
  this.QuestLogSystem.init();
  this.CardSyncSystem.init();
  this.AbilitySystem.init();
  this.PersistentTraitSystem.init();
  this.MajorActorMemorySystem.init();
  this.ContextPacketSystem.init();

  const rawText = runtime.text;
  this.TurnEngine.beginHook("input", rawText);

  try {
    const commandResult = this.AdminDebugSystem.handleCommand(rawText);
    if (commandResult) {
      state.aidrpg.pending.inputIntent = null;
      state.aidrpg.pending.abilityCandidate = null;
      return commandResult;
    }

    this.IntentParser.parseInput(rawText);
    this.AbilitySystem.processInput(rawText);
    this.TimeTravelSystem.processInput(rawText);
    return { text: rawText };
  } finally {
    this.TurnEngine.endHook("input");
  }
},

onContext(text) {
  const runtime = this.RuntimeGate.validate("context", text);
  if (!runtime.ok) {
    return this.RuntimeGate.failSoft("context", runtime, text);
  }

  this.CoreState.init();
  this.PlayerSystem.init();
  this.BuildIdentitySystem.init();
  this.ProgressionSystem.init();
  this.InventorySystem.init();
  this.SceneStateSystem.init();
  this.WorldEntitySystem.init();
  this.TimeTravelSystem.init();
  this.ReputationSystem.init();
  this.QuestLogSystem.init();
  this.CardSyncSystem.init();
  this.AbilitySystem.init();
  this.PersistentTraitSystem.init();
  this.SpecializationSystem.init();
  this.ActorProfileSystem.init();
  this.MajorActorMemorySystem.init();
  this.ContextPacketSystem.init();

  const rawText = runtime.text;
  this.TurnEngine.beginHook("context", rawText);

  try {
    return { text: this.ContextPacketSystem.buildContext(rawText) };
  } finally {
    this.TurnEngine.endHook("context", rawText);
  }
},

onOutput(text) {
  const runtime = this.RuntimeGate.validate("output", text);
  if (!runtime.ok) {
    return this.RuntimeGate.failSoft("output", runtime, text);
  }

  this.CoreState.init();
  this.PlayerSystem.init();
  this.BuildIdentitySystem.init();
  this.ProgressionSystem.init();
  this.InventorySystem.init();
  this.SceneStateSystem.init();
  this.WorldEntitySystem.init();
  this.TimeTravelSystem.init();
  this.ReputationSystem.init();
  this.QuestLogSystem.init();
  this.CardSyncSystem.init();
  this.AbilitySystem.init();
  this.PersistentTraitSystem.init();
  this.SpecializationSystem.init();
  this.ActorProfileSystem.init();
  this.MajorActorMemorySystem.init();
  this.ContextPacketSystem.init();

  if (this.ConsequenceParser && typeof this.ConsequenceParser.init === "function") {
    this.ConsequenceParser.init();
  }
  if (this.ValidationSystem && typeof this.ValidationSystem.init === "function") {
    this.ValidationSystem.init();
  }

  const rawText = runtime.text;
  this.TurnEngine.beginHook("output", rawText);

  let result;
  try {
    result = { text: rawText };

    if (this.ConsequenceParser && typeof this.ConsequenceParser.processOutput === "function") {
      this.ConsequenceParser.processOutput(rawText);
    }
    if (this.ValidationSystem && typeof this.ValidationSystem.processOutput === "function") {
      this.ValidationSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    }

    this.PlayerSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.SceneStateSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.WorldEntitySystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.TimeTravelSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.ReputationSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.QuestLogSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.InventorySystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.ProgressionSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.AbilitySystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.PersistentTraitSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.SpecializationSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.ActorProfileSystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.MajorActorMemorySystem.processOutput(rawText);
    this.CardSyncSystem.processOutput(rawText);

    return result;
  } finally {
    const finalText = result && typeof result.text === "string" ? result.text : rawText;
    this.TurnEngine.endHook("output", finalText);
  }
},

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------
    // PERMANENT SECTION MARKERS
    // Future chunks will insert code into these sections.
    // Do not remove these markers.
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------

    // === CORE STATE ===
CoreState: {
  SCHEMA_VERSION: 1,

  init() {
    if (!state.aidrpg || typeof state.aidrpg !== "object" || Array.isArray(state.aidrpg)) {
      state.aidrpg = this.createDefaultState();
      return state.aidrpg;
    }

    const root = state.aidrpg;

    this.ensureRepairBuckets(root);

    const report = this.validateState(root);
    if (!report.ok) {
      this.repairStateIfNeeded(root, report);
    }

    this.ensureTopLevelShape(root);

    try {
      this.migrate(root);
    } catch (error) {
      this.pushQuarantine(root, "migrate", "migration_exception", {
        message: error && error.message ? error.message : String(error),
        version:
          root &&
          root.meta &&
          typeof root.meta.version === "number"
            ? root.meta.version
            : null
      });

      this.ensureTopLevelShape(root);
      root.meta.version = this.SCHEMA_VERSION;
    }

    this.ensureTopLevelShape(root);
    return root;
  },

  createDefaultState() {
    return {
      meta: {
        version: this.SCHEMA_VERSION,
        createdAtTurn: 0,
        currentTurn: 0,
        currentHook: "",
        mode: "normal",
        seed: 0,
        lastAppliedOutputHash: "",
        debug: false,
        dirty: {
          player: false,
          abilities: [],
          items: [],
          actors: [],
          entities: [],
          routes: [],
          reputation: false,
          quests: false,
          time: false,
          cards: []
        }
      },

      player: {},
      build: {},
      abilities: {},
      items: {},
      actors: {},
      world: {},
      time: {},
      reputation: {},
      quests: {},
      pending: {},
      cards: {},
      cache: {},
      logs: {
        recentEvents: [],
        recentParses: [],
        recentErrors: [],
        quarantine: [],
        migrationHistory: []
      }
    };
  },

  ensureTopLevelShape(root) {
    if (!root.meta || typeof root.meta !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.meta)) root.meta = {};
    if (!root.player || typeof root.player !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player)) root.player = {};
    if (!root.build || typeof root.build !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.build)) root.build = {};
    if (!root.abilities || typeof root.abilities !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.abilities)) root.abilities = {};
    if (!root.items || typeof root.items !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.items)) root.items = {};
    if (!root.actors || typeof root.actors !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.actors)) root.actors = {};
    if (!root.world || typeof root.world !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.world)) root.world = {};
    if (!root.time || typeof root.time !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.time)) root.time = {};
    if (!root.reputation || typeof root.reputation !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.reputation)) root.reputation = {};
    if (!root.quests || typeof root.quests !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.quests)) root.quests = {};
    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) root.pending = {};
    if (!root.cards || typeof root.cards !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cards)) root.cards = {};
    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) root.cache = {};
    if (!root.logs || typeof root.logs !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.logs)) root.logs = {};

    const meta = root.meta;

    if (typeof meta.version !== "number") meta.version = this.SCHEMA_VERSION;
    if (typeof meta.createdAtTurn !== "number") meta.createdAtTurn = 0;
    if (typeof meta.currentTurn !== "number") meta.currentTurn = 0;
    if (typeof meta.currentHook !== "string") meta.currentHook = "";
    if (typeof meta.mode !== "string") meta.mode = "normal";
    if (typeof meta.seed !== "number") meta.seed = 0;
    if (typeof meta.lastAppliedOutputHash !== "string") meta.lastAppliedOutputHash = "";
    if (typeof meta.debug !== "boolean") meta.debug = false;

    if (!meta.dirty || typeof meta.dirty !== "object" || Array.isArray(meta.dirty)) {
      meta.dirty = {};
    }

    if (typeof meta.dirty.player !== "boolean") meta.dirty.player = false;
    if (!Array.isArray(meta.dirty.abilities)) meta.dirty.abilities = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(meta.dirty.items)) meta.dirty.items = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(meta.dirty.actors)) meta.dirty.actors = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(meta.dirty.entities)) meta.dirty.entities = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(meta.dirty.routes)) meta.dirty.routes = [];
    if (typeof meta.dirty.reputation !== "boolean") meta.dirty.reputation = false;
    if (typeof meta.dirty.quests !== "boolean") meta.dirty.quests = false;
    if (typeof meta.dirty.time !== "boolean") meta.dirty.time = false;
    if (!Array.isArray(meta.dirty.cards)) meta.dirty.cards = [];

    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentEvents)) root.logs.recentEvents = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentParses)) root.logs.recentParses = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentErrors)) root.logs.recentErrors = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.quarantine)) root.logs.quarantine = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.migrationHistory)) root.logs.migrationHistory = [];
  },

  ensureRepairBuckets(root) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return null;

    if (!root.logs || typeof root.logs !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.logs)) {
      root.logs = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentEvents)) root.logs.recentEvents = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentParses)) root.logs.recentParses = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentErrors)) root.logs.recentErrors = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.quarantine)) root.logs.quarantine = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.migrationHistory)) root.logs.migrationHistory = [];

    return root.logs;
  },

  snapshotForQuarantine(value) {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
    } catch (_error) {
      try {
        return String(value);
      } catch (_error2) {
        return "(unserializable)";
      }
    }
  },

  pushQuarantine(root, kind, reason, payload) {
    const logs = this.ensureRepairBuckets(root);
    if (!logs) return 0;

    logs.quarantine.push({
      turn:
        root &&
        root.meta &&
        typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number"
          ? root.meta.currentTurn
          : 0,
      kind: String(kind || "state"),
      reason: String(reason || "unspecified"),
      payload: this.snapshotForQuarantine(payload)
    });

    while (logs.quarantine.length > 12) {
      logs.quarantine.shift();
    }

    return 1;
  },

  recordMigration(root, entry) {
    const logs = this.ensureRepairBuckets(root);
    if (!logs) return 0;

    const cleanEntry = entry && typeof entry === "object" ? entry : {};

    logs.migrationHistory.push({
      turn:
        root &&
        root.meta &&
        typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number"
          ? root.meta.currentTurn
          : 0,
      from: Number.isFinite(Number(cleanEntry.from)) ? Math.floor(Number(cleanEntry.from)) : 0,
      to: Number.isFinite(Number(cleanEntry.to)) ? Math.floor(Number(cleanEntry.to)) : 0,
      step: String(cleanEntry.step || ""),
      repaired: Array.isArray(cleanEntry.repaired) ? cleanEntry.repaired.slice(0, 12) : []
    });

    while (logs.migrationHistory.length > 20) {
      logs.migrationHistory.shift();
    }

    return 1;
  },

  validateState(root) {
    const issues = [];

    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) {
      issues.push("invalid_root");
      return {
        ok: false,
        issues: issues
      };
    }

    const containers = [
      "meta",
      "player",
      "build",
      "abilities",
      "items",
      "actors",
      "world",
      "time",
      "reputation",
      "quests",
      "pending",
      "cards",
      "cache",
      "logs"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < containers.length; i += 1) {
      const key = containers[i];
      if (!root[key] || typeof root[key] !== "object" || Array.isArray(root[key])) {
        issues.push("bad_container:" + key);
      }
    }

    if (root.meta && typeof root.meta === "object" && !Array.isArray(root.meta)) {
      if (!Number.isFinite(Number(root.meta.version))) {
        issues.push("bad_meta_version");
      }

      if (!root.meta.dirty || typeof root.meta.dirty !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.meta.dirty)) {
        issues.push("bad_meta_dirty");
      }
    }

    return {
      ok: issues.length === 0,
      issues: issues
    };
  },

  repairStateIfNeeded(root, report) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return 0;

    let changes = 0;

    const containers = [
      "meta",
      "player",
      "build",
      "abilities",
      "items",
      "actors",
      "world",
      "time",
      "reputation",
      "quests",
      "pending",
      "cards",
      "cache",
      "logs"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < containers.length; i += 1) {
      const key = containers[i];

      if (!root[key] || typeof root[key] !== "object" || Array.isArray(root[key])) {
        this.pushQuarantine(root, "container", key, root[key]);
        root[key] = {};
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    this.ensureRepairBuckets(root);

    if (!root.meta.dirty || typeof root.meta.dirty !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.meta.dirty)) {
      this.pushQuarantine(root, "meta.dirty", "invalid_dirty_container", root.meta.dirty);
      root.meta.dirty = {};
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(root.meta.version))) {
      this.pushQuarantine(root, "meta.version", "invalid_version", root.meta.version);
      root.meta.version = 0;
      changes += 1;
    } else {
      root.meta.version = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(root.meta.version)));
    }

    if (report && Array.isArray(report.issues) && report.issues.length) {
      this.recordMigration(root, {
        from: root.meta.version,
        to: root.meta.version,
        step: "repair_state",
        repaired: report.issues
      });
    }

    return changes;
  },

  migrate(root) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return 0;

    this.ensureRepairBuckets(root);

    const startingVersion = Number.isFinite(Number(root.meta && root.meta.version))
      ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(root.meta.version)))
      : 0;

    let workingVersion = startingVersion;
    let changes = 0;

    if (workingVersion > this.SCHEMA_VERSION) {
      this.pushQuarantine(root, "meta.version", "future_version_detected", workingVersion);
      workingVersion = this.SCHEMA_VERSION;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (workingVersion < 1) {
      this.ensureTopLevelShape(root);

      workingVersion = 1;
      changes += 1;

      this.recordMigration(root, {
        from: startingVersion,
        to: 1,
        step: "baseline_schema_v1",
        repaired: []
      });
    }

    if (root.meta.version !== this.SCHEMA_VERSION) {
      root.meta.version = this.SCHEMA_VERSION;
      changes += 1;
    }

    return changes;
  }
},

    // === TURN ENGINE ===
TurnEngine: {
  ensureScratch(root) {
    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object") {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    if (!root.pending.turnScratch || typeof root.pending.turnScratch !== "object") {
      root.pending.turnScratch = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(root.pending.recentHookTrace)) {
      root.pending.recentHookTrace = [];
    }

    return root.pending.turnScratch;
  },

  stableHash(value) {
    const str = String(value ?? "");
    let hash = 5381;

    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i += 1) {
      hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) ^ str.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = hash >>> 0;
    }

    return String(hash);
  },

  beginHook(hookName, rawText) {
  const root = state.aidrpg;
  const meta = root.meta;
  const scratch = this.ensureScratch(root);

  meta.currentHook = hookName;

  if (hookName === "input") {
    meta.currentTurn += 1;

    root.pending.turnScratch = {
      turnNumber: meta.currentTurn,
      inputHash: this.stableHash(rawText),
      outputHash: "",
      duplicateOutput: false,
      lastHookStarted: "input"
    };

    if (globalThis.AIDRPG.OperationGuard && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.OperationGuard.startTurn === "function") {
      globalThis.AIDRPG.OperationGuard.startTurn(root);
    }
  } else {
    scratch.lastHookStarted = hookName;
  }

  scratch.hookName = hookName;
  scratch.startedAtTurn = meta.currentTurn;
  scratch.rawTextHash = this.stableHash(rawText);
  scratch.rawTextLength = String(rawText ?? "").length;

  root.pending.recentHookTrace.push({
    hook: hookName,
    turn: meta.currentTurn,
    hash: scratch.rawTextHash
  });

  if (root.pending.recentHookTrace.length > 12) {
    root.pending.recentHookTrace.shift();
  }

  if (hookName === "output") {
    const incomingHash = this.stableHash(rawText);
    scratch.outputHash = incomingHash;
    scratch.duplicateOutput = incomingHash === meta.lastAppliedOutputHash;
  }
},

  endHook(hookName, finalText) {
    const root = state.aidrpg;
    const meta = root.meta;
    const scratch = this.ensureScratch(root);

    if (hookName === "output") {
      const finalHash = this.stableHash(finalText);
      scratch.outputHash = finalHash;
      meta.lastAppliedOutputHash = finalHash;
    }

    scratch.lastHookEnded = hookName;
    meta.currentHook = "";
  },

  isDuplicateCurrentOutput() {
    const root = state.aidrpg;
    if (!root || !root.pending || !root.pending.turnScratch) {
      return false;
    }

    return root.pending.turnScratch.duplicateOutput === true;
  }
},

// === OPERATION GUARD ===
OperationGuard: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return null;

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    if (!root.pending.operationGuard || typeof root.pending.operationGuard !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending.operationGuard)) {
      root.pending.operationGuard = {};
    }

    const guard = root.pending.operationGuard;

    if (!Array.isArray(guard.recentKeys)) guard.recentKeys = [];
    if (typeof guard.currentTurn !== "number") {
      guard.currentTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    }
    if (typeof guard.maxKeys !== "number") guard.maxKeys = 240;
    if (typeof guard.keepTurns !== "number") guard.keepTurns = 6;

    return guard;
  },

  stableSerialize(value) {
    const seen = new WeakSet();

    const normalize = (input) => {
      if (input == null) return null;

      const type = typeof input;

      if (type === "string" || type === "number" || type === "boolean") {
        return input;
      }

      if (Array.isArray(input)) {
        return input.map(normalize);
      }

      if (type === "object") {
        if (seen.has(input)) return "[circular]";
        seen.add(input);

        const out = {};
        const keys = Object.keys(input).sort();
        for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
          const key = keys[i];
          const value = input[key];
          if (typeof value === "function") continue;
          out[key] = normalize(value);
        }
        return out;
      }

      return String(input);
    };

    try {
      return JSON.stringify(normalize(value));
    } catch (_error) {
      try {
        return String(value);
      } catch (_error2) {
        return "";
      }
    }
  },

  makeKey(spec = {}, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const meta = root && root.meta ? root.meta : {};
    const hook = String(spec.hook || meta.currentHook || "");
    const turn = typeof meta.currentTurn === "number" ? meta.currentTurn : 0;
    const kind = String(spec.kind || "operation");
    const target = String(spec.target || "");
    const fingerprintSource = {
      type: String(spec.type || ""),
      sourceId: String(spec.sourceId || ""),
      payload: spec.payload && typeof spec.payload === "object" ? spec.payload : spec.payload ?? null
    };

    const serialized = this.stableSerialize(fingerprintSource);
    const fingerprint =
      A &&
      A.TurnEngine &&
      typeof A.TurnEngine.stableHash === "function"
        ? A.TurnEngine.stableHash(serialized)
        : serialized;

    return [hook, turn, kind, target, fingerprint].join("|");
  },

  startTurn(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const guard = this.init(root);
    if (!guard) return 0;

    const turn = root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    guard.currentTurn = turn;

    return this.prune(root);
  },

  prune(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const guard = this.init(root);
    if (!guard) return 0;

    const currentTurn = typeof guard.currentTurn === "number" ? guard.currentTurn : 0;
    const keepTurns = typeof guard.keepTurns === "number" ? guard.keepTurns : 6;
    const minTurn = currentTurn - keepTurns;

    let removed = 0;

    guard.recentKeys = guard.recentKeys.filter((entry) => {
      const keep = entry && typeof entry === "object" && typeof entry.turn === "number" && entry.turn >= minTurn;
      if (!keep) removed += 1;
      return keep;
    });

    const maxKeys = typeof guard.maxKeys === "number" ? guard.maxKeys : 240;
    while (guard.recentKeys.length > maxKeys) {
      guard.recentKeys.shift();
      removed += 1;
    }

    return removed;
  },

  wasApplied(key, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const guard = this.init(root);
    if (!guard || !key) return false;

    for (let i = 0; i < guard.recentKeys.length; i += 1) {
      const entry = guard.recentKeys[i];
      if (entry && entry.key === key) {
        return true;
      }
    }

    return false;
  },

  recordApplied(key, spec = {}, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const guard = this.init(root);
    if (!guard || !key) return 0;

    if (this.wasApplied(key, root)) {
      return 0;
    }

    guard.recentKeys.push({
      key: key,
      turn: root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      hook: String(spec.hook || (root && root.meta ? root.meta.currentHook || "" : "")),
      kind: String(spec.kind || "operation"),
      target: String(spec.target || "")
    });

    this.prune(root);
    return 1;
  },

  applyOnce(spec = {}, fn = null, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (typeof fn !== "function") return 0;

    const key = this.makeKey(spec, root);
    if (this.wasApplied(key, root)) {
      return 0;
    }

    const changes = Number(fn() || 0);
    if (changes > 0) {
      this.recordApplied(key, spec, root);
    }

    return changes;
  }
},

    // === UTILS ===
Utils: {
  clamp(value, min, max) {
    const n = Number(value);
    const low = Number(min);
    const high = Number(max);

    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return Number.isFinite(low) ? low : 0;
    if (!Number.isFinite(low) && !Number.isFinite(high)) return n;
    if (!Number.isFinite(low)) return Math.min(n, high);
    if (!Number.isFinite(high)) return Math.max(n, low);

    return Math.max(low, Math.min(high, n));
  },

  round(value, places = 0) {
    const n = Number(value);
    const p = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(places) || 0));
    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;

    const factor = Math.pow(10, p);
    return Math.round(n * factor) / factor;
  },

  normalizeSpaces(value) {
    return String(value ?? "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();
  },

  safeString(value, fallback = "") {
    if (value == null) return String(fallback);
    return String(value);
  },

  toKey(value) {
    return this.safeString(value)
      .normalize("NFKD")
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
      .replace(/[’'`´"]/g, "")
      .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1_$2")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
      .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "")
      .replace(/_+/g, "_");
  },

  ensureObject(target, key) {
    if (!target[key] || typeof target[key] !== "object" || Array.isArray(target[key])) {
      target[key] = {};
    }
    return target[key];
  },

  ensureArray(target, key) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target[key])) {
      target[key] = [];
    }
    return target[key];
  },

  safeGet(root, path, fallback = undefined) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return fallback;
    const parts = Array.isArray(path) ? path : String(path ?? "").split(".").filter(Boolean);

    let ref = root;
    for (const part of parts) {
      if (ref == null || typeof ref !== "object" || !(part in ref)) {
        return fallback;
      }
      ref = ref[part];
    }

    return ref;
  },

  safeSet(root, path, value) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return root;
    const parts = Array.isArray(path) ? path : String(path ?? "").split(".").filter(Boolean);
    if (parts.length === 0) return root;

    let ref = root;
    for (let i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i += 1) {
      const part = parts[i];
      if (!ref[part] || typeof ref[part] !== "object" || Array.isArray(ref[part])) {
        ref[part] = {};
      }
      ref = ref[part];
    }

    ref[parts[parts.length - 1]] = value;
    return root;
  },

  pushUnique(arrayRef, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(arrayRef)) return arrayRef;
    if (!arrayRef.includes(value)) {
      arrayRef.push(value);
    }
    return arrayRef;
  },

  removeValue(arrayRef, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(arrayRef)) return arrayRef;
    const idx = arrayRef.indexOf(value);
    if (idx !== -1) {
      arrayRef.splice(idx, 1);
    }
    return arrayRef;
  },

  makeId(prefix = "id") {
    const root = state.aidrpg || {};
    const meta = root.meta || {};
    const turn = Number(meta.currentTurn || 0);
    const seed = Number(meta.seed || 0);

    meta.seed = seed + 1;
    if (root.meta) {
      root.meta.seed = meta.seed;
    }

    const base = `${prefix}_${turn}_${meta.seed}`;
    return this.toKey(base);
  },

  ensureLogBuckets(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;

    if (!root.logs || typeof root.logs !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.logs)) {
      root.logs = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentEvents)) root.logs.recentEvents = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentParses)) root.logs.recentParses = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(root.logs.recentErrors)) root.logs.recentErrors = [];
  },

  log(bucket, message, maxEntries = 30) {
    const root = state.aidrpg;
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;

    this.ensureLogBuckets(root);

    const allowed = new Set(["recentEvents", "recentParses", "recentErrors"]);
    const bucketName = allowed.has(bucket) ? bucket : "recentEvents";
    const list = root.logs[bucketName];

    list.push({
      turn: this.safeGet(root, "meta.currentTurn", 0),
      hook: this.safeGet(root, "meta.currentHook", ""),
      message: this.safeString(message)
    });

    while (list.length > maxEntries) {
      list.shift();
    }
  },

  logEvent(message, maxEntries = 30) {
    this.log("recentEvents", message, maxEntries);
  },

  logParse(message, maxEntries = 30) {
    this.log("recentParses", message, maxEntries);
  },

  logError(message, maxEntries = 30) {
    this.log("recentErrors", message, maxEntries);
  },

  ensureDirtyShape(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    if (!root.meta || typeof root.meta !== "object") root.meta = {};
    if (!root.meta.dirty || typeof root.meta.dirty !== "object") root.meta.dirty = {};

    const dirty = root.meta.dirty;

    if (typeof dirty.player !== "boolean") dirty.player = false;
    if (!Array.isArray(dirty.abilities)) dirty.abilities = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(dirty.items)) dirty.items = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(dirty.actors)) dirty.actors = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(dirty.entities)) dirty.entities = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(dirty.routes)) dirty.routes = [];
    if (typeof dirty.reputation !== "boolean") dirty.reputation = false;
    if (typeof dirty.quests !== "boolean") dirty.quests = false;
    if (typeof dirty.time !== "boolean") dirty.time = false;
    if (!Array.isArray(dirty.cards)) dirty.cards = [];
  },

  markDirty(kind, value = true, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    this.ensureDirtyShape(root);

    const dirty = root.meta.dirty;

    if (!(kind in dirty)) return;

    if (Array.isArray(dirty[kind])) {
      if (value !== false && value != null) {
        this.pushUnique(dirty[kind], value);
      }
    } else {
      dirty[kind] = Boolean(value);
    }
  },

  clearDirty(kind, value = null, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    this.ensureDirtyShape(root);

    const dirty = root.meta.dirty;

    if (!(kind in dirty)) return;

    if (Array.isArray(dirty[kind])) {
      if (value == null) {
        dirty[kind] = [];
      } else {
        this.removeValue(dirty[kind], value);
      }
    } else {
      dirty[kind] = false;
    }
  },

  invalidateSceneCache(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    root.cache.scenePacket = "";
    root.cache.playerPacket = "";
    root.cache.actorPacket = "";
    root.cache.lastSceneHash = "";
    root.cache.parserHints = {};
    root.cache.localEntityIds = [];
    root.cache.localActorIds = [];
  },

  invalidatePackets(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    root.cache.scenePacket = "";
    root.cache.playerPacket = "";
    root.cache.actorPacket = "";
    root.cache.lastSceneHash = "";
  }
},

// === RUNTIME GATE ===
RuntimeGate: {
  normalizeText(value) {
    try {
      return typeof value === "string" ? value : String(value ?? "");
    } catch (_error) {
      return "";
    }
  },

  ensureBootstrapDiagnostics() {
    if (typeof state !== "object" || state === null || Array.isArray(state)) {
      return null;
    }

    if (!state.__aidrpgBootstrap || typeof state.__aidrpgBootstrap !== "object" || Array.isArray(state.__aidrpgBootstrap)) {
      state.__aidrpgBootstrap = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(state.__aidrpgBootstrap.diagnostics)) {
      state.__aidrpgBootstrap.diagnostics = [];
    }

    return state.__aidrpgBootstrap.diagnostics;
  },

  logFailure(hookName, result) {
    const bucket = this.ensureBootstrapDiagnostics();
    if (!bucket) return;

    bucket.push({
      hook: String(hookName || ""),
      issues: Array.isArray(result && result.issues) ? result.issues.slice(0, 6) : ["unknown_runtime_failure"],
      turn:
        state &&
        state.aidrpg &&
        state.aidrpg.meta &&
        typeof state.aidrpg.meta.currentTurn === "number"
          ? state.aidrpg.meta.currentTurn
          : 0
    });

    while (bucket.length > 12) {
      bucket.shift();
    }
  },

  validate(hookName, rawValue) {
    const issues = [];
    const safeText = this.normalizeText(rawValue);

    if (typeof state !== "object" || state === null || Array.isArray(state)) {
      issues.push("invalid_state");
    }

    if (typeof safeText !== "string") {
      issues.push("invalid_text");
    }

    if (hookName === "context") {
      if (!globalThis.info || typeof info !== "object" || Array.isArray(info)) {
        issues.push("invalid_info");
      }
    }

    return {
      ok: issues.length === 0,
      hook: String(hookName || ""),
      text: safeText,
      issues: issues
    };
  },

  failSoft(hookName, result, rawValue) {
    const safeText =
      result && typeof result.text === "string"
        ? result.text
        : this.normalizeText(rawValue);

    this.logFailure(hookName, result || { issues: ["unknown_runtime_failure"] });

    return { text: safeText };
  }
},

    // === ADMIN / DEBUG ===
AdminDebugSystem: {
  handleCommand(rawText) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const cmd = U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || "").trim().toLowerCase());

    if (!cmd.startsWith("/")) {
      return null;
    }

    if (cmd === "/debug on") {
      state.aidrpg.meta.debug = true;
      return { text: this.formatNotice("Debug enabled.") };
    }

    if (cmd === "/debug off") {
      state.aidrpg.meta.debug = false;
      return { text: this.formatNotice("Debug disabled.") };
    }

    if (cmd === "/sheet") {
      return { text: this.formatSheet() };
    }

    if (cmd === "/inventory") {
      return { text: this.formatInventory() };
    }

    if (cmd === "/time") {
      return { text: this.formatTime() };
    }

    if (cmd === "/reputation") {
      return { text: this.formatReputation() };
    }

    if (cmd === "/questlog") {
      return { text: this.formatQuestlog() };
    }

    if (cmd === "/actormemory" || cmd === "/memory") {
      return { text: this.formatActorMemory() };
    }

    if (cmd === "/cardsync live on") {
      if (globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem.setLiveWrites === "function") {
        globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem.setLiveWrites(true, state.aidrpg);
      }
      return { text: this.formatNotice("CardSync live writes requested: on. Card Safe Mode still blocks live writes unless you also run /cardsync safe off.") };
    }

    if (cmd === "/cardsync live off") {
      if (globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem.setLiveWrites === "function") {
        globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem.setLiveWrites(false, state.aidrpg);
      }
      return { text: this.formatNotice("CardSync live writes disabled. Dry-run mode remains active.") };
    }

    if (cmd === "/cardsync safe on") {
      if (globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem.setCardSafeMode === "function") {
        globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem.setCardSafeMode(true, state.aidrpg);
      }
      return { text: this.formatNotice("CardSync safe mode enabled. Live Story Card mutation is blocked.") };
    }

    if (cmd === "/cardsync safe off") {
      if (globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem.setCardSafeMode === "function") {
        globalThis.AIDRPG.CardSyncSystem.setCardSafeMode(false, state.aidrpg);
      }
      return { text: this.formatNotice("CardSync safe mode disabled. Live writes still require /cardsync live on and a compatible Story Card API.") };
    }

    if (cmd === "/cardsync" || cmd === "/cardsync status") {
      return { text: this.formatCardSync() };
    }

    return null;
  },

  formatNotice(message) {
    return "[AIDRPG]\n" + String(message || "") + "\n";
  },

  fmtValue(value, fallback) {
    if (fallback == null) fallback = "(unset)";
    if (value == null) return fallback;
    const str = String(value).trim();
    return str ? str : fallback;
  },

  fmtList(list, fallback) {
    if (fallback == null) fallback = "none";
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || list.length === 0) return fallback;

    const cleaned = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
      const v = String(list[i] || "").trim();
      if (v) cleaned.push(v);
    }

    return cleaned.length ? cleaned.join(", ") : fallback;
  },

  formatSheet() {
  const root = state.aidrpg || {};
  const meta = root.meta || {};
  const player = root.player || {};
  const levelState = player.levelState || {};
  const stats = player.stats || {};
  const body = player.body || {};
  const conditions = player.conditions || {};
  const appearance = player.appearance || {};
  const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : {};
  const locationsById = root.world && root.world.locationsById ? root.world.locationsById : {};

  let parentLocationName = "(none)";
  if (scene.parentLocationRef && locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef] && locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef].identity) {
    parentLocationName = locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef].identity.displayName || scene.parentLocationRef;
  } else if (scene.parentLocationRef) {
    parentLocationName = scene.parentLocationRef;
  }

  const lines = [
    "[AIDRPG /sheet]",
    "Build Stage: " + this.fmtValue(globalThis.AIDRPG.BUILD_STAGE, "(unknown)"),
    "Current Turn: " + (meta.currentTurn != null ? meta.currentTurn : 0),
    "Debug: " + (meta.debug === true ? "on" : "off"),
    "",
    "Name: " + this.fmtValue(player.name),
    "Species: " + this.fmtValue(player.species),
    "Level: " + (levelState.level != null ? levelState.level : "(pending)"),
    "XP: " + (levelState.xp != null ? levelState.xp : "(pending)") + " / " + (levelState.xpToNext != null ? levelState.xpToNext : "(pending)"),
    "",
    "HP: " + (stats.hp != null ? stats.hp : "(pending)") + " / " + (stats.maxHp != null ? stats.maxHp : "(pending)"),
    "MP: " + (stats.mp != null ? stats.mp : "(pending)") + " / " + (stats.maxMp != null ? stats.maxMp : "(pending)"),
    "EP: " + (stats.ep != null ? stats.ep : "(pending)") + " / " + (stats.maxEp != null ? stats.maxEp : "(pending)"),
    "ATK: " + (stats.atk != null ? stats.atk : "(pending)"),
    "DEF: " + (stats.def != null ? stats.def : "(pending)"),
    "SPD: " + (stats.spd != null ? stats.spd : "(pending)"),
    "INT: " + (stats.intl != null ? stats.intl : "(pending)"),
    "LCK: " + (stats.lck != null ? stats.lck : "(pending)"),
    "",
    "Player Location Ref: " + this.fmtValue(player.locationRef, "(none)"),
    "Player Scene Ref: " + this.fmtValue(player.sceneRef, "(none)"),
    "Scene Name: " + this.fmtValue(scene.displayName, "(none)"),
    "Scene Type: " + this.fmtValue(scene.sceneType, "(none)"),
    "Scene Parent Location: " + parentLocationName,
    "Scene Parent Ref: " + this.fmtValue(scene.parentLocationRef, "(none)"),
    "Visible Traits: " + this.fmtList(appearance.visibleTraits),
    "Outfit Summary: " + this.fmtValue(appearance.outfitSummary, "(none)"),
    "Injuries: " + this.fmtList(body.injuries),
    "Mutations: " + this.fmtList(body.mutations),
    "Active Conditions: " + this.fmtList(conditions.active),
    "Permanent Conditions: " + this.fmtList(conditions.permanent)
  ];

  return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
},

  formatInventory() {
    const root = state.aidrpg || {};
    const items = root.items || {};
    const byId = items.byId || {};
    const equipment = items.equipment || {};
    const ownership = items.ownership || {};
    const currency = (items.currency && items.currency.byType) || {};

    const resolveItemName = (itemId) => {
      if (!itemId) return "(empty)";
      const item = byId[itemId];
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return String(itemId);
      return item.displayName || item.baseType || String(itemId);
    };

    const playerOwned = Array.isArray(ownership.player) ? ownership.player : [];
    const equippedLines = Object.keys(equipment).length
      ? Object.keys(equipment).map(function (slot) {
          return slot + ": " + resolveItemName(equipment[slot]);
        })
      : ["(equipment not initialized yet)"];

    const ownedNames = playerOwned.length
      ? playerOwned.map(resolveItemName).join(", ")
      : "none";

    const lines = [
      "[AIDRPG /inventory]",
      "Owned Item IDs: " + playerOwned.length,
      "Owned Items: " + ownedNames,
      "",
      "Equipped:"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < equippedLines.length; i += 1) {
      lines.push(equippedLines[i]);
    }

    lines.push("");
    lines.push("Currency - copper: " + (currency.copper != null ? currency.copper : 0));
    lines.push("Currency - silver: " + (currency.silver != null ? currency.silver : 0));
    lines.push("Currency - gold: " + (currency.gold != null ? currency.gold : 0));

    return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
  },

  formatTime() {
    const root = state.aidrpg || {};
    const time = root.time || {};
    const current = time.current || {};
    const elapsed = time.elapsed || {};
    const routes = time.routes || {};
    const contradictions = Array.isArray(time.contradictionFlags) ? time.contradictionFlags : [];

    const routeKeys = Object.keys(routes.knownTravelTimes || {});
    const lines = [
      "[AIDRPG /time]",
      "Day: " + (current.day != null ? current.day : "(pending)"),
      "Hour: " + (current.hour != null ? current.hour : "(pending)"),
      "Minute: " + (current.minute != null ? current.minute : "(pending)"),
      "Season: " + this.fmtValue(current.season, "(unset)"),
      "Weather: " + this.fmtValue(current.weather, "(unset)"),
      "",
      "Elapsed Hours: " + (elapsed.totalHours != null ? elapsed.totalHours : 0),
      "Known Route Times: " + routeKeys.length,
      "Contradiction Flags: " + (contradictions.length ? contradictions.join(", ") : "none")
    ];

    return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
  },

  formatReputation() {
    const root = state.aidrpg || {};
    const reputation = root.reputation || {};
    const bySubject = reputation.bySubject || {};
    const subjects = Object.keys(bySubject).sort();
    const recent = Array.isArray(reputation.recentChanges) ? reputation.recentChanges : [];
    const rumors = Array.isArray(reputation.rumors) ? reputation.rumors : [];
    const titles = Array.isArray(reputation.titles) ? reputation.titles : [];

    const lines = [
      "[AIDRPG /reputation]",
      "Tracked Subjects: " + subjects.length
    ];

    if (subjects.length) {
      for (let i = 0; i < subjects.length && i < 8; i += 1) {
        const subject = bySubject[subjects[i]] || {};
        const identity = subject.identity || {};
        const mutable = subject.mutable || {};
        const name = identity.displayName || subjects[i];
        const score = typeof mutable.score === "number" ? mutable.score : 0;
        const standing = mutable.standing || "neutral";
        const tags = Array.isArray(mutable.tags) && mutable.tags.length ? " | tags: " + mutable.tags.join(", ") : "";
        lines.push("- " + name + " | " + standing + " | score " + score + tags);
      }
    } else {
      lines.push("- none");
    }

    lines.push("");
    lines.push("Titles: " + (titles.length ? titles.join(", ") : "none"));
    lines.push("Rumors: " + (rumors.length ? rumors.slice(-3).map(function (r) { return r.label || r.text || String(r); }).join(" | ") : "none"));

    if (recent.length) {
      lines.push("");
      lines.push("Recent Changes:");
      for (let i = Math.max(0, recent.length - 5); i < recent.length; i += 1) {
        const row = recent[i] || {};
        lines.push("- " + (row.subject || "unknown") + ": " + (row.direction || "change") + " (" + (row.delta || 0) + ")");
      }
    }

    return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
  },

  formatQuestlog() {
    const root = state.aidrpg || {};
    const quests = root.quests || {};
    const active = Array.isArray(quests.active) ? quests.active : [];
    const archived = Array.isArray(quests.archived) ? quests.archived : [];
    const tracking = quests.tracking || {};
    const primaryId = tracking.primaryQuestId || null;

    const summarizeQuest = (quest) => {
      if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") return "(invalid quest)";
      const id = quest.id ? " [" + quest.id + "]" : "";
      const title = quest.title || "(untitled quest)";
      const status = quest.status || "unknown";
      const reward = quest.rewardSummary ? " | reward: " + quest.rewardSummary : "";
      return "- " + title + id + " | status: " + status + reward;
    };

    const lines = [
      "[AIDRPG /questlog]",
      "Primary Quest ID: " + (primaryId || "(none)"),
      "Active Quests: " + active.length
    ];

    if (active.length) {
      for (let i = 0; i < active.length; i += 1) {
        lines.push(summarizeQuest(active[i]));
      }
    } else {
      lines.push("- none");
    }

    lines.push("");
    lines.push("Archived Quests: " + archived.length);

    return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
  },


  formatActorMemory() {
    const root = state.aidrpg || {};
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const system = A.MajorActorMemorySystem || {};

    if (system && typeof system.init === "function") {
      system.init(root);
    }

    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const memory = actors.majorMemory || {};
    const byActorId = memory.byActorId || {};
    const activeIds = Array.isArray(memory.activeIds) ? memory.activeIds : [];
    const ids = Object.keys(byActorId).sort(function (a, b) {
      const left = byActorId[a] || {};
      const right = byActorId[b] || {};
      return Number(right.lastUpdatedTurn || right.lastSeenTurn || 0) - Number(left.lastUpdatedTurn || left.lastSeenTurn || 0);
    });
    const recentEvents = Array.isArray(memory.recentEvents) ? memory.recentEvents : [];

    const lines = [
      "[AIDRPG /actormemory]",
      "Major Actor Records: " + ids.length,
      "Active Major Actors: " + activeIds.length
    ];

    if (ids.length) {
      lines.push("");
      for (let i = 0; i < ids.length && i < 8; i += 1) {
        const rec = byActorId[ids[i]] || {};
        const name = rec.name || ids[i];
        const stance = rec.relationshipStance || "neutral";
        const goals = Array.isArray(rec.goals) ? rec.goals : [];
        const grudges = Array.isArray(rec.grudges) ? rec.grudges : [];
        const notes = Array.isArray(rec.attitudeNotes) ? rec.attitudeNotes : [];

        lines.push("- " + name + " | stance: " + stance + " | seen: " + (rec.lastSeenTurn || 0));
        if (goals.length) lines.push("  Goal: " + goals[goals.length - 1]);
        if (grudges.length) lines.push("  Grudge: " + grudges[grudges.length - 1]);
        if (notes.length) lines.push("  Note: " + notes[notes.length - 1]);
      }
    } else {
      lines.push("- none");
    }

    if (recentEvents.length) {
      lines.push("");
      lines.push("Recent Memory Events:");
      for (let i = Math.max(0, recentEvents.length - 5); i < recentEvents.length; i += 1) {
        const row = recentEvents[i] || {};
        lines.push("- " + (row.name || row.actorId || "actor") + ": " + (row.kind || "memory") + " | " + (row.text || ""));
      }
    }

    return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
},

  formatCardSync() {
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const system = A.CardSyncSystem || {};
    const root = state.aidrpg || {};

    if (system && typeof system.init === "function") {
      system.init(root);
      if (typeof system.refreshIndex === "function") {
        system.refreshIndex(root);
      }
    }

    const cards = root.cards || {};
    const config = cards.config || {};
    const index = cards.index || {};
    const dirtyQueue = Array.isArray(cards.dirtyQueue) ? cards.dirtyQueue : [];
    const pendingWrites = Array.isArray(cards.pendingWrites) ? cards.pendingWrites : [];
    const dryRunLog = Array.isArray(cards.dryRunLog) ? cards.dryRunLog : [];
    const recentOperations = Array.isArray(cards.recentOperations) ? cards.recentOperations : [];
    const duplicateRefs = Array.isArray(cards.duplicateRefs) ? cards.duplicateRefs : [];
    const namespaceCount = index.byNamespace && typeof index.byNamespace === "object" ? Object.keys(index.byNamespace).length : 0;

    const lines = [
      "[AIDRPG /cardsync]",
      "Live Writes: " + (config.liveWrites === true ? "on" : "off"),
      "Strict Mode: " + (config.strictCardSync === false ? "off" : "on"),
      "Namespace Prefix: " + (config.namespacePrefix || "SYS:AIDRPG"),
      "Indexed Managed Cards: " + namespaceCount,
      "Dirty Queue: " + dirtyQueue.length,
      "Pending Writes: " + pendingWrites.length,
      "Dry Run Log: " + dryRunLog.length,
      "Duplicate Managed Refs: " + duplicateRefs.length
    ];

    if (dirtyQueue.length) {
      lines.push("");
      lines.push("Dirty Targets:");
      for (let i = 0; i < dirtyQueue.length && i < 8; i += 1) {
        const row = dirtyQueue[i] || {};
        lines.push("- " + (row.family || "unknown") + ":" + (row.id || "unknown") + " | " + (row.reason || "dirty"));
      }
    }

    if (recentOperations.length) {
      lines.push("");
      lines.push("Recent Card Ops:");
      for (let i = Math.max(0, recentOperations.length - 5); i < recentOperations.length; i += 1) {
        const row = recentOperations[i] || {};
        lines.push("- " + (row.action || "op") + " | " + (row.namespace || row.cardId || "unknown") + " | " + (row.mode || "safe"));
      }
    }

    return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
  }
},

    // === PLAYER SYSTEM ===
PlayerSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!root.player || typeof root.player !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player)) {
      root.player = {};
    }

    const player = root.player;

    if (typeof player.id !== "string" || !player.id) player.id = "player";
    if (typeof player.name !== "string") player.name = "";
    if (typeof player.species !== "string" || !player.species) player.species = "human";

    if (!player.levelState || typeof player.levelState !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.levelState)) {
      player.levelState = {};
    }
    if (typeof player.levelState.level !== "number") player.levelState.level = 1;
    if (typeof player.levelState.xp !== "number") player.levelState.xp = 0;
    if (typeof player.levelState.xpToNext !== "number") player.levelState.xpToNext = 100;
    if (typeof player.levelState.totalXp !== "number") player.levelState.totalXp = 0;
    if (typeof player.levelState.growthClass !== "string" || !player.levelState.growthClass) {
      player.levelState.growthClass = "standard";
    }

    if (!player.stats || typeof player.stats !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.stats)) {
      player.stats = {};
    }

    const stats = player.stats;
    if (typeof stats.hp !== "number") stats.hp = 100;
    if (typeof stats.maxHp !== "number") stats.maxHp = 100;
    if (typeof stats.mp !== "number") stats.mp = 20;
    if (typeof stats.maxMp !== "number") stats.maxMp = 20;
    if (typeof stats.ep !== "number") stats.ep = 50;
    if (typeof stats.maxEp !== "number") stats.maxEp = 50;
    if (typeof stats.atk !== "number") stats.atk = 5;
    if (typeof stats.def !== "number") stats.def = 5;
    if (typeof stats.spd !== "number") stats.spd = 5;
    if (typeof stats.intl !== "number") stats.intl = 5;
    if (typeof stats.lck !== "number") stats.lck = 5;

    stats.hp = U.clamp(stats.hp, 0, stats.maxHp);
    stats.mp = U.clamp(stats.mp, 0, stats.maxMp);
    stats.ep = U.clamp(stats.ep, 0, stats.maxEp);

    if (!player.derived || typeof player.derived !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.derived)) {
      player.derived = {};
    }

    if (!player.body || typeof player.body !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.body)) {
      player.body = {};
    }
    if (!player.body.limbs || typeof player.body.limbs !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.body.limbs)) {
      player.body.limbs = {};
    }

    const limbs = player.body.limbs;
    if (typeof limbs.leftArm !== "string") limbs.leftArm = "present";
    if (typeof limbs.rightArm !== "string") limbs.rightArm = "present";
    if (typeof limbs.leftLeg !== "string") limbs.leftLeg = "present";
    if (typeof limbs.rightLeg !== "string") limbs.rightLeg = "present";

    if (!Array.isArray(player.body.injuries)) player.body.injuries = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.body.scars)) player.body.scars = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.body.mutations)) player.body.mutations = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.body.speciesTraits)) player.body.speciesTraits = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.body.bodyTags)) player.body.bodyTags = [];

    if (!player.conditions || typeof player.conditions !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.conditions)) {
      player.conditions = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.active)) player.conditions.active = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.permanent)) player.conditions.permanent = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.vulnerabilities)) player.conditions.vulnerabilities = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.immunities)) player.conditions.immunities = [];

    if (!player.appearance || typeof player.appearance !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.appearance)) {
      player.appearance = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(player.appearance.visibleTraits)) player.appearance.visibleTraits = [];
    if (typeof player.appearance.outfitSummary !== "string") player.appearance.outfitSummary = "";
    if (!Array.isArray(player.appearance.silhouetteTags)) player.appearance.silhouetteTags = [];

    if (typeof player.locationRef !== "string") player.locationRef = "";
    if (typeof player.sceneRef !== "string") player.sceneRef = "";

    this.recomputeDerived(player);

    return player;
  },

processContext(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);
  return 0;
},

processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const text = String(rawText || "");
  let changes = 0;

  changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

  if (text) {
    const hints = this.extractIdentityHints(text);
    changes += this.applyIdentityHints(hints, root);
  }

  return changes;
},

applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
  const player = root.player || {};
  const stats = player.stats || {};
  const body = player.body || {};
  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];

  let changes = 0;

  const pushUniqueLocal = (list, value) => {
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;
    list.push(text);
    return 1;
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "player" || candidate._playerApplied === true) {
      continue;
    }

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const target =
      candidate.type === "resource_state"
        ? String(payload.resource || "player")
        : candidate.type === "body_injury"
        ? String(payload.bodyPart || payload.injuryTag || "body")
        : String(payload.status || candidate.type || "player");

    const commit = () => {
      let localChanges = 0;

      if (candidate.type === "resource_state") {
        const resource = String(payload.resource || "");
        const stateName = String(payload.state || "");
        const maxKey = resource === "hp" ? "maxHp" : resource === "mp" ? "maxMp" : resource === "ep" ? "maxEp" : "";
        const currentValue = Number(stats[resource]);
        const maxValue = Number(stats[maxKey]);

        if (maxKey && Number.isFinite(currentValue) && Number.isFinite(maxValue)) {
          let nextValue = currentValue;

          if (stateName === "empty") {
            nextValue = 0;
          } else if (stateName === "low") {
            const ratio = resource === "hp" ? 0.30 : 0.25;
            nextValue = Math.min(currentValue, Math.max(0, Math.ceil(maxValue * ratio)));
          }

          nextValue = U.clamp(nextValue, 0, maxValue);

          if (stats[resource] !== nextValue) {
            stats[resource] = nextValue;
            localChanges += 1;
          }
        }
      } else if (candidate.type === "body_injury") {
        const bodyPart = String(payload.bodyPart || "").trim();
        const injuryTag = String(payload.injuryTag || "").trim();
        const label = bodyPart && injuryTag ? bodyPart + " " + injuryTag : injuryTag;

        if (label) {
          localChanges += pushUniqueLocal(body.injuries, label);
        }
      }

      return localChanges;
    };

    const localChanges =
      Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
        ? Guard.applyOnce({
            kind: "player_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
            target: target,
            payload: payload,
            sourceId: candidate.id || ""
          }, commit, root)
        : commit();

    changes += localChanges;
    candidate._playerApplied = true;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.recomputeDerived(player);

    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("player", true, root);
    }
    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Player consequences applied.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

extractIdentityHints(text) {
  const raw = String(text || "");
  const hints = {
    name: "",
    species: "",
    visibleTraits: [],
    bodyTags: []
  };

  let match = raw.match(/\bYou,\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,30}),\s*(?:an?\s+)?\d{1,3}\s+year old\s+([A-Za-z][A-Za-z -]{1,40}?)(?=[,.]| with\b| at\b| from\b| who\b)/i);
  if (match) {
    hints.name = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    hints.species = String(match[2] || "").trim();
  }

  if (!hints.name) {
    match = raw.match(/\bThe player begins as\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,30})\b/i);
    if (match) {
      hints.name = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    }
  }

  if (!hints.name) {
    match = raw.match(/\bYou are\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,30})\s+now\b/);
    if (match) {
      hints.name = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    }
  }

  if (!hints.name) {
    match = raw.match(/^\s*Name\s*:\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,30})\s*$/mi);
    if (match) {
      hints.name = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    }
  }

  if (!hints.species) {
    match = raw.match(/^\s*(?:Race|Species)\s*:\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z -]{1,40})\s*$/mi);
    if (match) {
      hints.species = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    }
  }

  if (
    /\bseven feet tall\b/i.test(raw) ||
    /\bhead brushing\b/i.test(raw) ||
    /\byou have to duck\b/i.test(raw) ||
    /\byour head brushing\b/i.test(raw)
  ) {
    hints.visibleTraits.push("very tall");
    hints.bodyTags.push("large_frame");
  }

  return hints;
},

normalizeSpeciesLabel(value) {
  const cleaned = String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[-_]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/^(?:a|an|the)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!cleaned) return "";
  if (/\d/.test(cleaned)) return "";
  if (cleaned.split(" ").length > 4) return "";

  const banned = new Set([
    "confused traveler",
    "traveler",
    "stranger",
    "person",
    "someone",
    "someone else",
    "man",
    "woman",
    "boy",
    "girl"
  ]);

  if (banned.has(cleaned)) return "";

  return cleaned;
},

applyIdentityHints(hints, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  if (!hints || typeof hints !== "object") return 0;

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  const player = root.player || {};
  let changes = 0;

  const pushUniqueLocal = (list, value) => {
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) === -1) {
      list.push(text);
      return 1;
    }
    return 0;
  };

  const currentName = String(player.name || "").trim();
  const currentSpecies = this.normalizeSpeciesLabel(player.species || "");
  const nextName = String(hints.name || "").trim();
  const nextSpecies = this.normalizeSpeciesLabel(hints.species || "");

  if (nextName && (!currentName || currentName.toLowerCase() === nextName.toLowerCase())) {
    if (player.name !== nextName) {
      player.name = nextName;
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  if (nextSpecies && (!currentSpecies || currentSpecies === "human" || currentSpecies === nextSpecies)) {
    if (player.species !== nextSpecies) {
      player.species = nextSpecies;
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(hints.visibleTraits)) {
    for (let i = 0; i < hints.visibleTraits.length; i += 1) {
      changes += pushUniqueLocal(player.appearance.visibleTraits, hints.visibleTraits[i]);
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(hints.bodyTags)) {
    for (let i = 0; i < hints.bodyTags.length; i += 1) {
      changes += pushUniqueLocal(player.body.bodyTags, hints.bodyTags[i]);
    }
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.recomputeDerived(player);

    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("player", true, root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Player identity synced.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

recomputeDerived(player = (state.aidrpg && state.aidrpg.player) || null) {
  if (!player || typeof player !== "object") return null;

  const stats = player.stats || {};
  const levelState = player.levelState || {};
  const derived = player.derived || (player.derived = {});

  const level = Number(levelState.level || 1);
  const atk = Number(stats.atk || 0);
  const def = Number(stats.def || 0);
  const spd = Number(stats.spd || 0);
  const intl = Number(stats.intl || 0);
  const maxHp = Number(stats.maxHp || 0);
  const maxEp = Number(stats.maxEp || 0);

  derived.combatPressure = Math.max(1, Math.round(((atk + def + spd + intl) / 4) + level));
  derived.mobility = Math.max(0, Math.round(spd + (maxEp / 25)));
  derived.threatRating = Math.max(1, Math.round((level * 5) + derived.combatPressure + (maxHp / 10)));

  return derived;
},

  getCompactSummary(player = (state.aidrpg && state.aidrpg.player) || null) {
    if (!player || typeof player !== "object") {
      return "Player summary unavailable.";
    }

    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const levelState = player.levelState || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};
    const appearance = player.appearance || {};

    const active = Array.isArray(conditions.active) && conditions.active.length
      ? conditions.active.join(", ")
      : "none";

    const outfit = appearance.outfitSummary && appearance.outfitSummary.trim()
      ? appearance.outfitSummary.trim()
      : "unspecified";

    const species = player.species && String(player.species).trim()
      ? String(player.species).trim()
      : "unknown";

    return [
      `Lvl ${levelState.level ?? 1}`,
      species,
      `HP ${stats.hp ?? 0}/${stats.maxHp ?? 0}`,
      `MP ${stats.mp ?? 0}/${stats.maxMp ?? 0}`,
      `EP ${stats.ep ?? 0}/${stats.maxEp ?? 0}`,
      `Cond: ${active}`,
      `Outfit: ${outfit}`
    ].join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === BUILD IDENTITY SYSTEM ===
BuildIdentitySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const player = root.player || {};

    if (!root.build || typeof root.build !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.build)) {
      root.build = {};
    }

    const build = root.build;

    if (!Array.isArray(build.originTags)) build.originTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.combatTags)) build.combatTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.methodTags)) build.methodTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.resourceTags)) build.resourceTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.defenseTags)) build.defenseTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.specializationTags)) build.specializationTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.temperamentTags)) build.temperamentTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.styleTags)) build.styleTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.archetypeSummary)) build.archetypeSummary = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.masteryNotes)) build.masteryNotes = [];

    if (!build.matchupProfile || typeof build.matchupProfile !== "object" || Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile)) {
      build.matchupProfile = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst = [];

    this.inferBaselineTags(build, player);
    this.rebuildArchetypeSummary(build, player);

    return build;
  },

  clearTagBuckets(build) {
    build.originTags = [];
    build.combatTags = [];
    build.methodTags = [];
    build.resourceTags = [];
    build.defenseTags = [];
    build.specializationTags = [];
    build.temperamentTags = [];
    build.styleTags = [];
  },

  addTag(bucket, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(bucket)) return;
    const tag = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!tag) return;
    if (bucket.indexOf(tag) === -1) {
      bucket.push(tag);
    }
  },

  inferBaselineTags(build, player) {
    this.clearTagBuckets(build);

    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const levelState = player.levelState || {};
    const derived = player.derived || {};
    const body = player.body || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};
    const appearance = player.appearance || {};

    const species = typeof player.species === "string" && player.species.trim()
      ? player.species.trim().toLowerCase()
      : "unknown";

    this.addTag(build.originTags, "species:" + species);

    if (species === "human") {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "baseline_mortal");
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "adaptable");
    } else if (species !== "unknown") {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "nonhuman");
    }

    const level = Number(levelState.level || 1);
    if (level <= 3) {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "novice");
    } else if (level <= 10) {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "developing");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "established");
    }

    const atk = Number(stats.atk || 0);
    const def = Number(stats.def || 0);
    const spd = Number(stats.spd || 0);
    const intl = Number(stats.intl || 0);
    const lck = Number(stats.lck || 0);
    const maxHp = Number(stats.maxHp || 0);
    const maxMp = Number(stats.maxMp || 0);
    const maxEp = Number(stats.maxEp || 0);
    const mobility = Number(derived.mobility || 0);
    const pressure = Number(derived.combatPressure || 0);

    const primaryStat = this.getPrimaryStatName({ atk, def, spd, intl, lck });

    if (primaryStat === "atk") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "direct_force");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "offensive");
    } else if (primaryStat === "def") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "anchor");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "steady");
    } else if (primaryStat === "spd") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "mobile");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "agile");
    } else if (primaryStat === "intl") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "technical");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "measured");
    } else if (primaryStat === "lck") {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "opportunistic");
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "adaptive");
    }

    if (pressure >= 8) {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "combat_capable");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.combatTags, "unproven");
    }

    if (atk >= def + 2) {
      this.addTag(build.methodTags, "pressure_first");
    } else if (def >= atk + 2) {
      this.addTag(build.methodTags, "durability_first");
    } else if (spd >= atk && spd >= def) {
      this.addTag(build.methodTags, "positioning_first");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.methodTags, "balanced_method");
    }

    if (maxMp > maxEp) {
      this.addTag(build.resourceTags, "mana_leaning");
    } else if (maxEp > maxMp) {
      this.addTag(build.resourceTags, "stamina_leaning");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.resourceTags, "mixed_resource");
    }

    if (intl >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.resourceTags, "control_sensitive");
    }

    if (def >= 7 || maxHp >= 120) {
      this.addTag(build.defenseTags, "durable");
    }

    if (mobility >= 7 || spd >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.defenseTags, "evasive");
    }

    if (maxHp < 90 && def < 5) {
      this.addTag(build.defenseTags, "fragile");
    }

    if (atk >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "power_bias");
    }
    if (def >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "resilience_bias");
    }
    if (spd >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "speed_bias");
    }
    if (intl >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "technical_bias");
    }
    if (lck >= 7) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "fortune_bias");
    }

    if (!build.specializationTags.length) {
      this.addTag(build.specializationTags, "generalist_bias");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(conditions.active) && conditions.active.length) {
      this.addTag(build.temperamentTags, "under_strain");
    } else {
      this.addTag(build.temperamentTags, "stable");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(body.injuries) && body.injuries.length) {
      this.addTag(build.temperamentTags, "pain_tested");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(body.mutations) && body.mutations.length) {
      this.addTag(build.originTags, "altered_body");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(appearance.visibleTraits) && appearance.visibleTraits.length) {
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "visibly_distinct");
    }

    if (!build.styleTags.length) {
      this.addTag(build.styleTags, "unformed");
    }
  },

  getPrimaryStatName(statMap) {
    let bestName = "atk";
    let bestValue = -Infinity;

    const names = ["atk", "def", "spd", "intl", "lck"];
    for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i += 1) {
      const name = names[i];
      const value = Number(statMap[name] || 0);
      if (value > bestValue) {
        bestValue = value;
        bestName = name;
      }
    }

    return bestName;
  },

  rebuildArchetypeSummary(build, player) {
    const summary = [];

    const pushUnique = (value) => {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text) return;
      if (summary.indexOf(text) === -1) {
        summary.push(text);
      }
    };

    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("direct_force") !== -1) pushUnique("force-leaning");
    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("anchor") !== -1) pushUnique("durability-leaning");
    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("mobile") !== -1) pushUnique("mobility-leaning");
    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("technical") !== -1) pushUnique("technical-leaning");
    if (build.combatTags.indexOf("opportunistic") !== -1) pushUnique("opportunistic");

    if (build.resourceTags.indexOf("mana_leaning") !== -1) pushUnique("mana-leaning");
    if (build.resourceTags.indexOf("stamina_leaning") !== -1) pushUnique("stamina-leaning");
    if (build.resourceTags.indexOf("mixed_resource") !== -1) pushUnique("mixed-resource");

    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("generalist_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("generalist");
    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("power_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("power-biased");
    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("resilience_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("resilience-biased");
    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("speed_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("speed-biased");
    if (build.specializationTags.indexOf("technical_bias") !== -1) pushUnique("technical-biased");

    if (!summary.length) {
      pushUnique("unformed");
    }

    build.archetypeSummary = summary;

    if (!build.matchupProfile || typeof build.matchupProfile !== "object") {
      build.matchupProfile = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst = [];

    if (!build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst.length) {
      build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst.push("unknown_styles");
    }

    return build.archetypeSummary;
  },

  getCompactSummary(build = (state.aidrpg && state.aidrpg.build) || null) {
    if (!build || typeof build !== "object") {
      return "Build summary unavailable.";
    }

    const summary = Array.isArray(build.archetypeSummary) ? build.archetypeSummary : [];
    const styles = Array.isArray(build.styleTags) ? build.styleTags : [];
    const methods = Array.isArray(build.methodTags) ? build.methodTags : [];

    const parts = [];

    if (summary.length) {
      parts.push("Archetype: " + summary.join(", "));
    }

    if (styles.length) {
      parts.push("Style: " + styles.join(", "));
    }

    if (methods.length) {
      parts.push("Method: " + methods.join(", "));
    }

    if (!parts.length) {
      return "Build summary unavailable.";
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === INTENT PARSER ===
IntentParser: {
  parseInput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const trimmed = U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""));

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    if (!trimmed) {
      root.pending.inputIntent = null;
      return null;
    }

    const lower = trimmed.toLowerCase();
    const scores = this.scoreActionFamilies(lower, trimmed);
    const primaryType = this.pickPrimaryType(scores);
    const alternates = this.buildAlternates(scores, primaryType);

    const intent = {
      primaryType: primaryType,
      confidence: this.scoreToConfidence(scores[primaryType] || 0),
      alternates: alternates,
      targetHint: this.extractTarget(trimmed, primaryType),
      weaponHint: this.extractWeapon(trimmed, primaryType),
      styleHint: this.extractStyle(trimmed, primaryType),
      abilityHint: this.extractAbility(trimmed, primaryType),
      itemHint: this.extractItem(trimmed, primaryType),
      destinationHint: this.extractDestination(trimmed, primaryType),
      trainingHint: this.extractTrainingFocus(trimmed, primaryType),
      environmentHint: "",
      tags: this.buildTags(primaryType, scores, trimmed),
      rawText: trimmed
    };

    root.pending.inputIntent = intent;

    if (root.meta && root.meta.debug === true && U && typeof U.logParse === "function") {
      U.logParse("Input intent: " + primaryType + " (" + intent.confidence + ")");
    }

    return intent;
  },

  scoreActionFamilies(lower, rawText) {
  const scores = {
    attack: 0,
    defend: 0,
    move: 0,
    travel: 0,
    inspect: 0,
    talk: 0,
    barter: 0,
    loot: 0,
    equip: 0,
    unequip: 0,
    rest: 0,
    train: 0,
    use_ability: 0,
    consume_item: 0,
    interact: 0
  };

  const addIf = (type, regex, amount) => {
    if (regex.test(lower)) {
      scores[type] += amount;
    }
  };

  addIf("attack", /\b(attack|strike|slash|stab|shoot|fire at|swing at|kick|punch|smash|hit|lunge|charge at|kill)\b/, 5);
  addIf("defend", /\b(defend|block|parry|guard|brace|protect|shield|take cover|hold position)\b/, 5);
  addIf("move", /\b(go|walk|run|step|move|enter|leave|approach|advance|retreat|back away|climb|crawl)\b/, 3);
  addIf("move", /\b(north|south|east|west|upstairs|downstairs|inside|outside)\b/, 2);
  addIf("travel", /\b(travel|journey|set out|head to|make for|ride to|sail to|return to)\b/, 5);
  addIf("inspect", /\b(look|look at|inspect|examine|search|study|check|observe|scan|peek|read)\b/, 5);
  addIf("talk", /\b(say|ask|tell|reply|speak|talk|whisper|shout|yell|greet|persuade|convince)\b/, 5);
  addIf("barter", /\b(buy|sell|trade|barter|haggle|purchase|pay|negotiate)\b/, 5);
  addIf("loot", /\b(loot|take|grab|pick up|collect|gather|steal)\b/, 5);
  addIf("equip", /\b(equip|wear|put on|draw|wield|ready|don)\b/, 5);
  addIf("unequip", /\b(unequip|remove|take off|sheath|stow|holster)\b/, 5);
  addIf("rest", /\b(rest|sleep|nap|wait|pause|camp|recover|breathe)\b/, 5);
  addIf("train", /\b(train|practice|drill|exercise|meditate|spar|work on)\b/, 5);
  addIf("use_ability", /\b(cast|channel|invoke|activate|unleash|focus)\b/, 5);
  addIf("consume_item", /\b(drink|eat|consume|swallow|sip)\b/, 5);
  addIf("interact", /\b(open|close|push|pull|touch|press|turn|unlock|lock|use|lift|drag)\b/, 4);

  if (/["“][^"”]+["”]/.test(rawText)) {
    scores.talk += 3;
  }

  if (/\bgo to\b/.test(lower) || /\bhead to\b/.test(lower) || /\breturn to\b/.test(lower)) {
    scores.travel += 2;
    scores.move += 1;
  }

  if (/\blook around\b/.test(lower)) {
    scores.inspect += 3;
  }

  if (/\buse\b/.test(lower) && !/\bconsume\b/.test(lower)) {
    scores.interact += 1;
    scores.use_ability += 1;
  }

  // Phase 5 recovery:
  // Explicit training language at the front of the input must stay training,
  // even if the purpose phrase contains defensive words like protect/shield/against.
  if (/^\s*(?:i\s+)?(?:train|practice|drill|exercise|meditate on|spar with|work on)\s+/i.test(String(rawText || ""))) {
    scores.train += 7;
  }

  return scores;
},

  pickPrimaryType(scores) {
    let bestType = "interact";
    let bestScore = -1;

    const order = [
      "attack",
      "defend",
      "travel",
      "inspect",
      "talk",
      "barter",
      "loot",
      "equip",
      "unequip",
      "rest",
      "train",
      "use_ability",
      "consume_item",
      "move",
      "interact"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < order.length; i += 1) {
      const type = order[i];
      const value = Number(scores[type] || 0);
      if (value > bestScore) {
        bestScore = value;
        bestType = type;
      }
    }

    if (bestScore <= 0) {
      return "interact";
    }

    return bestType;
  },

  buildAlternates(scores, primaryType) {
    const entries = [];
    for (const key in scores) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(scores, key)) continue;
      if (key === primaryType) continue;

      const value = Number(scores[key] || 0);
      if (value > 0) {
        entries.push({ type: key, score: value });
      }
    }

    entries.sort(function (a, b) {
      return b.score - a.score;
    });

    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < entries.length && i < 3; i += 1) {
      out.push({
        type: entries[i].type,
        confidence: this.scoreToConfidence(entries[i].score)
      });
    }

    return out;
  },

  scoreToConfidence(score) {
    const raw = 0.2 + (Number(score || 0) * 0.11);
    if (raw < 0.2) return 0.2;
    if (raw > 0.95) return 0.95;
    return Math.round(raw * 100) / 100;
  },

  buildTags(primaryType, scores, rawText) {
    const tags = [];
    const addTag = (value) => {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text) return;
      if (tags.indexOf(text) === -1) {
        tags.push(text);
      }
    };

    addTag("intent:" + primaryType);

    if ((scores.attack || 0) > 0) addTag("aggressive");
