    if ((scores.defend || 0) > 0) addTag("protective");
    if ((scores.inspect || 0) > 0) addTag("observational");
    if ((scores.talk || 0) > 0) addTag("social");
    if ((scores.travel || 0) > 0 || (scores.move || 0) > 0) addTag("movement");
    if ((scores.train || 0) > 0) addTag("improvement");
    if ((scores.loot || 0) > 0 || (scores.barter || 0) > 0) addTag("acquisition");
    if ((scores.use_ability || 0) > 0) addTag("specialized");
    if ((scores.consume_item || 0) > 0) addTag("consumable_use");

    if (/["“][^"”]+["”]/.test(rawText)) {
      addTag("quoted_speech");
    }

    return tags;
  },

  extractTarget(rawText, primaryType) {
    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:attack|strike|slash|stab|shoot|hit|kick|punch|smash|lunge at|charge at)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /\b(?:talk|speak|say|ask|tell|reply|greet)\s+(?:to\s+)?(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /\b(?:inspect|examine|check|search|study|open|close|push|pull|touch|unlock|lock)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /\b(?:at|toward|towards|against|into)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    if (primaryType === "inspect" && /\blook around\b/i.test(rawText)) {
      return "surroundings";
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractWeapon(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (primaryType !== "attack" && primaryType !== "defend" && primaryType !== "interact") {
      return "";
    }

    const match = rawText.match(/\bwith\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractStyle(rawText, primaryType) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();

    if (primaryType === "attack") {
      if (/\bcareful\b|\bprecise\b|\bmeasured\b/.test(lower)) return "measured";
      if (/\bfierce\b|\bbrutal\b|\bhard\b|\bfull force\b/.test(lower)) return "forceful";
      if (/\bquick\b|\bfast\b|\bsudden\b/.test(lower)) return "fast";
    }

    if (primaryType === "talk") {
      if (/\bcalmly\b|\bpolitely\b/.test(lower)) return "calm";
      if (/\bangrily\b|\bthreaten\b/.test(lower)) return "aggressive";
    }

    if (primaryType === "train") {
      return "practice";
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractAbility(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (primaryType !== "use_ability" && primaryType !== "train") {
      return "";
    }

    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:cast|channel|invoke|activate|unleash|focus on|practice|train)\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /\buse\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)\s+(?:on|at|against)\b/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractItem(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (
      primaryType !== "loot" &&
      primaryType !== "equip" &&
      primaryType !== "unequip" &&
      primaryType !== "consume_item" &&
      primaryType !== "barter" &&
      primaryType !== "interact"
    ) {
      return "";
    }

    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:pick up|grab|take|loot|collect|steal|equip|wear|put on|draw|wield|remove|take off|drink|eat|consume|buy|sell|trade|use)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|my\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractDestination(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (primaryType !== "travel" && primaryType !== "move") {
      return "";
    }

    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:go|walk|run|move|travel|journey|head|return|enter|leave|climb|crawl)\s+(?:to|toward|towards|into|inside|outside|back to)?\s*(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractTrainingFocus(rawText, primaryType) {
    if (primaryType !== "train") {
      return "";
    }

    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:train|practice|drill|exercise|meditate|spar|work on)\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = rawText.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanHint(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  cleanHint(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\b(?:using|with|at|against|toward|towards|into|to)\b\s*$/i, "")
    .trim();
}
},

    // === PROGRESSION SYSTEM ===
ProgressionSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.player || typeof root.player !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player)) {
      root.player = {};
    }

    if (!root.player.levelState || typeof root.player.levelState !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player.levelState)) {
      root.player.levelState = {};
    }

    const levelState = root.player.levelState;

    if (typeof levelState.level !== "number" || levelState.level < 1) levelState.level = 1;
    if (typeof levelState.xp !== "number" || levelState.xp < 0) levelState.xp = 0;
    if (typeof levelState.totalXp !== "number" || levelState.totalXp < 0) levelState.totalXp = 0;
    if (typeof levelState.growthClass !== "string" || !levelState.growthClass) {
      levelState.growthClass = "standard";
    }

    if (!levelState.history || typeof levelState.history !== "object" || Array.isArray(levelState.history)) {
      levelState.history = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(levelState.history.recentGains)) {
      levelState.history.recentGains = [];
    }

    if (!levelState.antiFarm || typeof levelState.antiFarm !== "object" || Array.isArray(levelState.antiFarm)) {
      levelState.antiFarm = {};
    }

    if (typeof levelState.antiFarm.lastSource !== "string") levelState.antiFarm.lastSource = "";
    if (typeof levelState.antiFarm.repeatCount !== "number" || levelState.antiFarm.repeatCount < 0) {
      levelState.antiFarm.repeatCount = 0;
    }

    if (typeof levelState.lastGainTurn !== "number" || levelState.lastGainTurn < 0) {
      levelState.lastGainTurn = 0;
    }

    const expectedXpToNext = this.xpNeededForLevel(levelState.level, levelState.growthClass);
    if (typeof levelState.xpToNext !== "number" || levelState.xpToNext <= 0) {
      levelState.xpToNext = expectedXpToNext;
    }

    return levelState;
  },

  xpNeededForLevel(level, growthClass) {
    const lvl = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(level || 1)));
    const growth = String(growthClass || "standard");

    let base = 100 + ((lvl - 1) * 25) + Math.floor(((lvl - 1) * (lvl - 1)) * 0.5);

    if (growth === "accelerated") {
      base = Math.max(80, Math.floor(base * 0.85));
    } else if (growth === "distorted") {
      base = Math.floor(base * 1.15);
    } else if (growth === "cursed") {
      base = Math.floor(base * 1.25);
    }

    return base;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const player = root.player || {};
    const levelState = player.levelState || {};
    const intent = root.pending && root.pending.inputIntent ? root.pending.inputIntent : null;

    if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") {
      return 0;
    }

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const cleanText = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
      ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
      : String(rawText || "").trim();

    if (!cleanText) {
      return 0;
    }

    const source = this.classifyIntent(intent);
    const baseXp = this.baseXpForSource(source, intent, cleanText);
    if (baseXp <= 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    const multiplier = this.antiFarmMultiplier(levelState, source);
    const gained = Math.max(0, Math.floor(baseXp * multiplier));

    if (gained <= 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    return this.awardXp(gained, source, intent.primaryType, root);
  },

  classifyIntent(intent) {
    const type = String(intent.primaryType || "");

    if (type === "attack" || type === "defend" || type === "use_ability") {
      return "combat";
    }

    if (type === "train") {
      return "training";
    }

    if (type === "travel" || type === "move" || type === "inspect") {
      return "exploration";
    }

    if (type === "talk" || type === "barter") {
      return "social";
    }

    if (
      type === "loot" ||
      type === "equip" ||
      type === "unequip" ||
      type === "consume_item" ||
      type === "interact"
    ) {
      return "logistics";
    }

    if (type === "rest") {
      return "recovery";
    }

    return "general";
  },

  baseXpForSource(source, intent, rawText) {
    const type = String(intent.primaryType || "");
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();

    if (source === "combat") {
      let xp = 3;
      if (/\b(hit|strike|wound|block|parry|fight|battle|bleed|stagger)\b/.test(lower)) {
        xp += 1;
      }
      return xp;
    }

    if (source === "training") {
      let xp = 3;
      if (/\b(practice|repeat|drill|exercise|meditate|focus|spar)\b/.test(lower)) {
        xp += 1;
      }
      return xp;
    }

    if (source === "exploration") {
      if (type === "inspect") return 2;
      if (type === "travel") return 2;
      return 1;
    }

    if (source === "social") {
      return 1;
    }

    if (source === "logistics") {
      if (type === "loot" || type === "consume_item") return 2;
      return 1;
    }

    if (source === "recovery") {
      return 1;
    }

    return 1;
  },

  antiFarmMultiplier(levelState, source) {
    const antiFarm = levelState.antiFarm || {};

    if (antiFarm.lastSource !== source) {
      antiFarm.lastSource = source;
      antiFarm.repeatCount = 0;
      levelState.antiFarm = antiFarm;
      return 1;
    }

    antiFarm.repeatCount += 1;
    levelState.antiFarm = antiFarm;

    if (antiFarm.repeatCount === 1) return 0.67;
    if (antiFarm.repeatCount === 2) return 0.5;
    return 0.34;
  },

  awardXp(amount, source, note, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const player = root.player || {};
    const levelState = player.levelState || {};
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    levelState.xp += amount;
    levelState.totalXp += amount;
    levelState.lastGainTurn = turn;

    if (!Array.isArray(levelState.history.recentGains)) {
      levelState.history.recentGains = [];
    }

    levelState.history.recentGains.push({
      turn: turn,
      amount: amount,
      source: String(source || "general"),
      note: String(note || "")
    });

    while (levelState.history.recentGains.length > 12) {
      levelState.history.recentGains.shift();
    }

    let leveled = 0;

    while (levelState.xp >= levelState.xpToNext) {
      levelState.xp -= levelState.xpToNext;
      levelState.level += 1;
      leveled += 1;

      this.applyLevelUpBenefits(root);

      levelState.xpToNext = this.xpNeededForLevel(levelState.level, levelState.growthClass);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("player", true, root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("XP +" + amount + " from " + source + (leveled ? " | level up +" + leveled : ""));
    }

    return amount;
  },

  applyLevelUpBenefits(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;

    const player = root.player || {};
    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const build = root.build || {};

    if (typeof stats.maxHp === "number") {
      stats.maxHp += 10;
      stats.hp = stats.maxHp;
    }

    if (typeof stats.maxEp === "number") {
      stats.maxEp += 4;
      stats.ep = stats.maxEp;
    }

    if (typeof stats.maxMp === "number") {
      stats.maxMp += 2;
      stats.mp = stats.maxMp;
    }

    const specs = Array.isArray(build.specializationTags) ? build.specializationTags : [];

    if (specs.indexOf("power_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.atk === "number") {
      stats.atk += 1;
    } else if (specs.indexOf("resilience_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.def === "number") {
      stats.def += 1;
    } else if (specs.indexOf("speed_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.spd === "number") {
      stats.spd += 1;
    } else if (specs.indexOf("technical_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.intl === "number") {
      stats.intl += 1;
    } else if (specs.indexOf("fortune_bias") !== -1 && typeof stats.lck === "number") {
      stats.lck += 1;
    } else if (typeof stats.atk === "number") {
      stats.atk += 1;
    }

    if (globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived === "function") {
      globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem.recomputeDerived(player);
    }
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !root.player || !root.player.levelState) {
      return "Progression summary unavailable.";
    }

    const levelState = root.player.levelState;
    return "Level " + levelState.level + " | XP " + levelState.xp + "/" + levelState.xpToNext + " | Total XP " + levelState.totalXp;
  }
},

    // === INVENTORY SYSTEM ===
InventorySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.items || typeof root.items !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.items)) {
      root.items = {};
    }

    const items = root.items;

    if (!items.byId || typeof items.byId !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.byId)) {
      items.byId = {};
    }

    if (!items.ownership || typeof items.ownership !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.ownership)) {
      items.ownership = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(items.ownership.player)) {
      items.ownership.player = [];
    }

    if (!items.ownership.storage || typeof items.ownership.storage !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.ownership.storage)) {
      items.ownership.storage = {};
    }

    if (!items.ownership.actors || typeof items.ownership.actors !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.ownership.actors)) {
      items.ownership.actors = {};
    }

    if (!items.equipment || typeof items.equipment !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.equipment)) {
      items.equipment = {};
    }

    const equipment = items.equipment;

    if (!("mainHand" in equipment)) equipment.mainHand = null;
    if (!("offHand" in equipment)) equipment.offHand = null;
    if (!("head" in equipment)) equipment.head = null;
    if (!("body" in equipment)) equipment.body = null;
    if (!("hands" in equipment)) equipment.hands = null;
    if (!("legs" in equipment)) equipment.legs = null;
    if (!("feet" in equipment)) equipment.feet = null;
    if (!("accessory1" in equipment)) equipment.accessory1 = null;
    if (!("accessory2" in equipment)) equipment.accessory2 = null;

    if (!items.currency || typeof items.currency !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.currency)) {
      items.currency = {};
    }

    if (!items.currency.byType || typeof items.currency.byType !== "object" || Array.isArray(items.currency.byType)) {
      items.currency.byType = {};
    }

    if (typeof items.currency.byType.copper !== "number") items.currency.byType.copper = 0;
    if (typeof items.currency.byType.silver !== "number") items.currency.byType.silver = 0;
    if (typeof items.currency.byType.gold !== "number") items.currency.byType.gold = 0;

    if (!Array.isArray(items.pendingNormalization)) {
      items.pendingNormalization = [];
    }

    this.ensureAllOwnedRecordsHaveShape(items);
    this.ensureEquipmentIntegrity(items);

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }
    if (typeof root.cache.visibleCarrySummary !== "string") {
      root.cache.visibleCarrySummary = "";
    }

    return items;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const text = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
    ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
    : String(rawText || "").trim();

  if (!text) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  const intent = root.pending && root.pending.inputIntent ? root.pending.inputIntent : null;
  let changes = 0;

  if (intent && typeof intent === "object") {
    changes += this.processIntentDrivenChanges(intent, text, root);
  }

  changes += this.processDirectStoryAcquisition(text, root);
  changes += this.processItemStateChanges(text, root);
  changes += this.normalizePending(root);
  changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Inventory changes: " + changes);
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
  const items = root.items || {};
  const byId = items.byId || {};
  const equipment = items.equipment || {};
  const currency = items.currency && items.currency.byType ? items.currency.byType : {};
  const intent = pending.inputIntent && typeof pending.inputIntent === "object" ? pending.inputIntent : null;

  let changes = 0;
  let currencyChanged = false;

  const sameConsumeIntent = (label) => {
    if (!intent || String(intent.primaryType || "") !== "consume_item") return false;

    const hint = String(intent.itemHint || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    const cleanLabel = String(label || "").toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!hint || !cleanLabel) return false;

    return hint === cleanLabel
      || hint.indexOf(cleanLabel) !== -1
      || cleanLabel.indexOf(hint) !== -1;
  };

  const preferredWornItemId = () => {
    const slots = ["body", "head", "hands", "legs", "feet", "mainHand", "offHand", "accessory1", "accessory2"];

    for (let i = 0; i < slots.length; i += 1) {
      const itemId = equipment[slots[i]];
      if (typeof itemId === "string" && itemId && byId[itemId]) {
        return itemId;
      }
    }

    return "";
  };

  const damageLossForTag = (tag) => {
    const lower = String(tag || "").toLowerCase();

    if (lower.indexOf("broken") !== -1) return 35;
    if (lower.indexOf("burn") !== -1) return 20;
    if (lower.indexOf("torn") !== -1) return 18;
    if (lower.indexOf("rust") !== -1) return 10;
    return 15;
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "item" || candidate._itemApplied === true) {
      continue;
    }

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const target =
      candidate.type === "currency_change"
        ? String(payload.currency || "currency")
        : candidate.type === "item_consumed" || candidate.type === "item_damage"
        ? String(payload.itemLabel || "item")
        : String(candidate.type || "item");

    const commit = () => {
      let localChanges = 0;

      if (candidate.type === "currency_change") {
        const currencyType = String(payload.currency || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        const amount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(payload.amount || 0)));
        const direction = String(payload.direction || "").trim().toLowerCase();

        if ((currencyType === "copper" || currencyType === "silver" || currencyType === "gold") && amount > 0) {
          const currentValue = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(currency[currencyType] || 0)));
          const nextValue = direction === "loss"
            ? Math.max(0, currentValue - amount)
            : currentValue + amount;

          if (currency[currencyType] !== nextValue) {
            currency[currencyType] = nextValue;
            localChanges += 1;
            currencyChanged = true;
          }
        }
      } else if (candidate.type === "item_consumed") {
        const label = this.cleanPhrase(payload.itemLabel || "");

        if (label && !sameConsumeIntent(label)) {
          const item = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(label, root);
          if (item && item.id) {
            if (this.consumeItem(item.id, root)) {
              localChanges += 1;
            }
          }
        }
      } else if (candidate.type === "item_damage") {
        const label = this.cleanPhrase(payload.itemLabel || "");
        const damageTag = this.cleanPhrase(payload.damageTag || "damaged") || "damaged";

        let itemId = "";

        if (label === "worn gear") {
          itemId = preferredWornItemId();
        } else if (label) {
          const matched = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(label, root);
          itemId = matched && matched.id ? matched.id : "";
        }

        if (itemId && byId[itemId]) {
          const item = byId[itemId];
          this.ensureItemShape(item);

          const alreadyTagged = item.condition
            && Array.isArray(item.condition.stateTags)
            && item.condition.stateTags.indexOf(damageTag) !== -1;

          if (!alreadyTagged) {
            if (this.applyStateTag(itemId, damageTag, damageLossForTag(damageTag), root)) {
              localChanges += 1;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      return localChanges;
    };

    const localChanges =
      Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
        ? Guard.applyOnce({
            kind: "item_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
            target: target,
            payload: payload,
            sourceId: candidate.id || ""
          }, commit, root)
        : commit();

    changes += localChanges;
    candidate._itemApplied = true;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);

    if (currencyChanged && U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("items", "currency", root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Validated item consequences applied.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  processIntentDrivenChanges(intent, text, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") return 0;
  if (this.isClearlyFailedAction(text)) return 0;

  let changes = 0;
  const type = String(intent.primaryType || "");
  const itemHint = this.cleanItemPhrase(String(intent.itemHint || "").trim());

  if (type === "loot" || type === "barter") {
    const phrases = this.extractAcquisitionPhrases(text);

    if (phrases.length) {
      for (let i = 0; i < phrases.length; i += 1) {
        if (this.queueNormalizationCandidate(phrases[i], "player", "packed", "story_gain", root)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }
    } else if (itemHint && this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(itemHint)) {
      if (this.queueNormalizationCandidate(itemHint, "player", "packed", "intent_gain", root)) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    }
  }

  if (type === "equip" && itemHint) {
    const item = this.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(itemHint, "carried", "equip_intent", root);
    if (item) {
      const slot = this.inferSlotFromItem(item);
      if (slot) {
        if (this.equipItem(slot, item.id, root)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (type === "unequip") {
    if (itemHint) {
      const item = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(itemHint, root);
      if (item && item.slot) {
        if (this.unequipSlot(item.slot, root)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      } else {
        const guessedSlot = this.inferSlotFromPhrase(itemHint);
        if (guessedSlot) {
          if (this.unequipSlot(guessedSlot, root)) {
            changes += 1;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (type === "consume_item" && itemHint) {
    const item = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(itemHint, root);
    if (item) {
      if (this.consumeItem(item.id, root)) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  processDirectStoryAcquisition(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const phrases = this.extractAcquisitionPhrases(text);
  let changes = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < phrases.length; i += 1) {
    if (this.queueNormalizationCandidate(phrases[i], "player", "packed", "story_gain", root)) {
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  processItemStateChanges(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const patterns = [
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:breaks|break|shatters|shatter|snaps|snap|is destroyed|are destroyed|is ruined|are ruined)\b/gi, tag: "broken", delta: 45 },
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:is torn|are torn|tears|tear|rips|rip|is cut|are cut|gets cut|get cut)\b/gi, tag: "torn", delta: 18 },
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:is damaged|are damaged|dents|dent|is dented|are dented|cracks|crack|is cracked|are cracked)\b/gi, tag: "damaged", delta: 15 },
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:burns|burn|is burned|are burned|scorches|scorch|is scorched|are scorched)\b/gi, tag: "burned", delta: 20 },
      { regex: /\b(?:your|the)\s+([^.!?;,]+?)\s+(?:rusts|rust|is rusted|are rusted)\b/gi, tag: "rusted", delta: 10 }
    ];

    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const pattern = patterns[i];
      let match;

      while ((match = pattern.regex.exec(text))) {
        const phrase = match[1] ? this.cleanPhrase(match[1]) : "";
        if (!phrase) continue;

        const item = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(phrase, root);
        if (!item) continue;

        if (this.applyStateTag(item.id, pattern.tag, pattern.delta, root)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  isClearlyFailedAction(text) {
    return /\b(fails?|failed|cannot|can't|unable to|could not|couldn't|refuses?|refused|without managing to)\b/i.test(String(text || ""));
  },

  extractAcquisitionPhrases(text) {
  const raw = String(text || "");
  const out = [];

  const addCandidate = (value) => {
    if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(value)) return;

    const cleaned = this.cleanItemPhrase(value);
    if (!cleaned) return;
    if (!this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(cleaned)) return;

    if (out.indexOf(cleaned) === -1) {
      out.push(cleaned);
    }
  };

  // Phase 6 inventory hardening:
  // Only player-received/player-acquired wording may create inventory.
  // This blocks lines like "the guard takes your sword" from granting items.
  const patterns = [
    /\byou\s+(?:pick(?:s|ed)?\s+up|grab(?:s|bed)?|take|takes|took|collect(?:s|ed)?|gather(?:s|ed)?|loot(?:s|ed)?|snatch(?:es|ed)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?|find(?:s|found)?|buy|buys|bought|purchase(?:s|d)?|receive(?:s|d)?|accept(?:s|ed)?)\s+([^.!?;\n]+)/gi,
    /\byou\s+are\s+(?:given|handed|offered|awarded|presented\s+with)\s+([^.!?;\n]+)/gi,
    /\b(?:someone|somebody|a\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|an\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{1,32}|[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,40})\s+(?:gives|gave|hands|handed|offers|offered|passes|passed)\s+you\s+([^.!?;\n]+)/g,
    /\b([^.!?;\n]{1,80}?)\s+(?:is|are)\s+(?:placed|pressed|dropped|slipped|tucked)\s+(?:into|in|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|sack|inventory|pocket)\b/gi
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
    let match;
    while ((match = patterns[i].exec(raw))) {
      if (!match[1]) continue;

      const pieces = this.splitAcquisitionCapture(match[1]);
      for (let p = 0; p < pieces.length; p += 1) {
        addCandidate(pieces[p]);
      }
    }
  }

  out.sort((a, b) => {
    const wordDiff = a.split(/\s+/).length - b.split(/\s+/).length;
    if (wordDiff !== 0) return wordDiff;
    return a.length - b.length;
  });

  return out;
},

splitAcquisitionCapture(value) {
  let text = String(value || "")
    .replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return [];

  text = text.split(/[.;!?]/, 1)[0];
  text = text.replace(/\s*,[\s\S]*$/, "");
  text = text.replace(/\s+(?:and then|then|but then|so then)\b[\s\S]*$/i, "");

  const rough = text.split(/\s+(?:and|plus)\s+/i);
  const out = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < rough.length && i < 4; i += 1) {
    const part = String(rough[i] || "").trim();
    if (part) out.push(part);
  }

  return out.length ? out : [text];
},

shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(value) {
  const lower = String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[“”"'`]+/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!lower) return true;

  // These are usually contact, movement, or idiom phrases, not inventory gain.
  if (/^(?:at|to|toward|towards|against|into|onto|in|on|from|with|using|by|behind|beside|near|around|through|across|along|under|over|beneath)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return true;
  }

  if (/^(?:hold of|ahold of|cover|care|notice|a look|the look|look|a glance|the glance|glance|a breath|the breath|breath|a moment|the moment|moment|a chance|the chance|chance|a rest|the rest|rest|a seat|the seat|seat)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return true;
  }

  if (/\b(?:in stride|in the sight|in the view|cover behind|cover under|hold of)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
},

cleanItemPhrase(value) {
  let text = String(value || "")
    .replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/[“”"'`]+/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return "";
  if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return "";

  text = this.trimAcquisitionClause(text);

  text = text
    .replace(/^(?:up|out)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|some|my|your)\s+/i, "")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return "";
  if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return "";

  const tokens = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (!tokens.length) return "";

  const headIndex = this.findConcreteItemHeadIndex(tokens);
  if (headIndex === -1) return "";

  const secondToken = tokens.length > 1 ? String(tokens[1] || "").toLowerCase() : "";

  if (headIndex === 0 && secondToken === "of") {
    text = tokens.slice(0, Math.min(tokens.length, 5)).join(" ");
  } else {
    const start = Math.max(0, headIndex - 3);
    text = tokens.slice(start, headIndex + 1).join(" ");
  }

  text = text
    .replace(/^(?:another|other)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return "";
  if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return "";

  return text;
},

trimAcquisitionClause(value) {
  let text = String(value || "");

  text = text.split(/[.;!?]/, 1)[0];

  text = text.replace(
    /\s+(?:and then|then|but then|so then|and|as|while|because|when|before|after|with|using|into|onto|from|at|to|toward|towards|against|behind|beside|near|around|through|across|along|under|over|beneath|where|which|that|who|without|instead|rather than)\b[\s\S]*$/i,
    ""
  );

  text = text.replace(/\s*,[\s\S]*$/, "");
  return text.trim();
},

isItemHeadToken(value) {
  const raw = String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");

  if (!raw) return false;

  const singular = raw.length > 3 && raw.endsWith("s") ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;

  const itemHeads = new Set([
    "stick", "branch", "pole", "staff", "rod", "post", "spear", "lance",
    "sword", "greatsword", "longsword", "shortsword", "dagger", "knife", "blade",
    "axe", "mace", "hammer", "club", "cudgel", "bow", "crossbow", "wand",
    "shield", "torch", "rope", "cord", "chain", "key", "lockpick", "coin", "purse",
    "bag", "sack", "pack", "pouch", "satchel", "chest", "box", "case", "bundle",
    "cloak", "shirt", "tunic", "robe", "coat", "armor", "helm", "helmet", "hood",
    "boot", "boots", "glove", "gloves", "gauntlet", "gauntlets", "pant", "pants",
    "trouser", "trousers", "shoe", "shoes", "hat", "ring", "amulet", "necklace",
    "pendant", "bracelet", "charm", "token", "potion", "tonic", "elixir", "vial",
    "herb", "ration", "bread", "meat", "flask", "bottle", "waterskin", "lantern",
    "book", "scroll", "map", "letter", "note", "paper", "document", "gem", "crystal",
    "orb", "relic", "artifact", "stone", "ore", "ingot", "tool", "crutch", "board", "plank",
    "needle", "thread", "cloth", "bandage", "feather", "fang", "claw", "scale", "shell"
  ]);

  return itemHeads.has(raw) || itemHeads.has(singular);
},

findConcreteItemHeadIndex(tokens) {
  if (!Array.isArray(tokens) || !tokens.length) return -1;

  const lastIndex = tokens.length - 1;
  if (this.isItemHeadToken(tokens[lastIndex])) {
    return lastIndex;
  }

  // Allow "flask of water" / "bottle of oil" style phrases where the
  // container is the actual item head.
  if (tokens.length >= 3 && String(tokens[1] || "").toLowerCase() === "of" && this.isItemHeadToken(tokens[0])) {
    return 0;
  }

  return -1;
},

isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(phrase) {
  const text = String(phrase || "").toLowerCase().trim();
  if (!text) return false;
  if (this.shouldRejectAcquisitionCapture(text)) return false;

  const abstractWords = new Set([
    "look", "glance", "effort", "pain", "nausea", "movement", "thought",
    "breath", "silence", "chance", "sound", "voice", "moment", "time",
    "focus", "haziness", "haze", "weight", "fear", "attention", "pulse",
    "cover", "seat", "rest", "damage", "hit", "stride", "view", "sight",
    "information", "news", "rumor", "rumour", "permission", "warning"
  ]);

  const sceneWords = new Set([
    "road", "path", "trail", "street", "lane", "room", "hall", "floor",
    "wall", "ceiling", "doorway", "market", "chapel", "smithy", "tavern",
    "inn", "forest", "woods", "cave", "cellar", "stairs", "bridge"
  ]);

  const tokens = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (!tokens.length) return false;
  if (tokens.length > 6) return false;

  for (let i = 0; i < tokens.length; i += 1) {
    const token = String(tokens[i] || "").replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");
    if (abstractWords.has(token)) return false;
  }

  const headIndex = this.findConcreteItemHeadIndex(tokens);
  if (headIndex === -1) return false;

  const headToken = String(tokens[headIndex] || "").replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/g, "");
  if (sceneWords.has(headToken)) return false;

  return true;
},

  queueNormalizationCandidate(phrase, holder, visibility, sourceType, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
  this.init(root);

  const cleaned = this.cleanItemPhrase(phrase);
  if (!cleaned) return false;
  if (!this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(cleaned)) return false;

  const pending = root.items.pendingNormalization;
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < pending.length; i += 1) {
    const row = pending[i];
    if (
      row &&
      row.phrase === cleaned &&
      row.holder === String(holder || "player") &&
      row.visibility === String(visibility || "packed") &&
      row.turn === turn
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  pending.push({
    phrase: cleaned,
    holder: String(holder || "player"),
    visibility: String(visibility || "packed"),
    sourceType: String(sourceType || "unknown"),
    turn: turn
  });

  return true;
},

  normalizePending(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.items.pendingNormalization;
    let changes = 0;

    while (pending.length > 0) {
      const candidate = pending.shift();
      if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") continue;

      const item = this.ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(
        candidate.phrase,
        candidate.visibility || "packed",
        candidate.sourceType || "story_gain",
        root
      );

      if (item) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  ensurePlayerItemFromPhrase(phrase, visibility, sourceContext, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
  this.init(root);

  const cleaned = this.cleanItemPhrase(phrase);
  if (!cleaned) return null;
  if (!this.isLikelyConcreteItemPhrase(cleaned)) return null;

  const existing = this.findMatchingPlayerItem(cleaned, root);
  if (existing) {
    if (existing.ownership) {
      existing.ownership.holder = "player";
      if (visibility) existing.ownership.visibility = visibility;
    }
    return existing;
  }

  const seed = this.makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase(cleaned, sourceContext);
  if (!seed.displayName) return null;

  const item = this.registerItem(seed, root);
  if (!item) return null;

  this.ensurePlayerOwns(item.id, root);

  if (item.ownership) {
    item.ownership.visibility = visibility || "packed";
  }

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
    U.markDirty("items", item.id, root);
  }

  return item;
},

  makeNormalizedSeedFromPhrase(phrase, sourceContext) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const cleaned = this.cleanPhrase(phrase);
    const lower = cleaned.toLowerCase();

    const baseType = this.detectBaseType(lower);
    const slot = this.inferSlotFromBaseType(baseType);
    const descriptors = this.extractDescriptors(cleaned, baseType);
    const materialTags = this.extractMaterialTags(lower);
    const quality = this.extractQuality(lower);

    return {
      displayName: cleaned,
      baseType: baseType,
      descriptors: descriptors,
      materialTags: materialTags,
      quality: quality,
      slot: slot,
      stackable: this.isLikelyStackable(baseType),
      quantity: 1,
      ownership: {
        holder: "player",
        visibility: "packed"
      },
      notes: {
        sourceContext: String(sourceContext || ""),
        observedChanges: []
      }
    };
  },

  detectBaseType(lowerPhrase) {
    const known = [
      "greatsword",
      "longsword",
      "shortsword",
      "dagger",
      "sword",
      "axe",
      "mace",
      "hammer",
      "spear",
      "staff",
      "wand",
      "bow",
      "crossbow",
      "shield",
      "helmet",
      "helm",
      "hood",
      "armor",
      "robe",
      "cloak",
      "coat",
      "shirt",
      "gloves",
      "gauntlets",
      "pants",
      "trousers",
      "boots",
      "shoes",
      "ring",
      "amulet",
      "necklace",
      "potion",
      "vial",
      "flask",
      "scroll",
      "book",
      "key",
      "map",
      "coin",
      "ration",
      "bread",
      "meat",
      "herb",
      "gem",
      "clothes",
      "outfit"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < known.length; i += 1) {
      if (lowerPhrase.indexOf(known[i]) !== -1) {
        return known[i];
      }
    }

    const words = lowerPhrase.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (words.length) {
      return words[words.length - 1];
    }

    return "item";
  },

  inferSlotFromPhrase(phrase) {
    return this.inferSlotFromBaseType(this.detectBaseType(String(phrase || "").toLowerCase()));
  },

  inferSlotFromBaseType(baseType) {
    const key = String(baseType || "").toLowerCase();

    if (key === "sword" || key === "greatsword" || key === "longsword" || key === "shortsword" || key === "dagger" || key === "axe" || key === "mace" || key === "hammer" || key === "spear" || key === "staff" || key === "wand" || key === "bow" || key === "crossbow") {
      return "mainHand";
    }

    if (key === "shield") return "offHand";
    if (key === "helmet" || key === "helm" || key === "hood") return "head";
    if (key === "armor" || key === "robe" || key === "cloak" || key === "coat" || key === "shirt" || key === "clothes" || key === "outfit") return "body";
    if (key === "gloves" || key === "gauntlets") return "hands";
    if (key === "pants" || key === "trousers") return "legs";
    if (key === "boots" || key === "shoes") return "feet";
    if (key === "ring") return "accessory1";
    if (key === "amulet" || key === "necklace") return "accessory2";

    return "";
  },

  inferSlotFromItem(item) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
    if (item.slot && String(item.slot).trim()) return String(item.slot).trim();
    return this.inferSlotFromBaseType(item.baseType || "");
  },

  extractDescriptors(cleanedPhrase, baseType) {
    const lowerBase = String(baseType || "").toLowerCase();
    const words = String(cleanedPhrase || "").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i += 1) {
      const w = words[i].toLowerCase();
      if (w === lowerBase) break;
      if (w === "a" || w === "an" || w === "the" || w === "my" || w === "some") continue;
      out.push(words[i]);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractMaterialTags(lowerPhrase) {
    const known = ["iron", "steel", "wooden", "wood", "leather", "cloth", "bronze", "silver", "gold", "stone", "bone"];
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < known.length; i += 1) {
      if (lowerPhrase.indexOf(known[i]) !== -1 && out.indexOf(known[i]) === -1) {
        out.push(known[i]);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractQuality(lowerPhrase) {
    const known = ["rusty", "fine", "worn", "broken", "poor", "crude", "sturdy", "ornate", "simple"];
    for (let i = 0; i < known.length; i += 1) {
      if (lowerPhrase.indexOf(known[i]) !== -1) {
        return known[i];
      }
    }
    return "";
  },

  isLikelyStackable(baseType) {
    const key = String(baseType || "").toLowerCase();
    return key === "coin" || key === "ration" || key === "herb" || key === "gem" || key === "potion" || key === "scroll";
  },

  findMatchingPlayerItem(query, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    this.init(root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const key = U.toKey(String(query || ""));
    if (!key) return null;

    const owned = root.items && root.items.ownership && Array.isArray(root.items.ownership.player)
      ? root.items.ownership.player
      : [];

    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i < owned.length; i += 1) {
      const itemId = owned[i];
      const item = root.items.byId[itemId];
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;

      const displayKey = U.toKey(this.getItemDisplayName(item));
      const baseKey = U.toKey(item.baseType || "");

      let score = 0;

      if (displayKey === key) score = 100;
      else if (baseKey === key) score = 95;
      else if (displayKey && displayKey.indexOf(key) !== -1) score = 80;
      else if (displayKey && key.indexOf(displayKey) !== -1) score = 70;
      else if (baseKey && key.indexOf(baseKey) !== -1) score = 60;

      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        best = item;
      }
    }

    // Phase 6/7u1 safety:
    // Never let a zero-score "best" match select the first owned item.
    // This was causing multi-item pickup to save only the first item, e.g.
    // "coins and a torch" became coins only because torch matched coins at score 0.
    return bestScore >= 60 ? best : null;
  },

  consumeItem(itemId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const item = root.items.byId[itemId];
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return false;

    if (item.quantity > 1) {
      item.quantity -= 1;
    } else {
      this.removeItem(itemId, root);
    }

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("items", itemId, root);
    }

    return true;
  },

  removeItem(itemId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!items.byId[itemId]) return false;

    U.removeValue(items.ownership.player, itemId);

    for (const slot in items.equipment) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(items.equipment, slot)) continue;
      if (items.equipment[slot] === itemId) {
        items.equipment[slot] = null;
      }
    }

    delete items.byId[itemId];
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
    return true;
  },

  applyStateTag(itemId, stateTag, durabilityLoss, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const item = root.items.byId[itemId];
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return false;

    this.ensureItemShape(item);

    const tag = String(stateTag || "").trim();
    if (!tag) return false;

    if (item.condition.stateTags.indexOf(tag) === -1) {
      item.condition.stateTags.push(tag);
    }

    const loss = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(durabilityLoss || 0)));
    item.condition.durability = Math.max(0, item.condition.durability - loss);

    if (item.notes && Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) {
      item.notes.observedChanges.push(tag);
      while (item.notes.observedChanges.length > 12) {
        item.notes.observedChanges.shift();
      }
    }

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("items", itemId, root);
    }

    return true;
  },

  cleanPhrase(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/^(?:a|an|the|my|some)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+(?:from|for)\s+.+$/i, "")
      .trim();
  },

  createItemRecord(seed) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const data = seed && typeof seed === "object" ? seed : {};

    return {
      id: typeof data.id === "string" && data.id ? data.id : U.makeId("item"),
      displayName: typeof data.displayName === "string" ? data.displayName : "",
      baseType: typeof data.baseType === "string" ? data.baseType : "",
      descriptors: Array.isArray(data.descriptors) ? data.descriptors.slice() : [],
      materialTags: Array.isArray(data.materialTags) ? data.materialTags.slice() : [],
      quality: typeof data.quality === "string" ? data.quality : "",
      condition: {
        durability: data.condition && typeof data.condition.durability === "number" ? data.condition.durability : 100,
        stateTags: data.condition && Array.isArray(data.condition.stateTags) ? data.condition.stateTags.slice() : []
      },
      slot: typeof data.slot === "string" ? data.slot : "",
      stackable: data.stackable === true,
      quantity: typeof data.quantity === "number" && data.quantity > 0 ? Math.floor(data.quantity) : 1,
      stats: data.stats && typeof data.stats === "object" && !Array.isArray(data.stats) ? data.stats : {},
      ownership: {
        holder: data.ownership && typeof data.ownership.holder === "string" ? data.ownership.holder : "player",
        visibility: data.ownership && typeof data.ownership.visibility === "string" ? data.ownership.visibility : "packed"
      },
      notes: {
        sourceContext: data.notes && typeof data.notes.sourceContext === "string" ? data.notes.sourceContext : "",
        observedChanges: data.notes && Array.isArray(data.notes.observedChanges) ? data.notes.observedChanges.slice() : []
      }
    };
  },

  ensureItemShape(item) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return item;

    if (typeof item.id !== "string") item.id = "";
    if (typeof item.displayName !== "string") item.displayName = "";
    if (typeof item.baseType !== "string") item.baseType = "";
    if (!Array.isArray(item.descriptors)) item.descriptors = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(item.materialTags)) item.materialTags = [];
    if (typeof item.quality !== "string") item.quality = "";

    if (!item.condition || typeof item.condition !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.condition)) {
      item.condition = {};
    }
    if (typeof item.condition.durability !== "number") item.condition.durability = 100;
    if (!Array.isArray(item.condition.stateTags)) item.condition.stateTags = [];

    if (typeof item.slot !== "string") item.slot = "";
    if (typeof item.stackable !== "boolean") item.stackable = false;
    if (typeof item.quantity !== "number" || item.quantity <= 0) item.quantity = 1;

    if (!item.stats || typeof item.stats !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.stats)) {
      item.stats = {};
    }

    if (!item.ownership || typeof item.ownership !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.ownership)) {
      item.ownership = {};
    }
    if (typeof item.ownership.holder !== "string") item.ownership.holder = "player";
    if (typeof item.ownership.visibility !== "string") item.ownership.visibility = "packed";

    if (!item.notes || typeof item.notes !== "object" || Array.isArray(item.notes)) {
      item.notes = {};
    }
    if (typeof item.notes.sourceContext !== "string") item.notes.sourceContext = "";
    if (!Array.isArray(item.notes.observedChanges)) item.notes.observedChanges = [];

    return item;
  },

  ensureAllOwnedRecordsHaveShape(items) {
    if (!items || typeof items !== "object" || !items.byId) return;

    for (const itemId in items.byId) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(items.byId, itemId)) continue;
      this.ensureItemShape(items.byId[itemId]);
      if (!items.byId[itemId].id) {
        items.byId[itemId].id = itemId;
      }
    }
  },

  ensureEquipmentIntegrity(items) {
    if (!items || typeof items !== "object") return;

    const equipment = items.equipment || {};
    const byId = items.byId || {};

    for (const slot in equipment) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(equipment, slot)) continue;

      const itemId = equipment[slot];
      if (!itemId) continue;

      if (!byId[itemId] || typeof byId[itemId] !== "object") {
        equipment[slot] = null;
        continue;
      }

      byId[itemId].ownership.holder = "player";
      byId[itemId].ownership.visibility = "equipped";
    }
  },

  registerItem(seed, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const item = this.createItemRecord(seed);
    items.byId[item.id] = item;

    return item;
  },

  ensurePlayerOwns(itemId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!items.byId[itemId]) return;

    U.pushUnique(items.ownership.player, itemId);
    items.byId[itemId].ownership.holder = "player";

    if (!items.byId[itemId].ownership.visibility) {
      items.byId[itemId].ownership.visibility = "packed";
    }
  },

  equipItem(slot, itemId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!items.equipment || !(slot in items.equipment)) return false;
    if (!items.byId[itemId]) return false;

    const oldItemId = items.equipment[slot];
    if (oldItemId && items.byId[oldItemId]) {
      items.byId[oldItemId].ownership.visibility = "packed";
    }

    items.equipment[slot] = itemId;
    this.ensurePlayerOwns(itemId, root);

    items.byId[itemId].slot = slot;
    items.byId[itemId].ownership.visibility = "equipped";

    U.markDirty("items", itemId, root);
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
    return true;
  },

  unequipSlot(slot, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return false;
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    if (!items.equipment || !(slot in items.equipment)) return false;

    const itemId = items.equipment[slot];
    if (!itemId || !items.byId[itemId]) {
      items.equipment[slot] = null;
      return false;
    }

    items.equipment[slot] = null;
    items.byId[itemId].ownership.visibility = "packed";

    U.markDirty("items", itemId, root);
    this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
    return true;
  },

  addCurrency(type, amount, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const key = String(type || "").trim();
    const n = Math.floor(Number(amount || 0));
    if (!(key in root.items.currency.byType) || !Number.isFinite(n) || n === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    root.items.currency.byType[key] += n;
    if (root.items.currency.byType[key] < 0) {
      root.items.currency.byType[key] = 0;
    }

    return root.items.currency.byType[key];
  },

  getVisibleCarryItems(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return [];
    this.init(root);

    const items = root.items;
    const byId = items.byId || {};
    const owned = Array.isArray(items.ownership.player) ? items.ownership.player : [];
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < owned.length; i += 1) {
      const itemId = owned[i];
      const item = byId[itemId];
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;

      const visibility = item.ownership && typeof item.ownership.visibility === "string"
        ? item.ownership.visibility
        : "packed";

      if (visibility === "equipped" || visibility === "carried") {
        out.push(item);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  getItemDisplayName(item) {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "(invalid item)";

    if (item.displayName && String(item.displayName).trim()) {
      return String(item.displayName).trim();
    }

    const parts = [];
    if (Array.isArray(item.descriptors) && item.descriptors.length) {
      for (let i = 0; i < item.descriptors.length; i += 1) {
        const part = String(item.descriptors[i] || "").trim();
        if (part) parts.push(part);
      }
    }

    const baseType = item.baseType && String(item.baseType).trim()
      ? String(item.baseType).trim()
      : "item";

    parts.push(baseType);
    return parts.join(" ");
  },

  refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";

    const visible = this.getVisibleCarryItems(root);
    const names = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < visible.length; i += 1) {
      names.push(this.getItemDisplayName(visible[i]));
    }

    const summary = names.length ? names.join(", ") : "nothing notable visible";

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }
    root.cache.visibleCarrySummary = summary;

    return summary;
  },

  getVisibleCarrySummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "nothing notable visible";
    this.init(root);

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    if (!root.cache.visibleCarrySummary || typeof root.cache.visibleCarrySummary !== "string") {
      return this.refreshVisibleCarrySummary(root);
    }

    return root.cache.visibleCarrySummary;
  }
},

    // === SCENE STATE SYSTEM ===
SceneStateSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

  if (!root.world || typeof root.world !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.world)) {
    root.world = {};
  }

  if (!root.world.currentScene || typeof root.world.currentScene !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.world.currentScene)) {
    root.world.currentScene = {};
  }

  const scene = root.world.currentScene;

  if (typeof scene.sceneRef !== "string") scene.sceneRef = "";
  if (typeof scene.displayName !== "string") scene.displayName = "";
  if (typeof scene.sceneType !== "string") scene.sceneType = "unknown";
  if (typeof scene.entityRef !== "string") scene.entityRef = "";
  if (typeof scene.parentLocationRef !== "string") scene.parentLocationRef = "";
  if (typeof scene.lastChangedTurn !== "number") scene.lastChangedTurn = 0;

  if (!Array.isArray(scene.localEntityIds)) scene.localEntityIds = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(scene.localActorIds)) scene.localActorIds = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(scene.localHazards)) scene.localHazards = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(scene.localExits)) scene.localExits = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(scene.conditionTags)) scene.conditionTags = [];

  if (!scene.localEntitiesById || typeof scene.localEntitiesById !== "object" || Array.isArray(scene.localEntitiesById)) {
    scene.localEntitiesById = {};
  }

  if (!scene.localActorsById || typeof scene.localActorsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(scene.localActorsById)) {
    scene.localActorsById = {};
  }

  if (typeof scene.sceneHintLabel !== "string") scene.sceneHintLabel = "";
  if (typeof scene.sceneHintStrength !== "number") scene.sceneHintStrength = 0;
  if (typeof scene.sceneHintTurn !== "number") scene.sceneHintTurn = 0;

  if (typeof scene.pendingSceneLabel !== "string") scene.pendingSceneLabel = "";
  if (typeof scene.pendingSceneStrength !== "number") scene.pendingSceneStrength = 0;
  if (typeof scene.pendingSceneCount !== "number") scene.pendingSceneCount = 0;
  if (typeof scene.pendingSceneTurn !== "number") scene.pendingSceneTurn = 0;

  if (typeof scene.sceneConfidence !== "number") scene.sceneConfidence = 0;

  if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
    root.cache = {};
  }

  if (typeof root.cache.sceneSummary !== "string") root.cache.sceneSummary = "";
  if (!Array.isArray(root.cache.localEntityIds)) root.cache.localEntityIds = [];
  if (!Array.isArray(root.cache.localActorIds)) root.cache.localActorIds = [];
  if (typeof root.cache.lastSceneHash !== "string") root.cache.lastSceneHash = "";

  if (root.player && typeof root.player === "object") {
    if (typeof root.player.sceneRef !== "string") root.player.sceneRef = "";
    if (typeof root.player.locationRef !== "string") root.player.locationRef = "";

    if (!scene.sceneRef && root.player.sceneRef) {
      scene.sceneRef = root.player.sceneRef;
    }
  }

  return scene;
},

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const text = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
    ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
    : String(rawText || "").trim();

  if (!text) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  const scene = root.world.currentScene;
  let changes = 0;
  let sceneChanged = 0;

  const sceneCandidate = this.extractSceneCandidate(text);
  if (sceneCandidate) {
    sceneChanged = this.applySceneCandidate(sceneCandidate, root);
    changes += sceneChanged;
  }

  const exits = this.extractLocalExits(text);
  if (sceneChanged || exits.length) {
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(scene.localExits, exits, 6);
  }

  const hazards = this.extractLocalHazards(text);
  if (sceneChanged || hazards.length) {
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(scene.localHazards, hazards, 6);
  }

  const conditionTags = this.extractConditionTags(text);
  if (sceneChanged || conditionTags.length) {
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(scene.conditionTags, conditionTags, 6);
  }

  const actorLabels = this.extractLocalActors(text);
  if (sceneChanged || actorLabels.length) {
    changes += this.syncLabeledRefs(scene.localActorsById, scene.localActorIds, actorLabels, "local_actor");
  }

  const entityLabels = this.extractLocalEntities(text);
  if (sceneChanged || entityLabels.length) {
    changes += this.syncLabeledRefs(scene.localEntitiesById, scene.localEntityIds, entityLabels, "local_entity");
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.refreshCache(root);

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Scene updated: " + this.getCompactSummary(root));
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  setCurrentSceneFromPhrase(phrase, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(phrase);
  if (!cleaned) return 0;

  return this.confirmSceneCandidate({
    label: cleaned,
    inferredType: this.inferSceneType(cleaned),
    strength: 3,
    sourceType: "forced_phrase"
  }, root);
},

  extractScenePhrase(text) {
  const candidate = this.extractSceneCandidate(text);
  if (!candidate || candidate.strength < 2) {
    return "";
  }

  return candidate.label;
},

pickSceneLabel(value) {
  const src = String(value || "").trim();
  if (!src) return "";

  const canonicalMatches = [
    { re: /\b(?:tavern|inn)\s+(?:common room|barroom|taproom)\b/i, label: "tavern common room" },
    { re: /\b(?:common room|barroom|taproom)\b/i, label: "common room" },
    { re: /\b(?:tavern\s+)?cellar\b/i, label: "cellar" },
    { re: /\bbarrel room\b/i, label: "cellar" },
    { re: /\b(?:smithy interior|forge room|smithy|forge|workshop)\b/i, label: "smithy" },
    { re: /\b(?:ruined\s+)?chapel\b/i, label: "chapel" },
    { re: /\b(?:chapel\s+)?nave\b/i, label: "chapel" },
    { re: /\bsanctuary\b/i, label: "chapel" },
    { re: /\bguild hall\b/i, label: "guild hall" },
    { re: /\bmain hall\b/i, label: "guild hall" },
    { re: /\bapothecary shop\b/i, label: "apothecary shop" },
    { re: /\bshop interior\b/i, label: "shop interior" },
    { re: /\bmarket lane\b/i, label: "market lane" },
    { re: /\bmarket square\b/i, label: "market square" }
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < canonicalMatches.length; i += 1) {
    if (canonicalMatches[i].re.test(src)) {
      const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(canonicalMatches[i].label);
      if (cleaned) return cleaned;
    }
  }

  const simplePatterns = [
    /\b(tavern|inn|cellar|smithy|chapel|guild hall|apothecary shop|shop interior|market lane|market square|square|lane|street|road|path|trail|courtyard|yard|bridge|gatehouse|gate|dock|pier|shore|beach|riverbank|river bank|boat|ship deck|deck|camp|forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cave|cavern|ruins|warehouse|granary|library|house|temple)\b/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < simplePatterns.length; i += 1) {
    const match = src.match(simplePatterns[i]);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(match[1]);
      if (cleaned && !this.isWeakSceneCandidate(cleaned)) {
        return cleaned;
      }
    }
  }

  const cleaned = this.cleanSceneCandidate(src);
  if (!cleaned || this.isWeakSceneCandidate(cleaned)) {
    return "";
  }

  if (!/\b(tavern|inn|common room|barroom|taproom|cellar|barrel room|smithy|forge|workshop|chapel|nave|sanctuary|guild hall|main hall|apothecary|shop interior|market lane|market square|square|lane|street|road|path|trail|courtyard|yard|bridge|gatehouse|gate|dock|pier|shore|beach|riverbank|river bank|boat|ship deck|deck|camp|forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cave|cavern|ruins|warehouse|granary|library|house|temple)\b/i.test(cleaned)) {
    return "";
  }

  return cleaned;
},

sceneFamilyKey(label) {
  const lower = this.cleanSceneCandidate(label || "").toLowerCase();
  if (!lower) return "";

  if ((/\b(tavern|inn)\b/.test(lower) && /\b(common room|barroom|taproom)\b/.test(lower)) || /\b(common room|barroom|taproom)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "tavern_common_room";
  }

  if (/\b(cellar|barrel room)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "cellar";
  }

  if (/\b(smithy|forge|workshop)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "smithy";
  }

  if (/\b(chapel|nave|sanctuary)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "chapel";
  }

  if (/\b(guild hall|main hall)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "guild_hall";
  }

  if (/\b(apothecary|shop interior)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "shop_interior";
  }

  if (/\bmarket lane\b/.test(lower) || /^lane$/.test(lower)) {
    return "market_lane";
  }

  if (/\bmarket square\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "market_square";
  }

  return "";
},

isGenericSceneLabel(label) {
  const lower = this.cleanSceneCandidate(label || "").toLowerCase();
  if (!lower) return true;

  return new Set([
    "lane",
    "room",
    "hall",
    "interior",
    "exterior",
    "outside",
    "inside",
    "place",
    "unknown",
    "unknown place"
  ]).has(lower);
},

labelsCompatible(a, b) {
  const left = this.cleanSceneCandidate(a || "").toLowerCase();
  const right = this.cleanSceneCandidate(b || "").toLowerCase();

  if (!left || !right) return false;
  if (left === right) return true;

  const leftFamily = this.sceneFamilyKey(left);
  const rightFamily = this.sceneFamilyKey(right);

  if (leftFamily && rightFamily && leftFamily === rightFamily) {
    return true;
  }

  if (left.indexOf(right) !== -1 || right.indexOf(left) !== -1) {
    if (!this.isGenericSceneLabel(left) || !this.isGenericSceneLabel(right)) {
      return true;
    }
  }

  return false;
},

preferSceneLabel(currentLabel, candidateLabel) {
  const current = this.cleanSceneCandidate(currentLabel || "");
  const candidate = this.cleanSceneCandidate(candidateLabel || "");

  if (!current) return candidate;
  if (!candidate) return current;
  if (!this.labelsCompatible(current, candidate)) return candidate;

  const family = this.sceneFamilyKey(current) || this.sceneFamilyKey(candidate);

  if (family === "tavern_common_room") {
    if (/\b(tavern|inn)\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\b(tavern|inn)\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "guild_hall") {
    if (/\bguild hall\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bguild hall\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "chapel") {
    if (/\bchapel\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bchapel\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "smithy") {
    if (/\bsmithy\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bsmithy\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "shop_interior") {
    if (/\bapothecary\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bapothecary\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (family === "market_lane") {
    if (/\bmarket lane\b/i.test(candidate)) return candidate;
    if (/\bmarket lane\b/i.test(current)) return current;
  }

  if (this.isGenericSceneLabel(candidate) && !this.isGenericSceneLabel(current)) {
    return current;
  }

  if (this.isGenericSceneLabel(current) && !this.isGenericSceneLabel(candidate)) {
    return candidate;
  }

  return candidate.split(/\s+/).length > current.split(/\s+/).length ? candidate : current;
},

extractSceneCandidate(text) {
  const raw = String(text || "");
  const candidates = [];

  const hasWeakReferenceLanguage =
    /\b(?:visible from|can see|could see|seen from|nearby|down the street|down the road|beyond|in the distance|somewhere|only consider|consider heading|consider going|think about heading|talks about|talk about|mentions|mention)\b/i.test(raw);

  const hasExplicitArrivalLanguage =
    /\byou\s+(?:enter|step into|walk into|move into|head into|cross into|make your way into|arrive in|arrive at|return to|descend into|climb into|duck into|go inside|step inside|walk inside)\b/i.test(raw) ||
    /\byou\s+(?:push through|push open|slip through)\b/i.test(raw);

  const weakReferenceOnly = hasWeakReferenceLanguage && !hasExplicitArrivalLanguage;

  const pushCandidate = (value, strength, sourceType) => {
    const picked = this.pickSceneLabel(value);
    if (!picked) return;

    candidates.push({
      label: picked,
      inferredType: this.inferSceneType(picked),
      strength: Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(strength) || 1)),
      sourceType: String(sourceType || "hint")
    });
  };

  // Phase 5 Recovery Patch 3:
  // Current-presence wording must extract only the place after "remain/stay/still in".
  // This prevents nearby/mentioned places earlier in the sentence from becoming current scene.
  const currentPresencePatterns = [
    /\byou\s+(?:remain|stay|wait|stand)\s+(?:inside|within|in|at|on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
    /\byou\s+are\s+still\s+(?:inside|within|in|at|on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
    /\bwhile\s+(?:staying|remaining)\s+(?:inside|within|in|at|on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i,
    /\bbut\s+you\s+(?:remain|stay)\s+(?:inside|within|in|at|on)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]+)/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < currentPresencePatterns.length; i += 1) {
    const match = raw.match(currentPresencePatterns[i]);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      pushCandidate(match[1], 3, "explicit_current_presence");
    }
  }

  const arrivalPatterns = [
    /\byou\s+(?:enter|step into|walk into|move into|head into|cross into|make your way into|arrive in|arrive at|return to|descend into|climb into|duck into|go inside|step inside|walk inside)\b/i,
    /\byou\s+(?:push through|push open|slip through)\b/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < arrivalPatterns.length; i += 1) {
    if (arrivalPatterns[i].test(raw)) {
      pushCandidate(raw, 3, "explicit_arrival");
    }
  }

  const mediumPatterns = [
    /\b(?:inside|within|beneath|under|along|on|at)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;]+)/i,

    // "The market lane remains busy..." and "The tavern common room remains..."
    // are current-scene confirmations. Compound room labels must be listed
    // before shorter labels so they do not downgrade to weak mention-only hints.
    /\bthe\s+(tavern common room|inn common room|tavern barroom|inn barroom|tavern taproom|inn taproom|common room|barroom|taproom|storeroom|kitchen|workshop|smithy|forge|loft|sleeping quarters|cellar|barrel room|guild hall|main hall|apothecary shop|shop interior|market lane|market square|square|lane|street|road|path|trail|courtyard|yard|bridge|gatehouse|gate|dock|pier|shore|beach|riverbank|river bank|tavern|inn|shop|temple|chapel|nave|camp|forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cave|cavern|ruins)\s+(?:is|lies|stretches|opens|remains|waits|stands|buzzes)\b/i,

    // In "the chapel is visible from the market lane", the current scene is
    // the "from" place, not the visible distant place.
    /\b(?:visible from|seen from|can be seen from)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;]+)/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < mediumPatterns.length; i += 1) {
    const match = raw.match(mediumPatterns[i]);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      pushCandidate(match[1], 2, "contextual_presence");
    }
  }

  // Do not allow weak nearby/future/distant mentions to become mention-only
  // scene candidates. They may appear in narration, but they are not arrival.
  if (
    !candidates.length &&
    !weakReferenceOnly &&
    /\b(tavern|inn|common room|cellar|smithy|forge|chapel|nave|guild hall|main hall|apothecary shop|shop interior|market lane|market square|bridge|gatehouse|dock|pier|forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cave|cavern|ruins)\b/i.test(raw)
  ) {
    pushCandidate(raw, 1, "mention_only");
  }

  if (!candidates.length) {
    return null;
  }

  candidates.sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.strength !== a.strength) return b.strength - a.strength;
    return b.label.split(/\s+/).length - a.label.split(/\s+/).length;
  });

  return candidates[0];
},

applySceneCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object" || !candidate.label) {
    return 0;
  }

  const scene = root.world.currentScene;
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
  let changes = 0;

  if (scene.sceneHintLabel !== candidate.label) {
    scene.sceneHintLabel = candidate.label;
    changes += 1;
  }
  if (scene.sceneHintStrength !== candidate.strength) {
    scene.sceneHintStrength = candidate.strength;
    changes += 1;
  }
  if (scene.sceneHintTurn !== turn) {
    scene.sceneHintTurn = turn;
    changes += 1;
  }

  const currentLabel = this.cleanSceneCandidate(scene.displayName || "");
  const currentStable = currentLabel && !this.isWeakSceneCandidate(currentLabel);
  const noConfirmedScene = !scene.sceneRef || !currentStable || Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0) < 2;
  const sameAsCurrent = currentStable && this.labelsCompatible(currentLabel, candidate.label);

  if (sameAsCurrent) {
    const preferred = this.preferSceneLabel(currentLabel, candidate.label);

    if (preferred && scene.displayName !== preferred) {
      scene.displayName = preferred;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const nextConfidence = Math.max(Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0), candidate.strength);
    if (scene.sceneConfidence !== nextConfidence) {
      scene.sceneConfidence = nextConfidence;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if ((!scene.sceneType || scene.sceneType === "unknown") && candidate.inferredType) {
      scene.sceneType = candidate.inferredType;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (scene.pendingSceneLabel || scene.pendingSceneStrength || scene.pendingSceneCount || scene.pendingSceneTurn) {
      scene.pendingSceneLabel = "";
      scene.pendingSceneStrength = 0;
      scene.pendingSceneCount = 0;
      scene.pendingSceneTurn = 0;
      changes += 1;
    }

    return changes;
  }

  if (candidate.strength >= 3 || (candidate.strength >= 2 && noConfirmedScene)) {
    return changes + this.confirmSceneCandidate(candidate, root);
  }

  if (candidate.strength < 2) {
    return changes;
  }

  if (scene.pendingSceneLabel && this.labelsCompatible(scene.pendingSceneLabel, candidate.label)) {
    const preferredPending = this.preferSceneLabel(scene.pendingSceneLabel, candidate.label);
    const nextCount = Number(scene.pendingSceneCount || 0) + 1;
    const nextStrength = Math.max(Number(scene.pendingSceneStrength || 0), candidate.strength);

    if (scene.pendingSceneLabel !== preferredPending) {
      scene.pendingSceneLabel = preferredPending;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (scene.pendingSceneCount !== nextCount) {
      scene.pendingSceneCount = nextCount;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (scene.pendingSceneStrength !== nextStrength) {
      scene.pendingSceneStrength = nextStrength;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (scene.pendingSceneTurn !== turn) {
      scene.pendingSceneTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }
  } else {
    scene.pendingSceneLabel = candidate.label;
    scene.pendingSceneStrength = candidate.strength;
    scene.pendingSceneCount = 1;
    scene.pendingSceneTurn = turn;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (scene.pendingSceneCount >= 2 && scene.pendingSceneStrength >= 2) {
    changes += this.confirmSceneCandidate({
      label: scene.pendingSceneLabel,
      inferredType: this.inferSceneType(scene.pendingSceneLabel),
      strength: scene.pendingSceneStrength,
      sourceType: "reinforced_presence"
    }, root);
  }

  return changes;
},

confirmSceneCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  let label = this.cleanSceneCandidate(candidate && candidate.label ? candidate.label : "");
  if (!label) return 0;

  const scene = root.world.currentScene;
  const currentLabel = this.cleanSceneCandidate(scene.displayName || "");
  if (currentLabel && this.labelsCompatible(currentLabel, label)) {
    label = this.preferSceneLabel(currentLabel, label);
  }

  const key = U.toKey(label);
  if (!key) return 0;

  const nextSceneRef = "scene_" + key;
  const inferredType = candidate && candidate.inferredType
    ? candidate.inferredType
    : this.inferSceneType(label);
  const strength = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(candidate && candidate.strength ? candidate.strength : 2)));
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  let changed = 0;

  if (scene.sceneRef !== nextSceneRef) {
    scene.sceneRef = nextSceneRef;
    scene.displayName = label;
    scene.sceneType = inferredType;
    scene.entityRef = "entity_" + key;
    scene.lastChangedTurn = turn;
    scene.sceneConfidence = strength;

    scene.localEntityIds = [];
    scene.localActorIds = [];
    scene.localHazards = [];
    scene.localExits = [];
    scene.conditionTags = [];
    scene.localEntitiesById = {};
    scene.localActorsById = {};

    changed = 1;

    if (root.player && typeof root.player === "object") {
      root.player.sceneRef = nextSceneRef;
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidateSceneCache === "function") {
      U.invalidateSceneCache(root);
    }
  } else {
    if (scene.displayName !== label) {
      scene.displayName = label;
      changed = 1;
    }

    if (scene.sceneType !== inferredType) {
      scene.sceneType = inferredType;
      changed = 1;
    }

    const nextConfidence = Math.max(Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0), strength);
    if (scene.sceneConfidence !== nextConfidence) {
      scene.sceneConfidence = nextConfidence;
      changed = 1;
    }
  }

  if (scene.sceneHintLabel !== label) {
    scene.sceneHintLabel = label;
    changed = 1;
  }
  if (scene.sceneHintStrength !== strength) {
    scene.sceneHintStrength = strength;
    changed = 1;
  }
  if (scene.sceneHintTurn !== turn) {
    scene.sceneHintTurn = turn;
    changed = 1;
  }

  if (scene.pendingSceneLabel || scene.pendingSceneStrength || scene.pendingSceneCount || scene.pendingSceneTurn) {
    scene.pendingSceneLabel = "";
    scene.pendingSceneStrength = 0;
    scene.pendingSceneCount = 0;
    scene.pendingSceneTurn = 0;
    changed = 1;
  }

  return changed;
},

  extractLocalExits(text) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (value) => {
      const v = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!v) return;
      if (out.indexOf(v) === -1) out.push(v);
    };

    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\bdoor\b/.test(lower)) add("door");
    if (/\bstairs?\b/.test(lower)) add("stairs");
    if (/\bgate\b/.test(lower)) add("gate");
    if (/\bhallway\b|\bcorridor\b/.test(lower)) add("hallway");
    if (/\bpath\b|\broad\b|\btrail\b/.test(lower)) add("path");
    if (/\bladder\b/.test(lower)) add("ladder");
    if (/\bwindow\b/.test(lower)) add("window");
    if (/\bbridge\b/.test(lower)) add("bridge");
    if (/\btunnel\b/.test(lower)) add("tunnel");

    if (/\bnorth\b/.test(lower)) add("north");
    if (/\bsouth\b/.test(lower)) add("south");
    if (/\beast\b/.test(lower)) add("east");
    if (/\bwest\b/.test(lower)) add("west");
    if (/\bupstairs\b|\bup\b/.test(lower)) add("up");
    if (/\bdownstairs\b|\bdown\b/.test(lower)) add("down");

    return out;
  },

  extractLocalHazards(text) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (value) => {
      const v = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!v) return;
      if (out.indexOf(v) === -1) out.push(v);
    };

    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\bfire\b|\bflames?\b|\bburning\b/.test(lower)) add("fire");
    if (/\bsmoke\b|\bsmoky\b/.test(lower)) add("smoke");
    if (/\bdebris\b|\brubble\b/.test(lower)) add("debris");
    if (/\bcollaps(?:e|ing|ed)\b|\bunstable\b/.test(lower)) add("collapse");
    if (/\bflood(?:ed|ing)?\b|\bwaterlogged\b/.test(lower)) add("flood");
    if (/\bpoison\b|\btoxic\b|\bgas\b/.test(lower)) add("poison");
    if (/\btrap\b|\btrapped\b/.test(lower)) add("trap");
    if (/\bblood\b|\bbloody\b/.test(lower)) add("blood");
    if (/\bcorpse\b|\bbody\b|\bodies\b/.test(lower)) add("corpses");
    if (/\bdark\b|\bpitch-black\b/.test(lower)) add("darkness");

    return out;
  },

  extractConditionTags(text) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (value) => {
      const v = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!v) return;
      if (out.indexOf(v) === -1) out.push(v);
    };

    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\bdamaged\b|\bbroken\b|\bcracked\b|\bburned\b|\bcharred\b/.test(lower)) add("damaged");
