    if (/\bcrowded\b|\bpacked\b|\bfull of people\b/.test(lower)) add("crowded");
    if (/\babandoned\b|\bempty\b|\bdeserted\b/.test(lower)) add("empty");
    if (/\bwet\b|\bdamp\b/.test(lower)) add("wet");
    if (/\bsmoky\b|\bhazy\b/.test(lower)) add("obscured");
    if (/\bbloody\b/.test(lower)) add("violent_aftermath");
    if (/\bcold\b|\bfreezing\b/.test(lower)) add("cold");
    if (/\bhot\b|\bsweltering\b/.test(lower)) add("hot");

    return out;
  },

  extractLocalActors(text) {
    const out = [];
    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    const actorTerms = [
      "guard",
      "merchant",
      "innkeeper",
      "bartender",
      "barkeep",
      "adventurer",
      "soldier",
      "bandit",
      "goblin",
      "wolf",
      "patron",
      "priest",
      "noble",
      "traveler",
      "mage",
      "knight",
      "child",
      "woman",
      "man",
      "beast"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < actorTerms.length; i += 1) {
      const term = actorTerms[i];
      const re = new RegExp("\\b(?:a|an|the|some)\\s+" + term + "s?\\b", "i");
      if (re.test(lower) && out.indexOf(term) === -1) {
        out.push(term);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractLocalEntities(text) {
    const out = [];
    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    const entityTerms = [
      "door",
      "table",
      "bar",
      "counter",
      "chest",
      "altar",
      "bed",
      "window",
      "stairs",
      "ladder",
      "gate",
      "bookshelf",
      "shelf",
      "crate",
      "wagon",
      "cart",
      "fireplace"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < entityTerms.length; i += 1) {
      const term = entityTerms[i];
      const re = new RegExp("\\b(?:a|an|the|some)\\s+" + term + "s?\\b", "i");
      if (re.test(lower) && out.indexOf(term) === -1) {
        out.push(term);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  syncLabeledRefs(mapObj, idArray, labels, prefix) {
    if (!mapObj || typeof mapObj !== "object" || Array.isArray(mapObj)) return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(idArray)) return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(labels)) labels = [];

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const nextMap = {};
    const nextIds = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length && i < 8; i += 1) {
      const label = this.cleanPhrase(labels[i]);
      if (!label) continue;

      const key = U.toKey(label);
      if (!key) continue;

      const ref = String(prefix || "local") + "_" + key;
      nextMap[ref] = label;
      nextIds.push(ref);
    }

    let changed = 0;

    if (idArray.length !== nextIds.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < idArray.length; i += 1) {
        if (idArray[i] !== nextIds[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) {
      const oldKeys = Object.keys(mapObj);
      const newKeys = Object.keys(nextMap);
      if (oldKeys.length !== newKeys.length) {
        changed = 1;
      } else {
        for (let i = 0; i < newKeys.length; i += 1) {
          const key = newKeys[i];
          if (mapObj[key] !== nextMap[key]) {
            changed = 1;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    idArray.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nextIds.length; i += 1) {
      idArray.push(nextIds[i]);
    }

    for (const key in mapObj) {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(mapObj, key)) {
        delete mapObj[key];
      }
    }

    for (const key in nextMap) {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(nextMap, key)) {
        mapObj[key] = nextMap[key];
      }
    }

    return 1;
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(targetArray, nextValues, maxCount) {
    if (!Array.isArray(targetArray)) return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(nextValues)) nextValues = [];

    const cleaned = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < nextValues.length && i < (maxCount || 8); i += 1) {
      const value = String(nextValues[i] || "").trim();
      if (!value) continue;
      if (cleaned.indexOf(value) === -1) cleaned.push(value);
    }

    let changed = 0;
    if (targetArray.length !== cleaned.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < targetArray.length; i += 1) {
        if (targetArray[i] !== cleaned[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    targetArray.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 1) {
      targetArray.push(cleaned[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  inferSceneType(label) {
  const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();

  if (/\b(common room|barroom|taproom|storeroom|kitchen|loft|sleeping quarters|cellar|room|hall|chamber)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "room";
  }

  if (/\b(tavern|inn|shop|temple|guildhall|house|building|library|warehouse|granary|smithy|forge|workshop|bar)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "interior";
  }

  if (/\b(market square|square|lane|street|road|path|trail|forest|field|bridge|gate|gatehouse|courtyard|yard|camp|dock|pier|shore|beach|riverbank|river bank|boat|ship deck|deck)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "outdoor";
  }

  return "scene";
},

  cleanSceneCandidate(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/\s*,\s*(?:catching|taking|trying|struggling|still|while|as)\b.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+(?:where|when|while|and then|but then)\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .trim();
},

isWeakSceneCandidate(value) {
  const lower = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!lower) return true;
  if (lower.length > 48) return true;
  if (/\b(catching your breath|looking around|trying to|beginning to|continuing to|still)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
  if (/^(there|here|somewhere|nearby|around you)$/.test(lower)) return true;
  return false;
},

cleanPhrase(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+(?:where|when|while|and|but)\s+.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .trim();
},

  refreshCache(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";

    const scene = this.init(root);

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    root.cache.localEntityIds = scene.localEntityIds.slice();
    root.cache.localActorIds = scene.localActorIds.slice();

    const summary = this.getCompactSummary(root);
    root.cache.sceneSummary = summary;
    root.cache.scenePacket = summary;

    if (globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine.stableHash === "function") {
      root.cache.lastSceneHash = globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine.stableHash(summary);
    }

    return summary;
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !root.world || !root.world.currentScene) {
      return "Scene unknown.";
    }

    const scene = root.world.currentScene;
    const parts = [];

    if (scene.displayName) {
      parts.push("Scene: " + scene.displayName);
    } else {
      parts.push("Scene: unknown");
    }

    if (scene.sceneType) {
      parts.push("Type: " + scene.sceneType);
    }

    if (Array.isArray(scene.conditionTags) && scene.conditionTags.length) {
      parts.push("State: " + scene.conditionTags.join(", "));
    }

    if (Array.isArray(scene.localHazards) && scene.localHazards.length) {
      parts.push("Hazards: " + scene.localHazards.join(", "));
    }

    if (Array.isArray(scene.localExits) && scene.localExits.length) {
      parts.push("Exits: " + scene.localExits.join(", "));
    }

    if (scene.localActorIds && scene.localActorIds.length && scene.localActorsById) {
      const names = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < scene.localActorIds.length; i += 1) {
        const ref = scene.localActorIds[i];
        if (scene.localActorsById[ref]) {
          names.push(scene.localActorsById[ref]);
        }
      }
      if (names.length) {
        parts.push("Actors: " + names.join(", "));
      }
    }

    if (scene.localEntityIds && scene.localEntityIds.length && scene.localEntitiesById) {
      const names = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < scene.localEntityIds.length; i += 1) {
        const ref = scene.localEntityIds[i];
        if (scene.localEntitiesById[ref]) {
          names.push(scene.localEntitiesById[ref]);
        }
      }
      if (names.length) {
        parts.push("Objects: " + names.join(", "));
      }
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === WORLD ENTITY SYSTEM ===
WorldEntitySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.world || typeof root.world !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.world)) {
      root.world = {};
    }

    const world = root.world;

    if (!world.locationsById || typeof world.locationsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.locationsById)) {
      world.locationsById = {};
    }

    if (!world.entitiesById || typeof world.entitiesById !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.entitiesById)) {
      world.entitiesById = {};
    }

    if (!world.factionsById || typeof world.factionsById !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.factionsById)) {
      world.factionsById = {};
    }

    if (!world.routesById || typeof world.routesById !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.routesById)) {
      world.routesById = {};
    }

    if (!world.currentScene || typeof world.currentScene !== "object" || Array.isArray(world.currentScene)) {
      world.currentScene = {};
    }

    return world;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const text = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
    ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
    : String(rawText || "").trim();

  if (!text) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  let changes = 0;

  changes += this.syncCurrentSceneToWorld(root);

  const locationPhrase = this.extractLocationPhrase(text);
  if (locationPhrase) {
    changes += this.applyLocationPhrase(locationPhrase, root);
  }

  const ownerHints = this.extractOwnerHints(text);
  const materialHints = this.extractMaterialHints(text);
  const districtHints = this.extractDistrictHints(text);
  const factionHints = this.extractFactionHints(text);

  changes += this.applyHintsToCurrentScene(ownerHints, materialHints, districtHints, factionHints, root);
  changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

  if (changes > 0) {
    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("World entity updates: " + changes);
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
  const world = root.world || {};
  const scene = world.currentScene || {};
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  let changes = 0;
  const dirtyWorldIds = [];
  const dirtyRouteIds = [];

  const markWorldDirty = (id) => {
    const clean = String(id || "").trim();
    if (!clean) return;
    if (dirtyWorldIds.indexOf(clean) === -1) dirtyWorldIds.push(clean);
  };

  const markRouteDirty = (id) => {
    const clean = String(id || "").trim();
    if (!clean) return;
    if (dirtyRouteIds.indexOf(clean) === -1) dirtyRouteIds.push(clean);
  };

  const ensureSceneShape = () => {
    if (!Array.isArray(scene.localHazards)) scene.localHazards = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(scene.localEntityIds)) scene.localEntityIds = [];
    if (!scene.localEntitiesById || typeof scene.localEntitiesById !== "object" || Array.isArray(scene.localEntitiesById)) {
      scene.localEntitiesById = {};
    }
  };

  const pushSceneHazard = (tag) => {
    ensureSceneShape();

    const clean = this.cleanPhrase(tag);
    if (!clean) return 0;
    if (scene.localHazards.indexOf(clean) !== -1) return 0;

    scene.localHazards.push(clean);
    return 1;
  };

  const pushSceneLocalEntity = (label) => {
    ensureSceneShape();

    const clean = this.cleanPhrase(label);
    if (!clean) return 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < scene.localEntityIds.length; i += 1) {
      const ref = scene.localEntityIds[i];
      if (scene.localEntitiesById[ref] === clean) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const ref = "local_entity_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(clean);
    scene.localEntitiesById[ref] = clean;
    scene.localEntityIds.push(ref);
    return 1;
  };

  const currentLocationRef = typeof scene.parentLocationRef === "string" && scene.parentLocationRef
    ? scene.parentLocationRef
    : (root.player && typeof root.player.locationRef === "string" ? root.player.locationRef : "");

  const currentLocationLabel = this.cleanPhrase(scene.displayName || currentLocationRef || "");

  const ensureCurrentLocation = () => {
    if (!currentLocationRef) return null;
    const record = this.ensureLocationRecord(currentLocationRef, currentLocationLabel || currentLocationRef, root);
    markWorldDirty(record.identity && record.identity.id ? record.identity.id : currentLocationRef);
    return record;
  };

  const ensureCurrentEntity = () => {
    if (!scene.entityRef && !scene.displayName) return null;

    const record = this.ensureEntityRecord(
      scene.entityRef || ("entity_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(scene.displayName || "scene")),
      scene.displayName || "scene",
      scene.sceneType || "scene",
      root
    );

    if (record && record.identity && record.identity.id) {
      markWorldDirty(record.identity.id);
    }

    return record;
  };

  const ensureTargetEntity = (label) => {
    const clean = this.cleanPhrase(label);
    if (!clean) return null;

    const currentEntity = ensureCurrentEntity();
    const currentName = currentEntity && currentEntity.identity
      ? this.cleanPhrase(currentEntity.identity.displayName || "")
      : "";

    if (currentEntity && currentName && (
      clean === currentName ||
      currentName.indexOf(clean) !== -1 ||
      clean.indexOf(currentName) !== -1
    )) {
      return currentEntity;
    }

    const record = this.ensureEntityRecord(
      "entity_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(clean),
      clean,
      "world_object",
      root
    );

    if (scene.sceneRef && record.links.sceneRef !== scene.sceneRef) {
      record.links.sceneRef = scene.sceneRef;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (currentLocationRef && record.links.parentLocationRef !== currentLocationRef) {
      record.links.parentLocationRef = currentLocationRef;
      changes += 1;

      const locationRecord = ensureCurrentLocation();
      if (locationRecord && this.pushUnique(locationRecord.mutable.linkedEntityIds, record.identity.id)) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    if (record && record.identity && record.identity.id) {
      markWorldDirty(record.identity.id);
    }

    return record;
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "world" || candidate._worldApplied === true) {
      continue;
    }

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};

    if (candidate.type === "world_change") {
      const targetLabel = this.cleanPhrase(payload.targetLabel || "");
      const changeTag = this.cleanPhrase(payload.changeTag || "general_damage") || "general_damage";

      const targetRecord = ensureTargetEntity(targetLabel || scene.displayName || "scene");
      if (targetRecord && targetRecord.mutable) {
        if (this.pushUnique(targetRecord.mutable.conditionTags, changeTag)) {
          changes += 1;
        }

        if (/(damage|burn|broken|collapse|blocked|crater)/i.test(changeTag)) {
          if (this.pushUnique(targetRecord.mutable.hazardTags, changeTag)) {
            changes += 1;
          }
        }

        if (targetRecord.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
          targetRecord.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
          changes += 1;
        }
      }

      const currentSceneName = this.cleanPhrase(scene.displayName || "");
      if (!targetLabel || (currentSceneName && targetLabel === currentSceneName)) {
        const locationRecord = ensureCurrentLocation();
        if (locationRecord && this.pushUnique(locationRecord.mutable.conditionTags, changeTag)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }

      if (targetLabel) {
        changes += pushSceneLocalEntity(targetLabel);
      }

      changes += pushSceneHazard(changeTag);
    } else if (candidate.type === "route_issue") {
      const issue = this.cleanPhrase(payload.issue || "blocked") || "blocked";
      const issueTag = "route_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(issue);

      const locationRecord = ensureCurrentLocation();
      if (locationRecord) {
        if (this.pushUnique(locationRecord.mutable.conditionTags, issueTag)) {
          changes += 1;
        }

        if (locationRecord.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
          locationRecord.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
          changes += 1;
        }
      }

      const currentEntity = ensureCurrentEntity();
      const currentName = currentEntity && currentEntity.identity
        ? String(currentEntity.identity.displayName || scene.displayName || "")
        : String(scene.displayName || "");

      if (currentEntity && /\b(road|trail|path|bridge|gate|gatehouse|dock|pier)\b/i.test(currentName)) {
        if (this.pushUnique(currentEntity.mutable.hazardTags, issueTag)) {
          changes += 1;
        }

        if (currentEntity.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
          currentEntity.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
          changes += 1;
        }
      }

      const routeIds = Object.keys(world.routesById || {});
      for (let r = 0; r < routeIds.length; r += 1) {
        const routeId = routeIds[r];
        const route = world.routesById[routeId];
        if (!route || !route.mutable) continue;

        if (route.mutable.fromRef === currentLocationRef || route.mutable.toRef === currentLocationRef) {
          if (!Array.isArray(route.mutable.hazardTags)) {
            route.mutable.hazardTags = [];
          }

          if (this.pushUnique(route.mutable.hazardTags, issueTag)) {
            changes += 1;
          }

          if (route.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
            route.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
            changes += 1;
          }

          markRouteDirty(routeId);
        }
      }

      changes += pushSceneHazard(issueTag);
    }

    candidate._worldApplied = true;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      for (let i = 0; i < dirtyWorldIds.length; i += 1) {
        U.markDirty("entities", dirtyWorldIds[i], root);
      }

      for (let i = 0; i < dirtyRouteIds.length; i += 1) {
        U.markDirty("routes", dirtyRouteIds[i], root);
      }
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Validated world consequences applied.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  syncCurrentSceneToWorld(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const scene = root.world.currentScene || {};
  const player = root.player || {};
  const world = root.world || {};
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
  const sceneConfidence = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0)));

  const inferBroadLocationLabel = (label) => {
    const lower = String(label || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!lower) return "";

    const broadTerms = [
      "forest",
      "woods",
      "swamp",
      "marsh",
      "jungle",
      "cave",
      "cavern",
      "ruins",
      "coast",
      "shore",
      "mountains",
      "hills",
      "valley",
      "plains",
      "meadow",
      "river",
      "stream",
      "creek",
      "lake",
      "pond",
      "road",
      "path",
      "trail",
      "camp"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < broadTerms.length; i += 1) {
      if (lower === broadTerms[i]) {
        return broadTerms[i];
      }
    }

    return "";
  };

  let changes = 0;
  let locationRecord = null;

  const playerLocationRef = typeof player.locationRef === "string" ? player.locationRef : "";
  const sceneParentRef = typeof scene.parentLocationRef === "string" ? scene.parentLocationRef : "";

  const invalidSceneStyleRef = (value) => {
    return !value || value.indexOf("scene_") === 0;
  };

  const broadSceneLabel = sceneConfidence >= 3
    ? inferBroadLocationLabel(scene.displayName || "")
    : "";

  if (broadSceneLabel && invalidSceneStyleRef(sceneParentRef)) {
    const promotedLocationRef = "location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(broadSceneLabel);
    scene.parentLocationRef = promotedLocationRef;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (scene.parentLocationRef) {
    const existingLocation = world.locationsById && world.locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef]
      ? world.locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef]
      : null;

    const displayName = existingLocation && existingLocation.identity
      ? existingLocation.identity.displayName
      : (broadSceneLabel || scene.parentLocationRef);

    locationRecord = this.ensureLocationRecord(scene.parentLocationRef, displayName, root);
  }

  if (!locationRecord && broadSceneLabel) {
    const fallbackLocationRef = "location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(broadSceneLabel);
    locationRecord = this.ensureLocationRecord(fallbackLocationRef, broadSceneLabel, root);

    if (scene.parentLocationRef !== locationRecord.identity.id) {
      scene.parentLocationRef = locationRecord.identity.id;
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  if (locationRecord && player.locationRef !== locationRecord.identity.id) {
    player.locationRef = locationRecord.identity.id;
    changes += 1;
  } else if (!locationRecord && invalidSceneStyleRef(playerLocationRef) && broadSceneLabel) {
    player.locationRef = "location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(broadSceneLabel);
    changes += 1;
  }

  const entityRef = scene.entityRef || scene.sceneRef;
  const entityName = scene.displayName || scene.sceneRef || "";

  if (entityRef || entityName) {
    const entityRecord = this.ensureEntityRecord(
      entityRef || ("entity_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(entityName || "scene")),
      entityName || "scene",
      scene.sceneType || "scene",
      root
    );

    if (entityRecord.links.sceneRef !== (scene.sceneRef || "")) {
      entityRecord.links.sceneRef = scene.sceneRef || "";
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (locationRecord && entityRecord.links.parentLocationRef !== locationRecord.identity.id) {
      entityRecord.links.parentLocationRef = locationRecord.identity.id;
      changes += 1;
    }

    entityRecord.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(entityRecord.mutable.conditionTags, scene.conditionTags || [], 8);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(entityRecord.mutable.hazardTags, scene.localHazards || [], 8);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(entityRecord.mutable.exitHints, scene.localExits || [], 8);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(
      entityRecord.mutable.actorHints,
      this.labelsFromRefMap(scene.localActorIds, scene.localActorsById),
      8
    );
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(
      entityRecord.mutable.objectHints,
      this.labelsFromRefMap(scene.localEntityIds, scene.localEntitiesById),
      8
    );

    if (locationRecord) {
      if (this.pushUnique(locationRecord.mutable.linkedEntityIds, entityRecord.identity.id)) {
        changes += 1;
      }
      locationRecord.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  ensureLocationRecord(locationRef, displayName, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const world = root.world;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    const id = String(locationRef || "").trim() || ("location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(displayName || "location"));

    if (!world.locationsById[id] || typeof world.locationsById[id] !== "object") {
      world.locationsById[id] = {
        identity: {
          id: id,
          displayName: this.cleanPhrase(displayName || id),
          kind: "location",
          createdTurn: turn
        },
        mutable: {
          districtHints: [],
          atmosphereTags: [],
          linkedEntityIds: [],
          linkedFactionIds: [],
          ownerHints: [],
          materialHints: [],
          conditionTags: [],
          lastConfirmedTurn: turn
        }
      };
    }

    const record = world.locationsById[id];

    if (!record.identity || typeof record.identity !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.identity)) {
      record.identity = {};
    }
    if (!record.mutable || typeof record.mutable !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.mutable)) {
      record.mutable = {};
    }

    if (typeof record.identity.id !== "string") record.identity.id = id;
    if (typeof record.identity.displayName !== "string" || !record.identity.displayName) {
      record.identity.displayName = this.cleanPhrase(displayName || id);
    }
    if (typeof record.identity.kind !== "string") record.identity.kind = "location";
    if (typeof record.identity.createdTurn !== "number") record.identity.createdTurn = turn;

    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.districtHints)) record.mutable.districtHints = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.atmosphereTags)) record.mutable.atmosphereTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.linkedEntityIds)) record.mutable.linkedEntityIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.linkedFactionIds)) record.mutable.linkedFactionIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.ownerHints)) record.mutable.ownerHints = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.materialHints)) record.mutable.materialHints = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.conditionTags)) record.mutable.conditionTags = [];
    if (typeof record.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn !== "number") record.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;

    return record;
  },

  ensureEntityRecord(entityRef, displayName, entityType, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const world = root.world;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    const id = String(entityRef || "").trim() || ("entity_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(displayName || "entity"));

    if (!world.entitiesById[id] || typeof world.entitiesById[id] !== "object") {
      world.entitiesById[id] = {
        identity: {
          id: id,
          displayName: this.cleanPhrase(displayName || id),
          kind: this.cleanPhrase(entityType || "entity") || "entity",
          createdTurn: turn
        },
        mutable: {
          conditionTags: [],
          hazardTags: [],
          exitHints: [],
          ownerHints: [],
          materialHints: [],
          districtHints: [],
          actorHints: [],
          objectHints: [],
          lastConfirmedTurn: turn
        },
        links: {
          sceneRef: "",
          parentLocationRef: "",
          factionIds: []
        }
      };
    }

    const record = world.entitiesById[id];

    if (!record.identity || typeof record.identity !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.identity)) {
      record.identity = {};
    }
    if (!record.mutable || typeof record.mutable !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.mutable)) {
      record.mutable = {};
    }
    if (!record.links || typeof record.links !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.links)) {
      record.links = {};
    }

    if (typeof record.identity.id !== "string") record.identity.id = id;
    if (typeof record.identity.displayName !== "string" || !record.identity.displayName) {
      record.identity.displayName = this.cleanPhrase(displayName || id);
    }
    if (typeof record.identity.kind !== "string" || !record.identity.kind) {
      record.identity.kind = this.cleanPhrase(entityType || "entity") || "entity";
    }
    if (typeof record.identity.createdTurn !== "number") record.identity.createdTurn = turn;

    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.conditionTags)) record.mutable.conditionTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.hazardTags)) record.mutable.hazardTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.exitHints)) record.mutable.exitHints = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.ownerHints)) record.mutable.ownerHints = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.materialHints)) record.mutable.materialHints = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.districtHints)) record.mutable.districtHints = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.actorHints)) record.mutable.actorHints = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.objectHints)) record.mutable.objectHints = [];
    if (typeof record.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn !== "number") record.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;

    if (typeof record.links.sceneRef !== "string") record.links.sceneRef = "";
    if (typeof record.links.parentLocationRef !== "string") record.links.parentLocationRef = "";
    if (!Array.isArray(record.links.factionIds)) record.links.factionIds = [];

    return record;
  },

  ensureFactionRecord(factionName, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const world = root.world;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    const clean = this.cleanPhrase(factionName);
    if (!clean) return null;

    const id = "faction_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(clean);

    if (!world.factionsById[id] || typeof world.factionsById[id] !== "object") {
      world.factionsById[id] = {
        identity: {
          id: id,
          displayName: clean,
          kind: "faction",
          createdTurn: turn
        },
        mutable: {
          influenceTags: [],
          locationRefs: [],
          entityRefs: [],
          lastConfirmedTurn: turn
        }
      };
    }

    const record = world.factionsById[id];

    if (!record.identity || typeof record.identity !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.identity)) {
      record.identity = {};
    }
    if (!record.mutable || typeof record.mutable !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.mutable)) {
      record.mutable = {};
    }

    if (typeof record.identity.id !== "string") record.identity.id = id;
    if (typeof record.identity.displayName !== "string" || !record.identity.displayName) {
      record.identity.displayName = clean;
    }
    if (typeof record.identity.kind !== "string") record.identity.kind = "faction";
    if (typeof record.identity.createdTurn !== "number") record.identity.createdTurn = turn;

    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.influenceTags)) record.mutable.influenceTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.locationRefs)) record.mutable.locationRefs = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.entityRefs)) record.mutable.entityRefs = [];
    if (typeof record.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn !== "number") record.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;

    return record;
  },

  ensureRouteRecord(routeRef, fromRef, toRef, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const world = root.world;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    const id = String(routeRef || "").trim();

    if (!id) return null;

    if (!world.routesById[id] || typeof world.routesById[id] !== "object") {
      world.routesById[id] = {
        identity: {
          id: id,
          kind: "route",
          createdTurn: turn
        },
        mutable: {
          fromRef: String(fromRef || ""),
          toRef: String(toRef || ""),
          travelHints: [],
          hazardTags: [],
          lastConfirmedTurn: turn
        }
      };
    }

    return world.routesById[id];
  },

  applyLocationPhrase(locationPhrase, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const clean = this.cleanPhrase(locationPhrase);
  if (!clean) return 0;

  const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : {};
  const player = root.player || {};
  const sceneConfidence = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(scene.sceneConfidence || 0)));

  const broadGeneric = new Set([
    "forest", "woods", "swamp", "marsh", "jungle", "cave", "cavern", "ruins",
    "coast", "shore", "mountains", "hills", "valley", "plains", "meadow",
    "river", "stream", "creek", "lake", "pond", "road", "path", "trail", "camp"
  ]);

  if (broadGeneric.has(clean.toLowerCase()) && sceneConfidence < 3) {
    return 0;
  }

  const locationRef = "location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(clean);
  const locationRecord = this.ensureLocationRecord(locationRef, clean, root);

  let changes = 0;

  if (scene.parentLocationRef !== locationRecord.identity.id) {
    scene.parentLocationRef = locationRecord.identity.id;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (player.locationRef !== locationRecord.identity.id) {
    player.locationRef = locationRecord.identity.id;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (scene.entityRef) {
    const entityRecord = this.ensureEntityRecord(
      scene.entityRef,
      scene.displayName || scene.entityRef,
      scene.sceneType || "scene",
      root
    );

    if (entityRecord.links.parentLocationRef !== locationRecord.identity.id) {
      entityRecord.links.parentLocationRef = locationRecord.identity.id;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (this.pushUnique(locationRecord.mutable.linkedEntityIds, entityRecord.identity.id)) {
      changes += 1;
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  applyHintsToCurrentScene(ownerHints, materialHints, districtHints, factionHints, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const scene = root.world.currentScene || {};
  let changes = 0;
  let entityRecord = null;
  let locationRecord = null;

  if (scene.entityRef || scene.displayName) {
    entityRecord = this.ensureEntityRecord(
      scene.entityRef || ("entity_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(scene.displayName || "scene")),
      scene.displayName || "scene",
      scene.sceneType || "scene",
      root
    );
  }

  if (scene.parentLocationRef) {
    locationRecord = this.ensureLocationRecord(scene.parentLocationRef, scene.parentLocationRef, root);
  }

  if (entityRecord) {
    if (ownerHints && ownerHints.length) {
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(entityRecord.mutable.ownerHints, ownerHints, 4);
    }
    if (materialHints && materialHints.length) {
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(entityRecord.mutable.materialHints, materialHints, 6);
    }
    if (districtHints && districtHints.length) {
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(entityRecord.mutable.districtHints, districtHints, 4);
    }
  }

  if (locationRecord) {
    if (districtHints && districtHints.length) {
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(locationRecord.mutable.districtHints, districtHints, 4);
    }
    if (ownerHints && ownerHints.length) {
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(locationRecord.mutable.ownerHints, ownerHints, 4);
    }
    if (materialHints && materialHints.length) {
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(locationRecord.mutable.materialHints, materialHints, 6);
    }
  }

  if (factionHints && factionHints.length) {
    for (let i = 0; i < factionHints.length; i += 1) {
      const faction = this.ensureFactionRecord(factionHints[i], root);
      if (!faction) continue;

      if (entityRecord) {
        if (this.pushUnique(entityRecord.links.factionIds, faction.identity.id)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
        if (this.pushUnique(faction.mutable.entityRefs, entityRecord.identity.id)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }

      if (locationRecord) {
        if (this.pushUnique(locationRecord.mutable.linkedFactionIds, faction.identity.id)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
        if (this.pushUnique(faction.mutable.locationRefs, locationRecord.identity.id)) {
          changes += 1;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  extractLocationPhrase(text) {
  const raw = String(text || "");
  const patterns = [
    /\b(?:town|village|city|district|region|forest|swamp|marsh|ruins|cavern|cave|kingdom)\s+of\s+([^.!?;,]+)/i,
    /\bin\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,40})(?:[.,;!?]|$)/,
    /\bat\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,40})(?:[.,;!?]|$)/,
    /\b(?:in|within|inside|through|across|along|near|beside|by)\s+(?:the\s+)?(forest|woods|swamp|marsh|jungle|cavern|cave|ruins|riverlands|coast|shore|mountains|hills|valley|plains|meadow)\b/i
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
    const match = raw.match(patterns[i]);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const cleaned = this.cleanPhrase(match[1]);
      if (cleaned) return cleaned;
    }
  }

  return "";
},

  extractOwnerHints(text) {
    const out = [];
    const patterns = [
      /\bowned by\s+([^.!?;,]+)/gi,
      /\brun by\s+([^.!?;,]+)/gi,
      /\bkept by\s+([^.!?;,]+)/gi,
      /\bmaintained by\s+([^.!?;,]+)/gi
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = patterns[i].exec(String(text || "")))) {
        const clean = this.cleanPhrase(match[1]);
        if (clean && out.indexOf(clean) === -1) {
          out.push(clean);
        }
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractMaterialHints(text) {
    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    const known = ["stone", "wood", "wooden", "brick", "iron", "steel", "cloth", "leather", "crystal", "bone"];
    const out = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < known.length; i += 1) {
      if (lower.indexOf(known[i]) !== -1 && out.indexOf(known[i]) === -1) {
        out.push(known[i]);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractDistrictHints(text) {
    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    if (/\bnorth side\b|\bnorthern district\b/.test(lower)) out.push("north");
    if (/\bsouth side\b|\bsouthern district\b/.test(lower)) out.push("south");
    if (/\beast side\b|\beastern district\b/.test(lower)) out.push("east");
    if (/\bwest side\b|\bwestern district\b/.test(lower)) out.push("west");
    if (/\bmarket district\b/.test(lower)) out.push("market");
    if (/\bouter district\b/.test(lower)) out.push("outer");
    if (/\binner district\b/.test(lower)) out.push("inner");

    return out;
  },

  extractFactionHints(text) {
    const out = [];
    const pattern = /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,40}\s(?:Guild|Company|Consortium|Clans|Circle|Hand|Synod|Dominion|Ascendancy|Order|Council))\b/g;
    let match;

    while ((match = pattern.exec(String(text || "")))) {
      const clean = this.cleanPhrase(match[1]);
      if (clean && out.indexOf(clean) === -1) {
        out.push(clean);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  labelsFromRefMap(idArray, mapObj) {
    const out = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(idArray) || !mapObj || typeof mapObj !== "object") return out;

    for (let i = 0; i < idArray.length; i += 1) {
      const ref = idArray[i];
      if (mapObj[ref]) {
        const clean = this.cleanPhrase(mapObj[ref]);
        if (clean && out.indexOf(clean) === -1) {
          out.push(clean);
        }
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(targetArray, nextValues, maxCount) {
    if (!Array.isArray(targetArray)) return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(nextValues)) nextValues = [];

    const cleaned = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < nextValues.length && i < (maxCount || 8); i += 1) {
      const value = this.cleanPhrase(nextValues[i]);
      if (!value) continue;
      if (cleaned.indexOf(value) === -1) cleaned.push(value);
    }

    let changed = 0;
    if (targetArray.length !== cleaned.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < targetArray.length; i += 1) {
        if (targetArray[i] !== cleaned[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    targetArray.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 1) {
      targetArray.push(cleaned[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushUnique(arrayRef, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(arrayRef)) return false;
    const clean = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!clean) return false;
    if (arrayRef.indexOf(clean) !== -1) return false;
    arrayRef.push(clean);
    return true;
  },

  makeKeyFromLabel(label) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.toKey === "function") {
      return U.toKey(label);
    }

    return String(label || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
      .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
  },

  cleanPhrase(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+(?:where|when|while|and|but)\s+.*$/i, "")
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .trim();
  }
},

    // === TIME / TRAVEL SYSTEM ===
TimeTravelSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.time || typeof root.time !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.time)) {
      root.time = {};
    }

    const time = root.time;

    if (!time.current || typeof time.current !== "object" || Array.isArray(time.current)) {
      time.current = {};
    }
    if (typeof time.current.day !== "number" || time.current.day < 1) time.current.day = 1;
    if (typeof time.current.hour !== "number" || time.current.hour < 0) time.current.hour = 8;
    if (typeof time.current.minute !== "number" || time.current.minute < 0) time.current.minute = 0;
    if (typeof time.current.season !== "string") time.current.season = "";
    if (typeof time.current.weather !== "string") time.current.weather = "";

    if (!time.elapsed || typeof time.elapsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(time.elapsed)) {
      time.elapsed = {};
    }
    if (typeof time.elapsed.totalHours !== "number" || time.elapsed.totalHours < 0) {
      time.elapsed.totalHours = 0;
    }

    if (!time.timeline || typeof time.timeline !== "object" || Array.isArray(time.timeline)) {
      time.timeline = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(time.timeline.recentEvents)) {
      time.timeline.recentEvents = [];
    }

    if (!time.routes || typeof time.routes !== "object" || Array.isArray(time.routes)) {
      time.routes = {};
    }
    if (!time.routes.knownTravelTimes || typeof time.routes.knownTravelTimes !== "object" || Array.isArray(time.routes.knownTravelTimes)) {
      time.routes.knownTravelTimes = {};
    }

    if (!time.pendingAdvance || typeof time.pendingAdvance !== "object" || Array.isArray(time.pendingAdvance)) {
      time.pendingAdvance = null;
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(time.contradictionFlags)) {
      time.contradictionFlags = [];
    }

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }
    if (typeof root.cache.timeSummary !== "string") {
      root.cache.timeSummary = "";
    }

    return time;
  },

  processInput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    this.init(root);

    const intent = root.pending && root.pending.inputIntent ? root.pending.inputIntent : null;
    if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") {
      root.time.pendingAdvance = null;
      return null;
    }

    const proposal = this.buildPendingAdvance(intent, rawText, root);
    root.time.pendingAdvance = proposal;
    return proposal;
  },

  buildPendingAdvance(intent, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const type = String(intent.primaryType || "");
    const text = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const currentLocationRef = root.player && typeof root.player.locationRef === "string"
      ? root.player.locationRef
      : "";

    const proposal = {
      kind: "general",
      minutes: 5,
      destinationHint: String(intent.destinationHint || "").trim(),
      startLocationRef: currentLocationRef,
      createdTurn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      note: type || "general"
    };

    if (type === "travel") {
      proposal.kind = "travel";
      proposal.minutes = this.estimateTravelMinutes(intent, text, root);
      proposal.note = "travel";
      return proposal;
    }

    if (type === "move") {
      proposal.kind = "movement";
      proposal.minutes = this.estimateMoveMinutes(text);
      proposal.note = "movement";
      return proposal;
    }

    if (type === "rest") {
      proposal.kind = "rest";
      proposal.minutes = this.estimateRestMinutes(text);
      proposal.note = "rest";
      return proposal;
    }

    if (type === "train") {
      proposal.kind = "training";
      proposal.minutes = this.estimateTrainingMinutes(text);
      proposal.note = "training";
      return proposal;
    }

    if (type === "inspect") {
      proposal.kind = "inspection";
      proposal.minutes = 5;
      proposal.note = "inspection";
      return proposal;
    }

    if (type === "talk" || type === "barter") {
      proposal.kind = "social";
      proposal.minutes = 8;
      proposal.note = "social";
      return proposal;
    }

    if (type === "loot" || type === "equip" || type === "unequip" || type === "consume_item" || type === "interact") {
      proposal.kind = "interaction";
      proposal.minutes = 4;
      proposal.note = "interaction";
      return proposal;
    }

    if (type === "attack" || type === "defend" || type === "use_ability") {
      proposal.kind = "combat";
      proposal.minutes = 6;
      proposal.note = "combat";
      return proposal;
    }

    proposal.kind = "general";
    proposal.minutes = 5;
    proposal.note = "general";
    return proposal;
  },

  estimateTravelMinutes(intent, lowerText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const destinationHint = String(intent.destinationHint || "").trim();
    const startLocationRef = root.player && typeof root.player.locationRef === "string"
      ? root.player.locationRef
      : "";
    const destinationRef = this.makeLocationRef(destinationHint);

    if (startLocationRef && destinationRef) {
      const routeKey = this.makeRouteKey(startLocationRef, destinationRef);
      const known = root.time && root.time.routes && root.time.routes.knownTravelTimes
        ? root.time.routes.knownTravelTimes[routeKey]
        : null;

      if (known && typeof known.minutes === "number" && known.minutes > 0) {
        return known.minutes;
      }
    }

    if (/\bfar\b|\bdistant\b|\blong journey\b/.test(lowerText)) return 480;
    if (/\bnearby\b|\bnear\b|\bclose\b/.test(lowerText)) return 30;
    if (/\breturn\b/.test(lowerText)) return 120;
    if (/\bset out\b|\bjourney\b/.test(lowerText)) return 180;
    if (/\bride\b|\bhorse\b|\bwagon\b/.test(lowerText)) return 120;

    return 180;
  },

  estimateMoveMinutes(lowerText) {
    if (/\brun\b|\bsprint\b/.test(lowerText)) return 5;
    if (/\bclimb\b|\bcrawl\b/.test(lowerText)) return 12;
    if (/\benter\b|\bleave\b|\bapproach\b/.test(lowerText)) return 6;
    return 8;
  },

  estimateRestMinutes(lowerText) {
    if (/\bsleep\b|\bnap\b|\bcamp\b/.test(lowerText)) return 480;
    if (/\bwait\b/.test(lowerText)) return 30;
    if (/\bbreathe\b|\bpause\b|\brecover\b/.test(lowerText)) return 20;
    return 60;
  },

  estimateTrainingMinutes(lowerText) {
    if (/\bspar\b/.test(lowerText)) return 90;
    if (/\bmeditate\b/.test(lowerText)) return 60;
    if (/\bdrill\b|\bexercise\b/.test(lowerText)) return 75;
    return 60;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const text = U && typeof U.normalizeSpaces === "function"
    ? U.normalizeSpaces(String(rawText || ""))
    : String(rawText || "").trim();

  if (!text) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  let changes = 0;

  changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);
  changes += this.updateWeatherAndSeasonHints(text, root);
  changes += this.applyPendingAdvance(text, root);
  changes += this.detectContradictions(text, root);
  this.refreshCache(root);

  return changes;
},

applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
  let changes = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "time" || candidate._timeApplied === true) {
      continue;
    }

    const payload = candidate && candidate.payload ? candidate.payload : {};
    const target = String(payload.label || payload.unit || candidate.type || "time");

    const commit = () => {
      const minutes = this.validatedCandidateToMinutes(candidate, root);
      if (minutes > 0) {
        return this.advanceMinutes(minutes, "validated time consequence", root);
      }
      return 0;
    };

    const localChanges =
      Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
        ? Guard.applyOnce({
            kind: "time_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
            target: target,
            payload: payload,
            sourceId: candidate.id || ""
          }, commit, root)
        : commit();

    changes += localChanges;
    candidate._timeApplied = true;
  }

  return changes;
},

validatedCandidateToMinutes(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const payload = candidate && candidate.payload ? candidate.payload : {};
  const type = String(candidate && candidate.type || "");
  const amount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(payload.amount || 0)));
  const unit = String(payload.unit || "");
  const label = String(payload.label || "");

  if (type === "time_advance") {
    if (amount > 0 && unit === "minutes") return amount;
    if (amount > 0 && unit === "hours") return amount * 60;
    if (label === "next_morning") return this.minutesUntilNextMorning(root);
  }

  if (type === "rest_completion" && label === "overnight") {
    return this.minutesUntilNextMorning(root);
  }

  return 0;
},

minutesUntilNextMorning(root = state.aidrpg) {
  const current = root && root.time && root.time.current ? root.time.current : {};
  const hour = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(current.hour || 0)));
  const minute = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(current.minute || 0)));
  const total = (hour * 60) + minute;
  const target = 6 * 60;

  if (total < target) {
    return target - total;
  }

  return (24 * 60 - total) + target;
},

  applyPendingAdvance(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.time.pendingAdvance;
    if (!pending || typeof pending !== "object") {
      return 0;
    }

    const appliedMinutes = this.resolveAppliedMinutes(pending, text, root);

    root.time.pendingAdvance = null;

    if (appliedMinutes <= 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    const beforeLocationRef = String(pending.startLocationRef || "");
    const afterLocationRef = root.player && typeof root.player.locationRef === "string"
      ? root.player.locationRef
      : "";
    const destinationRef = this.makeLocationRef(pending.destinationHint || "");

    const changes = this.advanceMinutes(appliedMinutes, pending.note || pending.kind || "time advance", root);

    if (pending.kind === "travel" && beforeLocationRef && (afterLocationRef || destinationRef)) {
      const toRef = afterLocationRef || destinationRef;
      if (toRef && toRef !== beforeLocationRef) {
        this.rememberRouteDuration(beforeLocationRef, toRef, appliedMinutes, root);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  resolveAppliedMinutes(pending, text, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
  const kind = String((pending && pending.kind) || "general");

  if (this.isClearlyFailedAction(lower)) {
    if (kind === "travel") return 10;
    if (kind === "rest") return 5;
    return 2;
  }

  let minutes = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number((pending && pending.minutes) || 0)));

  const hardCaps = {
    combat: 20,
    movement: 20,
    inspection: 15,
    interaction: 15,
    social: 20,
    general: 20,
    training: 360,
    rest: 720,
    travel: 720
  };

  const cap = hardCaps[kind] != null ? hardCaps[kind] : 20;

  if (/\bmoments? later\b|\bshortly after\b/.test(lower)) {
    minutes = Math.max(minutes, 5);
    minutes = Math.min(minutes, 15);
  }

  if (/\ban hour later\b|\bafter an hour\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (kind === "travel" || kind === "rest" || kind === "training") {
      minutes = Math.max(minutes, 60);
    }
  }

  if (/\bhours later\b|\bafter hours\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (kind === "travel" || kind === "rest" || kind === "training") {
      minutes = Math.max(minutes, 180);
    }
  }

  if (/\bthe next morning\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (kind === "rest" || kind === "travel" || kind === "training" || kind === "general") {
      return this.minutesUntilTargetHour(8, root);
    }
  }

  if (/\bthat evening\b|\blater that evening\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (kind === "rest" || kind === "travel" || kind === "training" || kind === "general") {
      return this.minutesUntilTargetHour(18, root);
    }
  }

  if (/\bthe next day\b|\btomorrow\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (kind === "rest" || kind === "travel" || kind === "training") {
      return Math.max(minutes, this.minutesUntilNextDay(root));
    }
  }

  if (/\bdays later\b|\bseveral days later\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (kind === "travel" || kind === "rest") {
      return Math.max(minutes, 72 * 60);
    }
  }

  if (minutes > cap) {
    minutes = cap;
  }

  return minutes;
},

  advanceMinutes(minutes, note, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const totalMinutes = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(minutes || 0)));
    if (totalMinutes <= 0) return 0;

    const current = root.time.current;
    const beforeAbsoluteMinutes = (((current.day - 1) * 24 + current.hour) * 60) + current.minute;
    const afterAbsoluteMinutes = beforeAbsoluteMinutes + totalMinutes;

    const nextDay = Math.floor(afterAbsoluteMinutes / (24 * 60)) + 1;
    const remainder = afterAbsoluteMinutes % (24 * 60);
    const nextHour = Math.floor(remainder / 60);
    const nextMinute = remainder % 60;

    current.day = nextDay;
    current.hour = nextHour;
    current.minute = nextMinute;

    root.time.elapsed.totalHours = Math.round((afterAbsoluteMinutes / 60) * 100) / 100;

    this.pushTimelineEvent({
      day: current.day,
      hour: current.hour,
      minute: current.minute,
      note: String(note || "time advance"),
      minutesAdvanced: totalMinutes
    }, root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("time", true, root);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushTimelineEvent(eventObj, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    this.init(root);

    root.time.timeline.recentEvents.push(eventObj);

    while (root.time.timeline.recentEvents.length > 12) {
      root.time.timeline.recentEvents.shift();
    }
  },

  rememberRouteDuration(fromRef, toRef, minutes, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    this.init(root);

    const from = String(fromRef || "").trim();
    const to = String(toRef || "").trim();
    const mins = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(minutes || 0)));

    if (!from || !to || from === to) return;

    const routeKey = this.makeRouteKey(from, to);
    root.time.routes.knownTravelTimes[routeKey] = {
      fromRef: from,
      toRef: to,
      minutes: mins,
      lastConfirmedTurn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0
    };

    if (globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.WorldEntitySystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.WorldEntitySystem.ensureRouteRecord === "function") {
      const routeRecord = globalThis.AIDRPG.WorldEntitySystem.ensureRouteRecord(routeKey, from, to, root);
      if (routeRecord && routeRecord.mutable) {
        routeRecord.mutable.fromRef = from;
        routeRecord.mutable.toRef = to;
        routeRecord.mutable.lastConfirmedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

        if (!Array.isArray(routeRecord.mutable.travelHints)) {
          routeRecord.mutable.travelHints = [];
        }

        const label = mins + "_minutes";
        if (routeRecord.mutable.travelHints.indexOf(label) === -1) {
          routeRecord.mutable.travelHints.push(label);
        }
      }
    }
  },

  detectContradictions(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const nextFlags = [];
  const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
  const hour = root.time.current.hour;

  if (/\bmorning\b/.test(lower) && !(hour >= 4 && hour <= 11)) {
    nextFlags.push("time_of_day_conflict");
  }
  if (/\bafternoon\b/.test(lower) && !(hour >= 12 && hour <= 16)) {
    nextFlags.push("time_of_day_conflict");
  }
  if (/\bevening\b/.test(lower) && !(hour >= 17 && hour <= 20)) {
    nextFlags.push("time_of_day_conflict");
  }
  if (/\bnight\b/.test(lower) && !(hour >= 21 || hour <= 3)) {
    nextFlags.push("time_of_day_conflict");
  }

  if (/\bjust woke up\b|\byou wake up\b/.test(lower) && hour >= 18) {
    nextFlags.push("wake_time_conflict");
  }

  const recent = root.time.timeline.recentEvents.length
    ? root.time.timeline.recentEvents[root.time.timeline.recentEvents.length - 1]
    : null;

  if (/\bdays later\b|\bseveral days later\b/.test(lower)) {
    if (!recent || typeof recent.minutesAdvanced !== "number" || recent.minutesAdvanced < (24 * 60)) {
      nextFlags.push("large_narrative_jump");
    }
  }

  if (recent && typeof recent.minutesAdvanced === "number") {
    const note = String(recent.note || "");
    const mins = recent.minutesAdvanced;

    if ((note === "combat" || note === "inspection" || note === "interaction" || note === "movement") && mins > 30) {
      nextFlags.push("implausible_action_time_jump");
    }

    if (note === "social" && mins > 60) {
      nextFlags.push("implausible_action_time_jump");
    }
  }

  return this.replaceSimpleArray(root.time.contradictionFlags, nextFlags, 6);
},

  updateWeatherAndSeasonHints(text, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    const current = root.time.current;
    let changes = 0;

    const season = this.extractSeason(lower);
    if (season && season !== current.season) {
      current.season = season;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const weather = this.extractWeather(lower);
    if (weather && weather !== current.weather) {
      current.weather = weather;
      changes += 1;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  extractSeason(lower) {
    if (/\bspring\b/.test(lower)) return "spring";
    if (/\bsummer\b/.test(lower)) return "summer";
    if (/\bautumn\b|\bfall\b/.test(lower)) return "autumn";
    if (/\bwinter\b/.test(lower)) return "winter";
    return "";
  },

  extractWeather(lower) {
    if (/\bstorm\b|\bthunder\b|\blightning\b/.test(lower)) return "storm";
    if (/\brain\b|\brains\b|\bdrizzle\b/.test(lower)) return "rain";
    if (/\bsnow\b|\bblizzard\b/.test(lower)) return "snow";
    if (/\bfog\b|\bmist\b/.test(lower)) return "fog";
    if (/\bclear sky\b|\bsunny\b/.test(lower)) return "clear";
    if (/\bcloudy\b|\bovercast\b/.test(lower)) return "cloudy";
    if (/\bwindy\b|\bgale\b/.test(lower)) return "windy";
    return "";
  },

  minutesUntilTargetHour(targetHour, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const current = root.time.current;
    const currentMinutes = (current.hour * 60) + current.minute;
    const target = Math.max(0, Math.min(23, Math.floor(Number(targetHour || 0)))) * 60;

    if (target > currentMinutes) {
      return target - currentMinutes;
    }

    return (24 * 60) - currentMinutes + target;
  },

  minutesUntilNextDay(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const current = root.time.current;
    const currentMinutes = (current.hour * 60) + current.minute;
    return (24 * 60) - currentMinutes + (8 * 60);
  },

  isClearlyFailedAction(lowerText) {
    return /\b(fails?|failed|cannot|can't|unable to|could not|couldn't|refuses?|refused|without managing to)\b/.test(String(lowerText || ""));
  },

  makeLocationRef(label) {
    const clean = String(label || "").trim();
    if (!clean) return "";
    return "location_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(clean);
  },

  makeRouteKey(fromRef, toRef) {
    return "route_" + this.makeKeyFromLabel(String(fromRef || "") + "_to_" + String(toRef || ""));
  },

  makeKeyFromLabel(label) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.toKey === "function") {
      return U.toKey(label);
    }

    return String(label || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
      .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(targetArray, nextValues, maxCount) {
    if (!Array.isArray(targetArray)) return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(nextValues)) nextValues = [];

    const cleaned = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < nextValues.length && i < (maxCount || 8); i += 1) {
      const value = String(nextValues[i] || "").trim();
      if (!value) continue;
      if (cleaned.indexOf(value) === -1) cleaned.push(value);
    }

    let changed = 0;
    if (targetArray.length !== cleaned.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < targetArray.length; i += 1) {
        if (targetArray[i] !== cleaned[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    targetArray.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < cleaned.length; i += 1) {
      targetArray.push(cleaned[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  refreshCache(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";

    this.init(root);

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    const summary = this.getCompactSummary(root);
    root.cache.timeSummary = summary;

    return summary;
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !root.time || !root.time.current) {
      return "Time unknown.";
    }

    const current = root.time.current;
    const parts = [];

    parts.push("Day " + current.day);
    parts.push(this.pad2(current.hour) + ":" + this.pad2(current.minute));

    if (current.season) {
      parts.push("Season: " + current.season);
    }

    if (current.weather) {
      parts.push("Weather: " + current.weather);
    }

    if (root.time.contradictionFlags && root.time.contradictionFlags.length) {
      parts.push("Flags: " + root.time.contradictionFlags.join(", "));
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  },

  pad2(value) {
    const n = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(value || 0)));
    return n < 10 ? "0" + n : String(n);
  }
},

    // === ABILITY SYSTEM ===
AbilitySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.abilities || typeof root.abilities !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.abilities)) {
      root.abilities = {};
    }

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    const abilities = root.abilities;

    if (!abilities.byId || typeof abilities.byId !== "object" || Array.isArray(abilities.byId)) {
      abilities.byId = {};
    }
    if (!abilities.byKey || typeof abilities.byKey !== "object" || Array.isArray(abilities.byKey)) {
      abilities.byKey = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.allIds)) abilities.allIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.skillIds)) abilities.skillIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.talentIds)) abilities.talentIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.passiveIds)) abilities.passiveIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(abilities.recentEvents)) abilities.recentEvents = [];

    if (typeof root.pending.abilityCandidate === "undefined") {
      root.pending.abilityCandidate = null;
    }

    return abilities;
  },

  processInput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    this.init(root);

    const intent = root.pending && root.pending.inputIntent ? root.pending.inputIntent : null;
    const candidate = this.extractInputCandidate(rawText, intent);
    root.pending.abilityCandidate = candidate;
    return candidate;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  let changes = 0;
  const pending = root.pending ? root.pending.abilityCandidate : null;

  // Keep explicit input-driven ability confirmation.
  // This is structured and anchored to player intent, so it is the safe canon path for now.
  if (pending && !this.isClearlyFailedOutput(raw)) {
    changes += this.confirmCandidate(pending, raw, root);
  }

  // Do NOT create brand-new abilities directly from raw output prose here.
  // Raw output-only discovery will return later behind a validated consequence path.
  const revisionCandidates = this.extractRevisionCandidates(raw, root);
  for (let i = 0; i < revisionCandidates.length; i += 1) {
    changes += this.applyRevisionCandidate(revisionCandidates[i], raw, root);
  }

  // Existing abilities may still accumulate observed-use notes.
  // These are softer observation updates, not brand-new major canon records.
  changes += this.captureObservedUseSignals(raw, root);

  if (root.pending && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(root.pending, "abilityCandidate")) {
    root.pending.abilityCandidate = null;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("abilities", "summary", root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Ability state updated through locked canon path.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  extractInputCandidate(rawText, intent) {
  if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") return null;

  const primaryType = String(intent.primaryType || "");
  const raw = String(rawText || "");

  const explicitTrainingInput =
    /^\s*(?:i\s+)?(?:train|practice|drill|exercise|meditate on|spar with|work on)\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?/i.test(raw);

  const effectiveType = explicitTrainingInput ? "train" : primaryType;

  if (effectiveType !== "use_ability" && effectiveType !== "train") {
    return null;
  }

  let rawName = "";
  if (effectiveType === "train") {
    rawName = intent.trainingHint || intent.abilityHint || this.extractNamedPhrase(raw);
  } else if (intent.abilityHint) {
    rawName = intent.abilityHint;
  } else {
    rawName = this.extractNamedPhrase(raw);
  }

  const cleanName = this.cleanAbilityName(rawName);
  if (!cleanName) return null;

  const abilityType = this.inferAbilityType(cleanName, raw, intent);
  const intendedFunction = this.inferIntendedFunction(intent, raw);

  return {
    name: cleanName,
    type: abilityType,
    mode: effectiveType === "train" ? "train" : "use",
    confidence: effectiveType === "train" ? 0.74 : 0.68,
    originContext: raw,
    intendedFunction: intendedFunction,
    stableMeaningFlags: ["explicit_input", "structured_record"],
    keyPhrases: this.buildKeyPhrases(cleanName, intent),
    sourceIntentType: effectiveType,
    sourceTags: Array.isArray(intent.tags) ? intent.tags.slice(0, 6) : []
  };
},

  extractOutputCandidates(rawText) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const candidates = [];
  const patterns = [
    /\b(?:learn|learns|learned|develop|develops|developed|gain|gains|gained|acquire|acquires|acquired|unlock|unlocks|unlocked|manifest|manifests|manifested|awaken|awakens|awakened)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?(skill|talent|passive|ability)\s+(?:called\s+|known as\s+)?["“]?([^"”\n.,;:!?]{2,60})/gi,
    /\bnew\s+(skill|talent|passive|ability)\s*:\s*["“]?([^"”\n.,;:!?]{2,60})/gi,
    /\b(?:the|this)\s+(skill|talent|passive|ability)\s+(?:is|was)\s+called\s+["“]?([^"”\n.,;:!?]{2,60})/gi
  ];

  for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
    let match;
    while ((match = patterns[p].exec(raw))) {
      const typeWord = String(match[1] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      const rawName = String(match[2] || "").trim();
      const cleanName = this.cleanAbilityName(rawName);
      if (!cleanName) continue;

      candidates.push({
        name: cleanName,
        type: typeWord === "ability" ? "skill" : typeWord,
        mode: "learn",
        confidence: 0.82,
        originContext: raw,
        intendedFunction: this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(raw, cleanName),
        stableMeaningFlags: ["explicit_output", "structured_record", "origin_preserved"],
        keyPhrases: this.buildKeyPhrases(cleanName, null),
        sourceIntentType: "output_discovery",
        sourceTags: this.extractSemanticTags(raw)
      });
    }
  }

  return candidates;
},

  confirmCandidate(candidate, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  const abilities = root.abilities;
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
  const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
  if (!record) return 0;

  let changes = 0;
  const evidence = this.makeEvidenceSnippet(rawText || candidate.originContext || "");
  const observedFunction = this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(rawText || candidate.originContext || "", record.identity.name);
  const emergentTags = this.extractSemanticTags(rawText || candidate.originContext || "");

  if (!record.meaning.originContext && candidate.originContext) {
    record.meaning.originContext = String(candidate.originContext);
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (!record.meaning.originSignature) {
    record.meaning.originSignature = this.makeOriginSignature(candidate.originContext || record.identity.name);
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (candidate.intendedFunction && (!record.meaning.intendedFunction || record.meaning.intendedFunction === "general")) {
    record.meaning.intendedFunction = String(candidate.intendedFunction);
    changes += 1;
  }

  changes += this.mergeUnique(record.meaning.stableMeaningFlags, candidate.stableMeaningFlags);
  changes += this.mergeUnique(record.keys.keyPhrases, candidate.keyPhrases);
  changes += this.mergeUnique(record.source.sourceTags, candidate.sourceTags);
  changes += this.mergeUnique(record.observation.semanticTags, emergentTags);

  if (candidate.sourceIntentType && record.source.sourceIntentType !== candidate.sourceIntentType) {
    record.source.sourceIntentType = String(candidate.sourceIntentType);
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (record.observation.lastEvidence !== evidence) {
    record.observation.lastEvidence = evidence;
    changes += 1;
  }

  const nextConfidence = Math.max(Number(record.observation.confidence || 0), Number(candidate.confidence || 0));
  if (record.observation.confidence !== nextConfidence) {
    record.observation.confidence = nextConfidence;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (record.progression.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
    record.progression.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (candidate.mode === "train") {
    record.progression.trainingCount += 1;
    record.progression.lastTrainedTurn = turn;
    record.progression.xp += 2;
    changes += 1;
  } else if (candidate.mode === "use") {
    record.progression.useCount += 1;
    record.progression.lastUsedTurn = turn;
    record.progression.xp += 1;
    changes += 1;
  } else if (candidate.mode === "learn") {
    record.progression.xp += 3;
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (observedFunction && observedFunction !== "general") {
    changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.observation.observedFunctions, observedFunction, 8);
    if (record.meaning.intendedFunction && record.meaning.intendedFunction !== observedFunction) {
      changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.meaning.emergentApplications, observedFunction, 8);
    }
  }

  const nextTier = this.tierFromXp(record.progression.xp);
  if (record.progression.tier !== nextTier) {
    record.progression.tier = nextTier;
    record.progression.masteryState = this.masteryLabelForTier(nextTier);
    changes += 1;
  }

  if (changes > 0) {
    this.pushRecentEvent({
      turn: turn,
      type: record.identity.type,
      name: record.identity.name,
      mode: candidate.mode
    }, abilities);

    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("abilities", record.identity.id, root);
    }

    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Ability updated: " + record.identity.name + " [" + record.identity.type + "]");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

  findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
  this.init(root);

  const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
  const abilities = root.abilities;
  const normalized = this.normalizeAbilityKey(candidate.name);
  if (!normalized) return null;

  let abilityId = abilities.byKey[normalized] || "";
  if (abilityId && abilities.byId[abilityId]) {
    return abilities.byId[abilityId];
  }

  abilityId = U.makeId(candidate.type || "ability");
  const type = this.normalizeAbilityType(candidate.type);
  const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

  const record = {
    identity: {
      id: abilityId,
      name: String(candidate.name),
      type: type
    },
    keys: {
      normalizedName: normalized,
      aliases: [],
      keyPhrases: []
    },
    meaning: {
      originContext: "",
      originSignature: "",
      intendedFunction: String(candidate.intendedFunction || "general"),
      stableMeaningFlags: [],
      emergentApplications: []
    },
    progression: {
      tier: 1,
      xp: 0,
      useCount: 0,
      trainingCount: 0,
      masteryState: "novice",
      lastConfirmedTurn: turn,
      lastUsedTurn: 0,
      lastTrainedTurn: 0
    },
    observation: {
      confidence: Number(candidate.confidence || 0.55),
      lastEvidence: "",
      notes: [],
      observedFunctions: [],
      semanticTags: []
    },
    source: {
      firstSeenTurn: turn,
      sourceIntentType: "",
      sourceTags: []
    }
  };

  abilities.byId[abilityId] = record;
  abilities.byKey[normalized] = abilityId;
  abilities.allIds.push(abilityId);

  if (type === "skill") {
    abilities.skillIds.push(abilityId);
  } else if (type === "talent") {
    abilities.talentIds.push(abilityId);
  } else {
    abilities.passiveIds.push(abilityId);
  }

  return record;
},

  cleanAbilityName(value) {
  const text = String(value || "")
    .replace(/["'`]+/g, "")
    .replace(/^(?:my|the|a|an)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/\s+(?:on|at|against|toward|towards|with|using|for|into|from|to|by|until|through|across|while|as)\b.*$/i, "")
    .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  if (!text) return "";
  if (text.length < 2 || text.length > 48) return "";
  if (text.split(" ").length > 6) return "";

  const lower = text.toLowerCase();
  const banned = new Set([
    "magic",
    "ability",
    "skill",
    "talent",
    "passive",
    "power",
    "attack",
    "move",
    "technique",
    "training",
    "it",
    "that",
    "this"
  ]);

  if (banned.has(lower)) return "";
  return text;
},

  extractNamedPhrase(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const patterns = [
      /\b(?:cast|channel|invoke|activate|use|unleash|focus|practice|train|drill|work on)\s+(?:my\s+|the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.,;!?]+)/i,
      /["“]([^"”]{2,48})["”]/
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = raw.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return match[1];
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  inferAbilityType(name, rawText, intent) {
    const lowerName = String(name || "").toLowerCase();
    const lowerRaw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const primaryType = intent && intent.primaryType ? String(intent.primaryType) : "";

    if (/\b(passive|affinity|resistance|adaptation|instinct|attunement|constitution|tolerance|aura)\b/.test(lowerName) ||
        /\bpassive\b/.test(lowerRaw)) {
      return "passive";
    }

    if (/\b(talent|technique|art|style|form|method|stance)\b/.test(lowerName) ||
        /\btalent\b/.test(lowerRaw)) {
      return "talent";
    }

    if (primaryType === "train" && /\b(passive|talent)\b/.test(lowerRaw)) {
      return /\bpassive\b/.test(lowerRaw) ? "passive" : "talent";
    }

    return "skill";
  },

  normalizeAbilityType(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "skill").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (lower === "talent") return "talent";
    if (lower === "passive") return "passive";
    return "skill";
  },

  normalizeAbilityKey(value) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    return U.toKey(String(value || ""));
  },

  inferIntendedFunction(intent, rawText) {
    if (!intent || typeof intent !== "object") return "general";

    const parts = [];

    if (intent.primaryType === "use_ability") {
      parts.push("activated technique");
    } else if (intent.primaryType === "train") {
      parts.push("trained technique");
    }

    if (intent.targetHint) {
      parts.push("targeting " + String(intent.targetHint));
    }

    if (intent.styleHint) {
      parts.push("style " + String(intent.styleHint));
    }

    if (intent.trainingHint && intent.primaryType === "train") {
      parts.push("focus " + String(intent.trainingHint));
    }

    if (parts.length) {
      return parts.join(" | ");
    }

    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    if (/\b(defend|guard|brace|protect)\b/.test(lower)) return "defensive application";
    if (/\b(move|step|dash|rush|advance|retreat)\b/.test(lower)) return "mobility application";
    if (/\b(attack|strike|hit|slash|stab|shoot)\b/.test(lower)) return "offensive application";
    return "general";
  },

  buildKeyPhrases(name, intent) {
    const values = [];
    const push = function (value) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text) return;
      if (values.indexOf(text) === -1) values.push(text);
    };

    push(String(name || ""));
    push(this.normalizeAbilityKey(name));

    if (intent && intent.styleHint) push(intent.styleHint);
    if (intent && intent.targetHint) push(intent.targetHint);

    return values;
  },

  isClearlyFailedOutput(rawText) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    return /\b(fail to|fails to|failed to|cannot|can't|unable to|nothing happens|no effect|fizzles|misfires|does not activate|doesn't activate)\b/.test(lower);
  },

  tierFromXp(xp) {
    const value = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(xp || 0)));
    if (value >= 40) return 5;
    if (value >= 22) return 4;
    if (value >= 10) return 3;
    if (value >= 4) return 2;
    return 1;
  },

  masteryLabelForTier(tier) {
    const value = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(tier || 1)));
