    if (value >= 5) return "mastered";
    if (value === 4) return "expert";
    if (value === 3) return "adept";
    if (value === 2) return "practiced";
    return "novice";
  },

  makeEvidenceSnippet(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 160);
  },

  makeOriginSignature(value) {
  const text = String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim()
    .slice(0, 120);

  let hash = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += 1) {
    hash ^= text.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619);
    hash >>>= 0;
  }

  return "sig_" + String(hash);
},

inferOutputIntendedFunction(rawText, abilityName) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  const lowerName = String(abilityName || "").toLowerCase();

  if (lowerName && lower.indexOf(lowerName) === -1 && !/\b(skill|talent|passive|ability)\b/.test(lower)) {
    return "general";
  }

  if (/\b(block|brace|guard|shield|ward|protect|deflect)\b/.test(lower)) return "defensive application";
  if (/\b(dash|rush|step|blink|move|advance|retreat|evade)\b/.test(lower)) return "mobility application";
  if (/\b(strike|slash|stab|pierce|blast|burn|shock|smash|attack|hit)\b/.test(lower)) return "offensive application";
  if (/\b(scan|sense|track|inspect|read|detect|locate|survey)\b/.test(lower)) return "detection application";
  if (/\b(train|practice|drill|repeat|refine|hone)\b/.test(lower)) return "training application";
  if (/\b(endure|withstand|resist|stabilize|recover|heal)\b/.test(lower)) return "sustainment application";

  return "general";
},

extractSemanticTags(rawText) {
  const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
  const tags = [];

  const push = function (value) {
    if (tags.indexOf(value) === -1) tags.push(value);
  };

  if (/\bfire|burn|flame|ember|heat\b/.test(lower)) push("fire");
  if (/\bice|frost|cold|freeze\b/.test(lower)) push("ice");
  if (/\blightning|storm|shock|thunder\b/.test(lower)) push("lightning");
  if (/\bshadow|dark|veil|obscure\b/.test(lower)) push("shadow");
  if (/\blight|radiant|glow|holy\b/.test(lower)) push("light");
  if (/\bguard|block|brace|shield|ward\b/.test(lower)) push("defense");
  if (/\bdash|rush|evade|retreat|advance|blink\b/.test(lower)) push("mobility");
  if (/\bscan|detect|sense|track|survey|inspect\b/.test(lower)) push("detection");
  if (/\bheal|recover|restore|stabilize\b/.test(lower)) push("recovery");
  if (/\bweapon|blade|spear|arrow|bow|hammer|fist\b/.test(lower)) push("martial");

  return tags;
},

extractRevisionCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return [];
  this.init(root);

  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const ids = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds : [];
  const candidates = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
    const record = abilities.byId[ids[i]];
    if (!record || !record.identity) continue;

    const name = String(record.identity.name || "");
    const lowerName = name.toLowerCase();
    if (!lowerName) continue;
    if (lower.indexOf(lowerName) === -1) continue;

    const observedFunction = this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(raw, name);
    const semanticTags = this.extractSemanticTags(raw);

    candidates.push({
      abilityId: record.identity.id,
      observedFunction: observedFunction,
      semanticTags: semanticTags,
      evidence: this.makeEvidenceSnippet(raw)
    });
  }

  return candidates;
},

applyRevisionCandidate(candidate, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const record = abilities.byId && candidate.abilityId ? abilities.byId[candidate.abilityId] : null;
  if (!record) return 0;

  let changes = 0;

  if (candidate.observedFunction && candidate.observedFunction !== "general") {
    changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.observation.observedFunctions, candidate.observedFunction, 8);

    if (record.meaning.intendedFunction && record.meaning.intendedFunction !== candidate.observedFunction) {
      changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.meaning.emergentApplications, candidate.observedFunction, 8);
      changes += this.mergeUnique(record.meaning.stableMeaningFlags, ["emergent_application_seen"]);
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(candidate.semanticTags)) {
    changes += this.mergeUnique(record.observation.semanticTags, candidate.semanticTags);
  }

  if (candidate.evidence && record.observation.lastEvidence !== candidate.evidence) {
    record.observation.lastEvidence = candidate.evidence;
    changes += 1;
  }

  return changes;
},

captureObservedUseSignals(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const ids = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds : [];
  let changes = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
    const record = abilities.byId[ids[i]];
    if (!record || !record.identity) continue;

    const name = String(record.identity.name || "").toLowerCase();
    if (!name || raw.toLowerCase().indexOf(name) === -1) continue;

    const fn = this.inferOutputIntendedFunction(raw, name);
    if (fn && fn !== "general") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueNote(record.observation.observedFunctions, fn, 8);
    }

    changes += this.mergeUnique(record.observation.semanticTags, this.extractSemanticTags(raw));
  }

  return changes;
},

pushUniqueNote(list, value, maxEntries) {
  if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
  const text = String(value || "").trim();
  if (!text) return 0;
  if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;

  list.push(text);

  const limit = Math.max(1, Number(maxEntries || 8));
  while (list.length > limit) {
    list.shift();
  }

  return 1;
},

  mergeUnique(target, values) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target) || !Array.isArray(values)) return 0;
    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i += 1) {
      const text = String(values[i] || "").trim();
      if (!text) continue;
      if (target.indexOf(text) === -1) {
        target.push(text);
        changes += 1;
      }
    }
    return changes;
  },

  pushRecentEvent(eventRecord, abilities) {
    if (!abilities || !Array.isArray(abilities.recentEvents)) return;
    abilities.recentEvents.push(eventRecord);
    while (abilities.recentEvents.length > 12) {
      abilities.recentEvents.shift();
    }
  },

  getRelevantContextHint(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.pending ? root.pending.abilityCandidate : null;
    if (pending && pending.name) {
      const parts = ["Ability focus: " + pending.name, "Type: " + this.normalizeAbilityType(pending.type)];
      if (pending.intendedFunction && pending.intendedFunction !== "general") {
        parts.push("Intent: " + pending.intendedFunction);
      }
      return parts.join(" | ");
    }

    return "";
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg, maxCount = 2) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
  this.init(root);

  const abilities = root.abilities || {};
  const ids = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds.slice() : [];
  if (!ids.length) return "";

  ids.sort(function (a, b) {
    const recordA = abilities.byId[a];
    const recordB = abilities.byId[b];
    const turnA = recordA && recordA.progression ? Number(recordA.progression.lastConfirmedTurn || 0) : 0;
    const turnB = recordB && recordB.progression ? Number(recordB.progression.lastConfirmedTurn || 0) : 0;
    return turnB - turnA;
  });

  const names = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < ids.length && names.length < maxCount; i += 1) {
    const record = abilities.byId[ids[i]];
    if (!record || !record.identity || !record.progression) continue;

    const name = String(record.identity.name || "").trim();
    const mastery = String(record.progression.masteryState || "novice").trim();
    if (name) {
      names.push(name + " (" + mastery + ")");
    }
  }

  const parts = [
    "Abilities: " + ids.length,
    "Skills: " + (Array.isArray(abilities.skillIds) ? abilities.skillIds.length : 0),
    "Talents: " + (Array.isArray(abilities.talentIds) ? abilities.talentIds.length : 0),
    "Passives: " + (Array.isArray(abilities.passiveIds) ? abilities.passiveIds.length : 0)
  ];

  if (names.length) {
    parts.push("Recent: " + names.join(", "));
  }

  return parts.join(" | ");
},
},

    // === PERSISTENT TRAIT / CONDITION SYSTEM ===
PersistentTraitSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.player || typeof root.player !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.player)) {
      root.player = {};
    }

    const player = root.player;

    if (!player.conditions || typeof player.conditions !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.conditions)) {
      player.conditions = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.active)) player.conditions.active = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.permanent)) player.conditions.permanent = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.vulnerabilities)) player.conditions.vulnerabilities = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(player.conditions.immunities)) player.conditions.immunities = [];

    if (!player.traitState || typeof player.traitState !== "object" || Array.isArray(player.traitState)) {
      player.traitState = {};
    }

    const traitState = player.traitState;

    if (!traitState.byId || typeof traitState.byId !== "object" || Array.isArray(traitState.byId)) {
      traitState.byId = {};
    }
    if (!traitState.byKey || typeof traitState.byKey !== "object" || Array.isArray(traitState.byKey)) {
      traitState.byKey = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.allIds)) traitState.allIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.conditionIds)) traitState.conditionIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.traitIds)) traitState.traitIds = [];

    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.resistanceTags)) traitState.resistanceTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.immunityTags)) traitState.immunityTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.vulnerabilityTags)) traitState.vulnerabilityTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.adaptationTags)) traitState.adaptationTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.sensitivityTags)) traitState.sensitivityTags = [];

    if (!traitState.exposureByDomain || typeof traitState.exposureByDomain !== "object" || Array.isArray(traitState.exposureByDomain)) {
      traitState.exposureByDomain = {};
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.growthHooks)) traitState.growthHooks = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(traitState.recentEvents)) traitState.recentEvents = [];

    return traitState;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const U = A.Utils;
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

  if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
    if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
      return 0;
    }
  }

  let changes = 0;

  // Major condition canon now comes from validated consequences only.
  changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

  // Keep legacy visible arrays synchronized from the canonical trait/condition records.
  changes += this.syncLegacyPlayerArrays(root);

  // Allow low-risk exposure tracking only when it is anchored to already-validated player evidence.
  changes += this.captureValidatedExposureSignals(root);

  if (changes > 0) {
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("player", true, root);
    }
    if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
      U.invalidatePackets(root);
    }
    if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
      U.logEvent("Persistent trait state updated through locked canon path.");
    }
  }

  return changes;
},

applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
  const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
  let changes = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "player" || candidate._traitApplied === true) {
      continue;
    }

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const status = String(payload.status || "").trim();

    const commit = () => {
      let localChanges = 0;

      if (candidate.type === "status_gain" && status) {
        localChanges += this.applyConditionCandidate({
          name: status,
          mode: "active",
          severity: this.validationSeverityForStatus(status)
        }, candidate.evidence || status, root);
      } else if (candidate.type === "status_loss" && status) {
        localChanges += this.removeConditionByName(status, root);
      }

      return localChanges;
    };

    const localChanges =
      Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
        ? Guard.applyOnce({
            kind: "trait_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
            target: status || String(candidate.type || "trait"),
            payload: payload,
            sourceId: candidate.id || ""
          }, commit, root)
        : commit();

    changes += localChanges;
    candidate._traitApplied = true;
  }

  return changes;
},

captureValidatedExposureSignals(root = state.aidrpg) {
  if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
  this.init(root);

  const pending = root.pending || {};
  const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
  const textParts = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
    const candidate = validated[i];
    if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "player") continue;

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};

    if (typeof candidate.evidence === "string" && candidate.evidence.trim()) {
      textParts.push(candidate.evidence.trim());
    }

    if (typeof payload.status === "string" && payload.status.trim()) {
      textParts.push(payload.status.trim());
    }

    if (typeof payload.injuryTag === "string" && payload.injuryTag.trim()) {
      textParts.push(payload.injuryTag.trim());
    }

    if (typeof payload.bodyPart === "string" && payload.bodyPart.trim()) {
      textParts.push(payload.bodyPart.trim());
    }
  }

  if (!textParts.length) return 0;
  return this.captureExposureSignals(textParts.join(" | "), root);
},

validationSeverityForStatus(status) {
  const lower = String(status || "").toLowerCase();
  if (lower === "burned" || lower === "bleeding" || lower === "poisoned" || lower === "infected") {
    return "severe";
  }
  if (lower === "chilled" || lower === "soaked" || lower === "fatigued" || lower === "hungry") {
    return "moderate";
  }
  return "moderate";
},

  validationSeverityForStatus(status) {
  const lower = String(status || "").toLowerCase();

  if (
    lower === "burned" ||
    lower === "bleeding" ||
    lower === "poisoned" ||
    lower === "infected" ||
    lower === "exhausted"
  ) {
    return "severe";
  }

  if (
    lower === "chilled" ||
    lower === "soaked" ||
    lower === "fatigued" ||
    lower === "hungry" ||
    lower === "weakened"
  ) {
    return "moderate";
  }

  return "moderate";
},

  extractConditionRemovals(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const removals = [];
    const names = [
      "bleeding",
      "poisoned",
      "burned",
      "chilled",
      "frozen",
      "exhausted",
      "fatigued",
      "hungry",
      "soaked",
      "cursed",
      "diseased",
      "stunned",
      "dazed",
      "blinded",
      "deafened",
      "weakened",
      "infected"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i += 1) {
      const name = names[i];
      const re = new RegExp(
        "\\b(?:no longer|without|free of|recovered from|recovers from|recovers from|cured of|cure[d]? of|ends|ended|fades|faded|stops|stopped|lifts|lifted|clears|cleared)\\b[^.!?]{0,30}\\b" + name + "\\b|\\b" + name + "\\b[^.!?]{0,20}\\b(?:ends|ended|fades|faded|stops|stopped|lifts|lifted|clears|cleared)\\b",
        "i"
      );

      if (re.test(raw)) {
        removals.push(name);
      }
    }

    return removals;
  },

  extractTraitCandidates(rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const candidates = [];
    const patterns = [
      { category: "immunity", re: /\b(?:immune|immunity)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "resistance", re: /\b(?:resistant|resistance)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "vulnerability", re: /\b(?:vulnerable|susceptible|weak)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "adaptation", re: /\b(?:adapted|acclimated|accustomed|tolerant)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "sensitivity", re: /\b(?:sensitive)\s+to\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi },
      { category: "resistance", re: /\bresists?\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\b/gi }
    ];

    for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = patterns[p].re.exec(raw))) {
        const domain = this.cleanDomainName(match[1]);
        if (!domain) continue;

        candidates.push({
          category: patterns[p].category,
          domain: domain
        });
      }
    }

    return candidates;
  },

  inferConditionMode(rawText, name) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const lowerName = String(name || "").toLowerCase();

    const permanentRe = new RegExp("\\b(?:permanent|chronic|lingering|lasting|lasting permanently|won't heal|will not heal)\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b" + lowerName + "\\b|\\b" + lowerName + "\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b(?:permanent|chronic|lingering|lasting)\\b", "i");
    if (permanentRe.test(raw)) return "permanent";

    return "active";
  },

  inferSeverity(rawText, name) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const lowerName = String(name || "").toLowerCase();

    const severeRe = new RegExp("\\b(?:severe|serious|deep|major|critical|grave)\\b[^.!?]{0,18}\\b" + lowerName + "\\b|\\b" + lowerName + "\\b[^.!?]{0,18}\\b(?:severe|serious|deep|major|critical|grave)\\b", "i");
    if (severeRe.test(raw)) return "severe";

    const minorRe = new RegExp("\\b(?:light|minor|slight|faint)\\b[^.!?]{0,18}\\b" + lowerName + "\\b|\\b" + lowerName + "\\b[^.!?]{0,18}\\b(?:light|minor|slight|faint)\\b", "i");
    if (minorRe.test(raw)) return "minor";

    return "moderate";
  },

  cleanDomainName(value) {
    const text = String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (!text) return "";
    if (text.length > 24) return "";
    if (/\d/.test(text)) return "";
    if (text.split(" ").length > 3) return "";

    const banned = new Set([
      "it",
      "them",
      "that",
      "this",
      "everything",
      "anything"
    ]);

    if (banned.has(text)) return "";
    return text;
  },

  makeRecordName(category, domain) {
    const cleanDomain = this.cleanDomainName(domain);
    if (!cleanDomain) return "";

    if (category === "adaptation") return cleanDomain + " acclimation";
    if (category === "sensitivity") return cleanDomain + " sensitivity";
    if (category === "immunity") return cleanDomain + " immunity";
    if (category === "vulnerability") return cleanDomain + " vulnerability";
    return cleanDomain + " resistance";
  },

  findOrCreateRecord(name, kind, category, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const traitState = root.player.traitState;
    const normalized = U.toKey(String(name || ""));
    if (!normalized) return null;

    let recordId = traitState.byKey[normalized] || "";
    if (recordId && traitState.byId[recordId]) {
      return traitState.byId[recordId];
    }

    recordId = U.makeId(kind === "trait" ? "trait" : "condition");
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    const record = {
      identity: {
        id: recordId,
        name: String(name || ""),
        kind: kind === "trait" ? "trait" : "condition",
        category: String(category || (kind === "trait" ? "trait" : "condition"))
      },
      state: {
        mode: "active",
        severity: "moderate",
        stage: 1,
        startedTurn: turn,
        lastConfirmedTurn: turn,
        lastChangedTurn: turn
      },
      meaning: {
        originContext: "",
        stableFlags: [],
        domain: ""
      },
      progression: {
        exposureCount: 0,
        adaptationScore: 0,
        growthHooks: []
      }
    };

    traitState.byId[recordId] = record;
    traitState.byKey[normalized] = recordId;
    traitState.allIds.push(recordId);

    if (kind === "trait") {
      traitState.traitIds.push(recordId);
    } else {
      traitState.conditionIds.push(recordId);
    }

    return record;
  },

  applyConditionCandidate(candidate, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(candidate.name, "condition", "condition", root);
    if (!record) return 0;

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (record.state.mode !== String(candidate.mode || "active")) {
      record.state.mode = String(candidate.mode || "active");
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.severity !== String(candidate.severity || "moderate")) {
      record.state.severity = String(candidate.severity || "moderate");
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
      record.state.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.lastChangedTurn !== turn) {
      record.state.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const evidence = this.makeEvidenceSnippet(rawText);
    if (record.meaning.originContext !== evidence) {
      record.meaning.originContext = evidence;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.identity.category !== "condition") {
      record.identity.category = "condition";
      changes += 1;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  removeConditionByName(name, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const traitState = root.player.traitState;
    const key = U.toKey(String(name || ""));
    if (!key) return 0;

    const recordId = traitState.byKey[key];
    if (!recordId || !traitState.byId[recordId]) return 0;

    const record = traitState.byId[recordId];
    if (record.identity.kind !== "condition") return 0;

    delete traitState.byId[recordId];
    delete traitState.byKey[key];

    this.removeValueLocal(traitState.allIds, recordId);
    this.removeValueLocal(traitState.conditionIds, recordId);

    return 1;
  },

  applyTraitCandidate(candidate, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const traitState = root.player.traitState;
    const name = this.makeRecordName(candidate.category, candidate.domain);
    if (!name) return 0;

    const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(name, "trait", candidate.category, root);
    if (!record) return 0;

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (record.identity.category !== candidate.category) {
      record.identity.category = candidate.category;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.meaning.domain !== candidate.domain) {
      record.meaning.domain = candidate.domain;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.mode !== "permanent") {
      record.state.mode = "permanent";
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.lastConfirmedTurn !== turn) {
      record.state.lastConfirmedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.state.lastChangedTurn !== turn) {
      record.state.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const evidence = this.makeEvidenceSnippet(rawText);
    if (record.meaning.originContext !== evidence) {
      record.meaning.originContext = evidence;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (candidate.category === "resistance") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.resistanceTags, candidate.domain);
    } else if (candidate.category === "immunity") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.immunityTags, candidate.domain);
    } else if (candidate.category === "vulnerability") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.vulnerabilityTags, candidate.domain);
    } else if (candidate.category === "adaptation") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.adaptationTags, candidate.domain);
    } else if (candidate.category === "sensitivity") {
      changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.sensitivityTags, candidate.domain);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  captureExposureSignals(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const raw = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const traitState = root.player.traitState;
    const domains = this.extractExposureDomains(raw);
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < domains.length; i += 1) {
      const domain = domains[i];

      if (!traitState.exposureByDomain[domain] || typeof traitState.exposureByDomain[domain] !== "object") {
        traitState.exposureByDomain[domain] = {
          count: 0,
          lastTurn: 0
        };
      }

      traitState.exposureByDomain[domain].count += 1;
      traitState.exposureByDomain[domain].lastTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number"
        ? root.meta.currentTurn
        : 0;
      changes += 1;

      if (traitState.exposureByDomain[domain].count >= 3) {
        changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(traitState.growthHooks, domain + "_adaptation_candidate");
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  extractExposureDomains(rawText) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const found = [];

    const push = function (value) {
      if (found.indexOf(value) === -1) found.push(value);
    };

    if (/\bfire|burn|flame|ember|heat\b/.test(lower)) push("fire");
    if (/\bcold|freeze|frost|ice|chill\b/.test(lower)) push("cold");
    if (/\bpoison|venom|toxic\b/.test(lower)) push("poison");
    if (/\blightning|storm|shock|thunder\b/.test(lower)) push("lightning");
    if (/\bshadow|dark|gloom|veil\b/.test(lower)) push("shadow");
    if (/\blight|radiant|sunlight|holy\b/.test(lower)) push("light");
    if (/\bwater|rain|soaked|drowned|flood\b/.test(lower)) push("water");
    if (/\bdisease|plague|sick|infect\b/.test(lower)) push("disease");
    if (/\brot|decay|wither|blight\b/.test(lower)) push("rot");
    if (/\bmagic|mana|arcane|spell\b/.test(lower)) push("magic");

    return found;
  },

  syncLegacyPlayerArrays(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const player = root.player;
    const traitState = player.traitState;
    let changes = 0;

    const nextActive = [];
    const nextPermanent = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < traitState.conditionIds.length; i += 1) {
      const record = traitState.byId[traitState.conditionIds[i]];
      if (!record || !record.identity) continue;

      if (record.state.mode === "permanent") {
        nextPermanent.push(record.identity.name);
      } else {
        nextActive.push(record.identity.name);
      }
    }

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(player.conditions.active, nextActive);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(player.conditions.permanent, nextPermanent);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(player.conditions.vulnerabilities, traitState.vulnerabilityTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(player.conditions.immunities, traitState.immunityTags);

    return changes;
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg, maxCount = 2) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const player = root.player || {};
    const traitState = player.traitState || {};
    const parts = [];

    const active = Array.isArray(player.conditions.active) ? player.conditions.active.slice(0, maxCount) : [];
    const permanent = Array.isArray(player.conditions.permanent) ? player.conditions.permanent.slice(0, maxCount) : [];
    const resistances = Array.isArray(traitState.resistanceTags) ? traitState.resistanceTags.slice(0, maxCount) : [];
    const adaptations = Array.isArray(traitState.adaptationTags) ? traitState.adaptationTags.slice(0, maxCount) : [];
    const sensitivities = Array.isArray(traitState.sensitivityTags) ? traitState.sensitivityTags.slice(0, maxCount) : [];

    if (active.length) parts.push("Active: " + active.join(", "));
    if (permanent.length) parts.push("Permanent: " + permanent.join(", "));
    if (resistances.length) parts.push("Resist: " + resistances.join(", "));
    if (adaptations.length) parts.push("Adapt: " + adaptations.join(", "));
    if (sensitivities.length) parts.push("Sensitive: " + sensitivities.join(", "));

    return parts.join(" | ");
  },

  makeEvidenceSnippet(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 140);
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(target, nextValues) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target)) return 0;
    const next = Array.isArray(nextValues) ? nextValues.slice() : [];

    let changed = 0;
    if (target.length !== next.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i += 1) {
        if (target[i] !== next[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    target.splice(0, target.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i += 1) {
      target.push(next[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushUniqueLocal(list, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;
    list.push(text);
    return 1;
  },

  removeValueLocal(list, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const idx = list.indexOf(value);
    if (idx === -1) return 0;
    list.splice(idx, 1);
    return 1;
  }
},

    // === CONSEQUENCE PARSER ===
ConsequenceParser: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    const pending = root.pending;

    if (!Array.isArray(pending.outputCandidates)) pending.outputCandidates = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.unresolvedConsequences)) pending.unresolvedConsequences = [];
    if (!pending.lastConsequenceParse || typeof pending.lastConsequenceParse !== "object" || Array.isArray(pending.lastConsequenceParse)) {
      pending.lastConsequenceParse = {};
    }

    return pending;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const pending = root.pending;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");

    pending.outputCandidates = [];
    pending.unresolvedConsequences = [];

    if (!raw.trim()) {
      return 0;
    }

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const candidates = this.dedupeCandidates(
      []
        .concat(this.extractPlayerCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractItemCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractWorldCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractActorCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractTimeCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractQuestCandidates(raw, root))
        .concat(this.extractReputationCandidates(raw, root))
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = candidates[i];
      if (Number(candidate.confidence || 0) >= 0.72) {
        pending.outputCandidates.push(candidate);
      } else {
        pending.unresolvedConsequences.push(candidate);
      }
    }

    pending.lastConsequenceParse = {
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      candidateCount: candidates.length,
      committedCount: pending.outputCandidates.length,
      unresolvedCount: pending.unresolvedConsequences.length
    };

    if (candidates.length && U && typeof U.logParse === "function") {
      U.logParse(
        "ConsequenceParser: " +
        pending.outputCandidates.length +
        " high-confidence / " +
        pending.unresolvedConsequences.length +
        " tentative"
      );
    }

    return candidates.length;
  },

  extractPlayerCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const raw = String(rawText || "");
  const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  const out = [];

  const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.76) => {
    out.push(this.makeCandidate("player", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
  };

  if (/\bno mp left\b|\bno mana left\b|\bout of mana\b|\bspent magic\b|\bmagic is gone\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "mp", state: "empty" }, 0.84);
  } else if (/\bmp is low\b|\bmana is low\b|\bmagic is low\b|\bnearly empty\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "mp", state: "low" }, 0.76);
  }

  if (/\bno ep left\b|\bfully exhausted\b|\bcan hardly stand\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "ep", state: "empty" }, 0.82);
  } else if (/\bep is dwindling\b|\benergy is dwindling\b|\bbreathing is ragged\b|\bexhausted\b|\bfatigued\b|\blegs shake\b|\bbreath gives out\b|\bwinded\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("resource_state", { resource: "ep", state: "low" }, 0.70);
  }

  if (/\bexhausted\b|\bfully exhausted\b|\bcan hardly stand\b|\blegs shake\b[^.!?]{0,40}\bexhausted\b|\bexhausted\b[^.!?]{0,40}\bclimb\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "exhausted" }, 0.78);
  }

  if (/\bfatigued\b|\bweary\b|\bworn out\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "fatigued" }, 0.72);
  }

  if (/\bburned\b|\bblistered\b|\bcharred\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "burned" }, 0.86);
  }

  if (/\bbleeding\b|\blosing blood\b|\bblood runs\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "bleeding" }, 0.84);
  }

  if (/\bpoisoned\b|\bvenom\b|\btoxins?\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "poisoned" }, 0.82);
  }

  if ((/\bshivering\b|\bchilled\b|\bfreezing\b|\bicy\b/.test(lower)) && /\brain\b|\bcold\b|\bfrost\b|\bice\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "chilled" }, 0.69);
  }

  if (/\bsoaked\b|\bdrenched\b|\brain-soaked\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_gain", { status: "soaked" }, 0.71);
  }

  if (/\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bburning\b|\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bburned\b|\bthe burn fades\b|\bthe fire burned out\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_loss", { status: "burned" }, 0.68);
  }

  if (/\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bbleeding\b|\bthe bleeding stops\b|\bblood loss ends\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_loss", { status: "bleeding" }, 0.80);
  }

  if (/\bno longer\b[^.!?]{0,24}\bexhausted\b|\bexhaustion fades\b|\brecovered from exhaustion\b|\bcatch your breath\b[^.!?]{0,30}\bsteady\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("status_loss", { status: "exhausted" }, 0.70);
  }

  if (/\barm\b[^.!?]{0,18}\bblistered\b|\barm\b[^.!?]{0,18}\bcharred\b|\bburned arm\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "arm", injuryTag: "burn" }, 0.87);
  }

  if (/\bthroat is raw\b|\bcough\b[^.!?]{0,18}\bpainful\b|\bsmoke\b/.test(lower)) {
    add("body_injury", { bodyPart: "throat", injuryTag: "smoke_irritation" }, 0.66);
  }

  return out;
},

  extractItemCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.76) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("item", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    const currencyGainPatterns = [
      /\b(?:gain|gains|gained|receive|receives|received|earn|earns|earned|find|finds|found|take|takes|took)\s+(\d+)\s+(gold|silver|copper)\b/gi,
      /\b(\d+)\s+(gold|silver|copper)\s+(?:coins?\s+)?(?:is|are|was|were)?\s*(?:given|awarded|paid)\b/gi
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < currencyGainPatterns.length; i += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = currencyGainPatterns[i].exec(raw))) {
        add("currency_change", {
          currency: String(match[2] || "").toLowerCase(),
          amount: Number(match[1] || 0),
          direction: "gain"
        }, 0.88);
      }
    }

    const currencyLossPatterns = [
      /\b(?:pay|pays|paid|spend|spends|spent|lose|loses|lost)\s+(\d+)\s+(gold|silver|copper)\b/gi
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < currencyLossPatterns.length; i += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = currencyLossPatterns[i].exec(raw))) {
        add("currency_change", {
          currency: String(match[2] || "").toLowerCase(),
          amount: Number(match[1] || 0),
          direction: "loss"
        }, 0.88);
      }
    }

    if (/\bsleeve is gone\b|\bshirt is torn\b|\bcloak is torn\b|\barmor is cracked\b|\bcharred cloth\b|\bbag tears\b/.test(raw.toLowerCase())) {
      add("item_damage", { itemLabel: "worn gear", damageTag: "damaged" }, 0.74);
    }

    if (/\bdrink(?:s|ing| drank)?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?(potion|tonic|elixir)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      const match = raw.match(/\bdrink(?:s|ing| drank)?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?(potion|tonic|elixir)\b/i);
      add("item_consumed", { itemLabel: this.normalizeShortLabel(match && match[1]) }, 0.82);
    }

    if (/\beat(?:s|ing| ate)?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?(ration|bread|stew|meat|herb)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      const match = raw.match(/\beat(?:s|ing| ate)?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?(ration|bread|stew|meat|herb)\b/i);
      add("item_consumed", { itemLabel: this.normalizeShortLabel(match && match[1]) }, 0.78);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractWorldCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.76) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("world", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    const targets = [
      "wall",
      "door",
      "gate",
      "gatehouse",
      "palisade",
      "granary",
      "warehouse",
      "tavern",
      "inn",
      "shop",
      "smithy",
      "bridge",
      "road",
      "trail",
      "cart",
      "stall",
      "wagon",
      "brush",
      "tree"
    ];

    const damageWords = [
      "burned",
      "scorched",
      "charred",
      "collapsed",
      "broken",
      "splintered",
      "wrecked",
      "blasted",
      "damaged",
      "crater"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < targets.length; i += 1) {
      const target = targets[i];
      const re = new RegExp(
        "\\b(" + damageWords.join("|") + ")\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b" + target + "\\b|\\b" + target + "\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b(" + damageWords.join("|") + ")\\b",
        "i"
      );

      const match = lower.match(re);
      if (match) {
        add("world_change", {
          targetLabel: target,
          changeTag: this.inferWorldChangeTag(match[0])
        }, 0.80);
      }
    }

    if (/\boccupied by\b|\bfull of\b|\bpacked with\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("occupancy_shift", { state: "occupied" }, 0.68);
    }

    if (/\babandoned\b|\bempty now\b|\bdeserted\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("occupancy_shift", { state: "abandoned" }, 0.68);
    }

    if (/\broad is blocked\b|\bbridge is out\b|\broute is cut off\b|\btrail is blocked\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("route_issue", { issue: "blocked" }, 0.82);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractActorCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.74) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("actor", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    const roles = [
      "smith",
      "guard",
      "barkeep",
      "merchant",
      "hunter",
      "kid",
      "child",
      "man",
      "woman"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < roles.length; i += 1) {
      const role = roles[i];

      if (new RegExp("\\b" + role + "\\b[^.!?]{0,30}\\b(dies|dead|killed)\\b", "i").test(raw)) {
        add("actor_state_change", { actorLabel: role, state: "dead" }, 0.84);
      }

      if (new RegExp("\\b" + role + "\\b[^.!?]{0,30}\\b(injured|wounded|burned|bleeding)\\b", "i").test(raw)) {
        add("actor_state_change", { actorLabel: role, state: "injured" }, 0.76);
      }

      if (new RegExp("\\b" + role + "\\b[^.!?]{0,30}\\b(fled|retreated|backs away|backed away|runs away|ran away)\\b", "i").test(raw)) {
        add("actor_state_change", { actorLabel: role, state: "fled" }, 0.78);
      }
    }

    if (/\b(?:trusts|believes|fears|hates|respects)\s+you\b/i.test(raw)) {
      const match = raw.match(/\b(?:trusts|believes|fears|hates|respects)\s+you\b/i);
      add("relationship_shift", { relationTag: this.normalizeShortLabel(match && match[0]) }, 0.67);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractTimeCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.78) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("time", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    let match = raw.match(/\b(\d+)\s+hours?\s+later\b/i);
    if (match) {
      add("time_advance", { amount: Number(match[1] || 0), unit: "hours" }, 0.90);
    }

    match = raw.match(/\bafter\s+(\d+)\s+hours?\b/i);
    if (match) {
      add("time_advance", { amount: Number(match[1] || 0), unit: "hours" }, 0.88);
    }

    match = raw.match(/\bafter\s+(\d+)\s+minutes?\b/i);
    if (match) {
      add("time_advance", { amount: Number(match[1] || 0), unit: "minutes" }, 0.86);
    }

    if (/\bovernight\b|\bthrough the night\b/.test(raw.toLowerCase())) {
      add("rest_completion", { label: "overnight" }, 0.84);
    }

    if (/\bnext morning\b|\bby dawn\b|\bat dawn\b/.test(raw.toLowerCase())) {
      add("time_advance", { label: "next_morning" }, 0.82);
    }

    if (/\btravel completed\b|\byou arrive\b|\byou reach\b/.test(raw.toLowerCase())) {
      add("travel_completion", { status: "arrived" }, 0.74);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractQuestCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.78) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("quest", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    if (/\b(?:accept|accepted|agree to|agreed to|take on|took on)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\b(quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("quest_status", { status: "accepted" }, 0.84);
    }

    if (/\b(?:complete|completed|finish|finished|fulfill|fulfilled)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\b(quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("quest_status", { status: "completed" }, 0.84);
    }

    if (/\b(?:fail|failed|abandon|abandoned)\b[^.!?]{0,30}\b(quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\b/.test(lower)) {
      add("quest_status", { status: "failed" }, 0.82);
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractReputationCandidates(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const add = (type, payload, confidence = 0.76) => {
      out.push(this.makeCandidate("reputation", type, payload, raw, confidence, root));
    };

    // Chunk 23 official reputation coverage:
    // Keep candidate extraction broad enough to notice player-facing social truth,
    // while ReputationSystem.shouldApplyReputationCandidate keeps the hard-save gate strict.
    const localGroup = "(?:locals|townsfolk|villagers|guards|patrons|crowd|people|citizens|merchants|traders|residents)";

    const negativeLocalRe = new RegExp(
      "\\b" + localGroup + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:wary|afraid|hostile|suspicious|fear|fears|fearful|hateful|angry|resentful|distrustful)\\b",
      "i"
    );

    const positiveLocalRe = new RegExp(
      "\\b" + localGroup + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:respectful|trusting|trusted|grateful|thankful|cheering|cheers|welcoming|welcome|welcomes|praised|praise|praises|approving|friendly)\\b",
      "i"
    );

    if (negativeLocalRe.test(lower)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "negative", subject: "local_group" }, 0.76);
    }

    if (positiveLocalRe.test(lower)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "positive", subject: "local_group" }, 0.76);
    }

    if (/\bpeople\s+(?:see|saw|view|viewed)\s+you\s+as\s+(?:trusted|respected|liked|beloved|welcome|welcomed|helpful|heroic|honest)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "positive", subject: "local_group" }, 0.78);
    }

    if (/\bpeople\s+(?:see|saw|view|viewed)\s+you\s+as\s+(?:feared|dangerous|threatening|hostile|cruel|notorious|infamous)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "negative", subject: "local_group" }, 0.78);
    }

    if (/\byou\s+(?:are|were|become|became)\s+(?:praised|respected|trusted|welcomed|liked|thanked)\s+by\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:locals|townsfolk|villagers|guards|patrons|crowd|people|citizens|merchants|traders|residents)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "positive", subject: "local_group" }, 0.78);
    }

    if (/\byou\s+(?:are|were|become|became)\s+(?:feared|hated|watched|avoided|suspected|resented)\s+by\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:locals|townsfolk|villagers|guards|patrons|crowd|people|citizens|merchants|traders|residents)\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "negative", subject: "local_group" }, 0.78);
    }

    if (/\b(?:rumors?|rumours?)\b|\bwhispers?\b|\bwhispered\b|\bword spreads\b|\bnews spreads\b|\bpeople are saying\b|\btalk spreads\b/i.test(raw)) {
      add("reputation_shift", { direction: "rumor_activity", subject: "public" }, 0.76);
    }

    return out;
  },

  makeCandidate(category, type, payload, evidence, confidence, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const rounded = U && typeof U.round === "function"
      ? U.round(confidence, 2)
      : Number(confidence || 0);

    return {
      id: U && typeof U.makeId === "function" ? U.makeId("candidate") : String(Date.now()),
      category: String(category || ""),
      type: String(type || ""),
      confidence: rounded,
      evidence: this.makeEvidenceSnippet(evidence),
      payload: payload && typeof payload === "object" ? payload : {},
      turn: root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0
    };
  },

  dedupeCandidates(candidates) {
    const out = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    for (let i = 0; i < (Array.isArray(candidates) ? candidates.length : 0); i += 1) {
      const candidate = candidates[i];
      if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") continue;

      const signature = this.buildCandidateSignature(candidate);
      if (!signature || seen.has(signature)) continue;

      seen.add(signature);
      out.push(candidate);
    }

    return out;
  },

  buildCandidateSignature(candidate) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return "";

    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    return [
      String(candidate.category || ""),
      String(candidate.type || ""),
      String(payload.resource || ""),
      String(payload.state || ""),
      String(payload.status || ""),
      String(payload.bodyPart || ""),
      String(payload.injuryTag || ""),
      String(payload.itemLabel || ""),
      String(payload.currency || ""),
      String(payload.direction || ""),
      String(payload.targetLabel || ""),
      String(payload.changeTag || ""),
      String(payload.actorLabel || ""),
      String(payload.amount || ""),
      String(payload.unit || ""),
      String(payload.label || ""),
      String(payload.subject || "")
    ].join("|").toLowerCase();
  },

  inferWorldChangeTag(text) {
    const lower = String(text || "").toLowerCase();
    if (/\bscorch|char|burn/.test(lower)) return "burn_damage";
    if (/\bcollapse|broken|splinter/.test(lower)) return "structural_damage";
    if (/\bcrater|blast/.test(lower)) return "blast_damage";
    if (/\bdamaged|wrecked/.test(lower)) return "general_damage";
    return "change";
  },

  normalizeShortLabel(value) {
    const text = String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (!text) return "";
    if (text.split(" ").length > 4) return "";
    return text;
  },

  makeEvidenceSnippet(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 180);
  }
},

    // === VALIDATION SYSTEM ===
ValidationSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.pending || typeof root.pending !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.pending)) {
      root.pending = {};
    }

    const pending = root.pending;

    if (!Array.isArray(pending.outputCandidates)) pending.outputCandidates = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.unresolvedConsequences)) pending.unresolvedConsequences = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences)) pending.validatedConsequences = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.tentativeConsequences)) pending.tentativeConsequences = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(pending.ignoredConsequences)) pending.ignoredConsequences = [];
    if (!pending.validationSummary || typeof pending.validationSummary !== "object" || Array.isArray(pending.validationSummary)) {
      pending.validationSummary = {};
    }

    return pending;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const pending = root.pending;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");

    pending.validatedConsequences = [];
    pending.tentativeConsequences = [];
    pending.ignoredConsequences = [];

    if (!raw.trim()) {
      return 0;
    }

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const source = []
      .concat(Array.isArray(pending.outputCandidates) ? pending.outputCandidates : [])
      .concat(Array.isArray(pending.unresolvedConsequences) ? pending.unresolvedConsequences : []);

    let processed = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < source.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = source[i];
      const result = this.validateCandidate(candidate, root);
      if (!result || !result.action) continue;

      processed += 1;

      if (result.action === "hard_save") {
        pending.validatedConsequences.push(result.candidate);
      } else if (result.action === "tentative") {
        pending.tentativeConsequences.push(result.candidate);
      } else {
        pending.ignoredConsequences.push(result.candidate);
      }
    }

    pending.validationSummary = {
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      parsed: source.length,
      validated: pending.validatedConsequences.length,
      tentative: pending.tentativeConsequences.length,
      ignored: pending.ignoredConsequences.length
    };

    if (U && typeof U.logParse === "function") {
      U.logParse(
        "Validation: " +
        pending.validatedConsequences.length +
        " hard / " +
        pending.tentativeConsequences.length +
        " tentative / " +
        pending.ignoredConsequences.length +
        " ignored"
      );
    }

    return processed;
  },

  validateCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") {
      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    const category = String(candidate.category || "");
    if (!category) {
      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    if (category === "player") return this.validatePlayerCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "item") return this.validateItemCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "world") return this.validateWorldCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "actor") return this.validateActorCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "time") return this.validateTimeCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "quest") return this.validateQuestCandidate(candidate, root);
    if (category === "reputation") return this.validateReputationCandidate(candidate, root);

    return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
  },

  validatePlayerCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const type = String(candidate.type || "");
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (type === "resource_state") {
      const resource = String(payload.resource || "");
      const stateName = String(payload.state || "");

      if (!/^(hp|mp|ep)$/.test(resource)) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }
      if (!/^(empty|low)$/.test(stateName)) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      if (confidence >= 0.68) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }

    if (type === "status_gain" || type === "status_loss") {
      const status = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.status);
      if (!status) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.status = status;

      if (confidence >= 0.66) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    if (type === "body_injury") {
      const bodyPart = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.bodyPart);
      const injuryTag = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.injuryTag);

      if (!injuryTag) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.bodyPart = bodyPart;
      nextCandidate.payload.injuryTag = injuryTag;

      if (confidence >= 0.72) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateItemCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const type = String(candidate.type || "");
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (type === "currency_change") {
      const currency = String(payload.currency || "");
      const amount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(payload.amount || 0)));
      const direction = String(payload.direction || "");

      if (!/^(gold|silver|copper)$/.test(currency) || amount <= 0 || !/^(gain|loss)$/.test(direction)) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.currency = currency;
      nextCandidate.payload.amount = amount;
      nextCandidate.payload.direction = direction;

      if (confidence >= 0.82) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    if (type === "item_consumed") {
      const itemLabel = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.itemLabel);
      if (!itemLabel) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.itemLabel = itemLabel;

      if (confidence >= 0.80) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    if (type === "item_damage") {
      const itemLabel = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.itemLabel);
      const damageTag = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.damageTag);
      if (!itemLabel && !damageTag) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.itemLabel = itemLabel;
      nextCandidate.payload.damageTag = damageTag || "damaged";

      if (confidence >= 0.84) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateWorldCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (candidate.type === "world_change") {
      const targetLabel = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.targetLabel);
      const changeTag = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.changeTag);
      if (!targetLabel || !changeTag) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.targetLabel = targetLabel;
      nextCandidate.payload.changeTag = changeTag;

      if (confidence >= 0.84) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      nextCandidate.payload.changeTag = "general_damage";
      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    if (candidate.type === "route_issue") {
      const issue = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.issue);
      if (!issue) {
        return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
      }

      const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
      nextCandidate.payload.issue = issue;

      if (confidence >= 0.82) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateActorCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (!this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.actorLabel) && !this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.relationTag)) {
      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    if (confidence >= 0.82) {
      return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateTimeCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (candidate.type === "time_advance") {
      const amount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(payload.amount || 0)));
      const unit = String(payload.unit || "");
      const label = String(payload.label || "");

      if ((amount > 0 && /^(minutes|hours)$/.test(unit)) || /^(next_morning)$/.test(label)) {
        const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);

        if (confidence >= 0.80) {
          return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
        }
        return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
      }

      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    if (candidate.type === "rest_completion") {
      const label = String(payload.label || "");
      if (label === "overnight") {
        if (confidence >= 0.82) {
          return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
        }
        return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
      }

      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    if (candidate.type === "travel_completion") {
      if (confidence >= 0.74) {
        return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
      }
      return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateQuestCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);
    if (confidence >= 0.82) {
      return { action: "hard_save", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }
    return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
  },

  validateReputationCandidate(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const type = String(candidate.type || "");
    const payload = candidate.payload || {};
    const confidence = Number(candidate.confidence || 0);

    if (type !== "reputation_shift") {
      return { action: "tentative", candidate: this.cloneCandidate(candidate) };
    }

    const direction = String(payload.direction || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    const subject = this.normalizeShortLabel(payload.subject || "local_group") || "local_group";

    if (!/^(positive|negative|rumor_activity)$/.test(direction)) {
      return { action: "ignore", candidate: null };
    }

    const nextCandidate = this.cloneCandidate(candidate);
    nextCandidate.payload.direction = direction;
    nextCandidate.payload.subject = subject;

    if (confidence >= 0.65) {
      return { action: "hard_save", candidate: nextCandidate };
    }

    return { action: "tentative", candidate: nextCandidate };
  },

  cloneCandidate(candidate) {
    const copy = {};
    const keys = Object.keys(candidate || {});
    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
      const key = keys[i];
      if (key === "payload" && candidate.payload && typeof candidate.payload === "object") {
        copy.payload = {};
        const payloadKeys = Object.keys(candidate.payload);
        for (let j = 0; j < payloadKeys.length; j += 1) {
          const payloadKey = payloadKeys[j];
          copy.payload[payloadKey] = candidate.payload[payloadKey];
        }
      } else {
        copy[key] = candidate[key];
      }
    }
    return copy;
  },

  normalizeShortLabel(value) {
    const text = String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (!text) return "";
    if (text.split(" ").length > 5) return "";
    return text;
  }
},

    // === SPECIALIZATION / MATCHUP SYSTEM ===
SpecializationSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.build || typeof root.build !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.build)) {
      root.build = {};
    }

    const build = root.build;

    if (!build.matchupProfile || typeof build.matchupProfile !== "object" || Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile)) {
      build.matchupProfile = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.weakAgainst = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst)) build.matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst = [];

    if (!build.specializationState || typeof build.specializationState !== "object" || Array.isArray(build.specializationState)) {
      build.specializationState = {};
    }

    const spec = build.specializationState;

    if (!Array.isArray(spec.profileTags)) spec.profileTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.profileSourceTags)) spec.profileSourceTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.currentTargetTags)) spec.currentTargetTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.currentEnvironmentTags)) spec.currentEnvironmentTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.currentSituationTags)) spec.currentSituationTags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(spec.recentAssessments)) spec.recentAssessments = [];

    if (typeof spec.currentEdge !== "string") spec.currentEdge = "none";
    if (typeof spec.currentPressure !== "string") spec.currentPressure = "none";
    if (typeof spec.currentSummary !== "string") spec.currentSummary = "";
    if (typeof spec.noveltyActive !== "boolean") spec.noveltyActive = false;
    if (typeof spec.lastAssessmentTurn !== "number") spec.lastAssessmentTurn = 0;

    return spec;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const build = root.build || {};
    const spec = build.specializationState || {};
    let changes = 0;

    const profile = this.derivePlayerProfile(root);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.profileTags, profile.tags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.profileSourceTags, profile.sourceTags);

    const targetTags = this.extractTargetTags(raw, root);
    const envTags = this.extractEnvironmentTags(raw, root);
    const situationTags = this.extractSituationTags(raw, root);
    const combatLikely = this.isCombatRelevant(raw, targetTags, situationTags);

    if (!combatLikely) {
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentTargetTags, []);
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentEnvironmentTags, []);
      changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentSituationTags, []);
      if (spec.currentEdge !== "none") {
        spec.currentEdge = "none";
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (spec.currentPressure !== "none") {
        spec.currentPressure = "none";
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (spec.currentSummary !== "") {
        spec.currentSummary = "";
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (spec.noveltyActive !== false) {
        spec.noveltyActive = false;
        changes += 1;
      }
      return changes;
    }

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentTargetTags, targetTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentEnvironmentTags, envTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(spec.currentSituationTags, situationTags);

    const assessment = this.assessMatchup(profile, targetTags, envTags, situationTags, raw, root);

    if (spec.currentEdge !== assessment.edge) {
      spec.currentEdge = assessment.edge;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (spec.currentPressure !== assessment.pressure) {
      spec.currentPressure = assessment.pressure;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (spec.currentSummary !== assessment.summary) {
      spec.currentSummary = assessment.summary;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (spec.noveltyActive !== assessment.noveltyActive) {
      spec.noveltyActive = assessment.noveltyActive;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    if (spec.lastAssessmentTurn !== turn) {
      spec.lastAssessmentTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    changes += this.applyAssessmentToProfile(assessment, build.matchupProfile, root);
    changes += this.pushRecentAssessment(spec.recentAssessments, {
      turn: turn,
      edge: assessment.edge,
      pressure: assessment.pressure,
      targets: targetTags.slice(0, 4),
      environment: envTags.slice(0, 4),
      noveltyActive: assessment.noveltyActive,
      summary: assessment.summary
    });

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
        U.markDirty("player", true, root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
        U.invalidatePackets(root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
        U.logEvent("Specialization/matchup assessment updated.");
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  derivePlayerProfile(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const player = root && root.player ? root.player : {};
    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const body = player.body || {};
    const appearance = player.appearance || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};
    const build = root && root.build ? root.build : {};
    const sourceTags = this.collectSourceTags(root);

    const profileTags = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < sourceTags.length; i += 1) {
      const mapped = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(sourceTags[i]);
      if (mapped) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, mapped);
    }

    const atk = Number(stats.atk || 0);
    const def = Number(stats.def || 0);
    const spd = Number(stats.spd || 0);
    const intl = Number(stats.intl || 0);
    const maxHp = Number(stats.maxHp || 0);
    const maxMp = Number(stats.maxMp || 0);

    if (atk >= intl + 2) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "martial");
    if (intl >= atk + 2 || maxMp >= 25) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "magic");
    if (spd >= Math.max(atk, def)) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "mobility");
    if (def >= Math.max(atk, intl)) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "defense");
    if (maxHp >= 110 || def >= 8) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, "durable");

    if (Array.isArray(body.bodyTags)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < body.bodyTags.length; i += 1) {
        const tag = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(body.bodyTags[i]);
        if (tag) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, tag);
      }
    }

    if (Array.isArray(appearance.visibleTraits)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < appearance.visibleTraits.length; i += 1) {
        const tag = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(appearance.visibleTraits[i]);
        if (tag) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, tag);
      }
    }

    const active = Array.isArray(conditions.active) ? conditions.active : [];
    for (let i = 0; i < active.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(active[i]);
      if (tag) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, tag);
    }

    if (build.matchupProfile) {
      const strong = Array.isArray(build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst) ? build.matchupProfile.strongAgainst : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < strong.length; i += 1) {
        const tag = this.normalizeCapabilityTag(strong[i]);
        if (tag) this.pushUniqueLocal(profileTags, tag);
      }
    }

    return {
      tags: profileTags,
      sourceTags: sourceTags
    };
  },

  collectSourceTags(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const build = root && root.build ? root.build : {};
    const out = [];
    const buckets = [
      build.originTags,
      build.combatTags,
      build.methodTags,
      build.resourceTags,
      build.defenseTags,
      build.specializationTags,
      build.temperamentTags,
      build.styleTags
    ];

    for (let b = 0; b < buckets.length; b += 1) {
      const list = Array.isArray(buckets[b]) ? buckets[b] : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, String(list[i] || ""));
      }
    }

    const abilities = root && root.abilities ? root.abilities : {};
    const byId = abilities.byId || {};
    const allIds = Array.isArray(abilities.allIds) ? abilities.allIds : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < allIds.length; i += 1) {
      const record = byId[allIds[i]];
      if (!record || typeof record !== "object") continue;

      const semanticTags = record.observation && Array.isArray(record.observation.semanticTags)
        ? record.observation.semanticTags
        : [];

      for (let j = 0; j < semanticTags.length; j += 1) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, String(semanticTags[j] || ""));
      }

      if (record.meaning && typeof record.meaning.intendedFunction === "string") {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, record.meaning.intendedFunction);
      }

      if (record.keys && Array.isArray(record.keys.keyPhrases)) {
        for (let j = 0; j < record.keys.keyPhrases.length; j += 1) {
          this.pushUniqueLocal(out, String(record.keys.keyPhrases[j] || ""));
        }
      }
    }

    const player = root && root.player ? root.player : {};
    const traitState = player.traitState || {};
    const traitBuckets = [
      traitState.resistanceTags,
      traitState.adaptationTags,
      traitState.sensitivityTags,
      traitState.immunityTags,
      traitState.vulnerabilityTags
    ];

    for (let b = 0; b < traitBuckets.length; b += 1) {
      const list = Array.isArray(traitBuckets[b]) ? traitBuckets[b] : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, String(list[i] || ""));
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  normalizeCapabilityTag(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[_-]+/g, " ")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (!lower) return "";

    if (/\bvery tall\b|\blarge frame\b|\bgiant\b|\btowering\b/.test(lower)) return "large_frame";
    if (/\bmobility\b|\bspeed\b|\bfast\b|\bagile\b|\bevasive\b|\bdash\b|\bstep\b/.test(lower)) return "mobility";
    if (/\bdefense\b|\bguard\b|\bshield\b|\bbrace\b|\btank\b|\bdurable\b|\bendure\b|\bresist\b/.test(lower)) return "defense";
    if (/\bmartial\b|\bblade\b|\bsword\b|\baxe\b|\bhammer\b|\bfist\b|\bmelee\b/.test(lower)) return "martial";
    if (/\bmagic\b|\barcane\b|\bmana\b|\bspell\b|\bcaster\b/.test(lower)) return "magic";
    if (/\branged\b|\barcher\b|\bbow\b|\bcrossbow\b|\bshot\b|\bprojectile\b/.test(lower)) return "ranged";
    if (/\bstealth\b|\bhidden\b|\bsilent\b|\bassassin\b/.test(lower)) return "stealth";
    if (/\bdetection\b|\btrack\b|\bsense\b|\bscout\b|\binspect\b|\bsurvey\b/.test(lower)) return "detection";
    if (/\bpierce\b|\bspear\b|\blance\b|\bthrust\b/.test(lower)) return "pierce";
    if (/\bfire\b|\bflame\b|\bember\b|\bburn\b/.test(lower)) return "fire";
    if (/\blightning\b|\bstorm\b|\bshock\b|\bthunder\b/.test(lower)) return "lightning";
    if (/\bice\b|\bfrost\b|\bcold\b/.test(lower)) return "cold";
    if (/\bshadow\b|\bdark\b|\bveil\b/.test(lower)) return "shadow";
    if (/\blight\b|\bholy\b|\bradiant\b/.test(lower)) return "light";
    if (/\bwater\b|\btide\b|\briver\b|\bsea\b|\baquatic\b/.test(lower)) return "water";
    if (/\bpoison\b|\bvenom\b|\btoxic\b/.test(lower)) return "poison";
    if (/\brot\b|\bdecay\b|\bwither\b|\bblight\b/.test(lower)) return "rot";
    if (/\brecovery\b|\bheal\b|\brestore\b|\bregenerat/.test(lower)) return "recovery";
    if (/\bburned\b|\bbleeding\b|\bpoisoned\b|\bchilled\b|\bsoaked\b|\bfatigued\b/.test(lower)) return "strained";

    return "";
  },

  extractTargetTags(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const checks = [
      { re: /\bundead\b|\bskeleton\b|\bzombie\b|\bwraith\b|\brevenant\b|\bghoul\b/, tag: "undead" },
      { re: /\bdragon\b|\bdrake\b|\bwyvern\b|\bdraconic\b/, tag: "draconic" },
      { re: /\bbeast\b|\bpredator\b|\bwolf\b|\bboar\b|\bbear\b|\bhound\b/, tag: "beast" },
      { re: /\barmored\b|\bplate\b|\bmail\b|\bshielded\b|\bironclad\b/, tag: "armored" },
      { re: /\bmage\b|\bwizard\b|\bsorcerer\b|\bspellcaster\b|\bshaman\b|\bwarlock\b/, tag: "caster" },
      { re: /\barcher\b|\bbowman\b|\bcrossbowman\b|\bmarksman\b|\branged\b/, tag: "ranged" },
      { re: /\bwinged\b|\bflying\b|\bairborne\b|\bharpy\b|\boverhead\b/, tag: "flying" },
      { re: /\bhidden\b|\bconcealed\b|\binvisible\b|\bunseen\b|\bstealth\b|\bcamouflaged\b/, tag: "hidden" },
      { re: /\bpack\b|\bgroup\b|\bswarm\b|\bseveral\b|\bmultiple\b|\bcrowd\b/, tag: "pack" },
      { re: /\bgiant\b|\bhuge\b|\bmassive\b|\bogre\b|\btroll\b|\btowering\b|\bcolossal\b/, tag: "large" },
      { re: /\baquatic\b|\bmerfolk\b|\bwaterborne\b|\bsea[- ]?born\b|\bswimming\b/, tag: "aquatic" },
      { re: /\bfast\b|\bswift\b|\bquick\b|\bagile\b|\bnimble\b|\bblur\b/, tag: "fast" },
      { re: /\bbrute\b|\bhulking\b|\bheavy\b|\bthickset\b/, tag: "brute" }
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < checks.length; i += 1) {
      if (checks[i].re.test(lower)) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, checks[i].tag);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  extractEnvironmentTags(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    const scene = root && root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : {};
    const sceneName = String(scene.displayName || "").toLowerCase();
    const sceneType = String(scene.sceneType || "").toLowerCase();

    if (sceneType === "interior" || sceneType === "room" || /\btavern\b|\binn\b|\bworkshop\b|\bsmithy\b|\bhall\b|\broom\b/.test(sceneName)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "interior");
    }
    if (sceneType === "outdoor" || /\bsquare\b|\blane\b|\broad\b|\btrail\b|\bbridge\b|\bgate\b/.test(sceneName)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "open");
    }

    if (/\bcramped\b|\bnarrow\b|\bconfined\b|\blow ceiling\b|\bdoorway\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "cramped");
    if (/\brain\b|\bstorm\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "rain");
    if (/\bmud\b|\bslippery\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "mud");
    if (/\bdark\b|\bnight\b|\bshadowed\b|\bgloom\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "darkness");
    if (/\bstream\b|\briver\b|\bwater\b|\bflood\b|\bsoaked\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "water");
    if (/\bfire\b|\bflame\b|\bember\b|\bsmoke\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "fire");
    if (/\bcold\b|\bfrost\b|\bice\b|\bfreezing\b|\bchill\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "cold");
    if (/\bcliff\b|\bhigh ground\b|\brooftop\b|\bbalcony\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "high_ground");
    if (/\bcover\b|\bbehind the cart\b|\bdoorframe\b|\bbarrel\b|\bwall\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "cover");

    return out;
  },

  extractSituationTags(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    if (/\bunfamiliar\b|\bstrange style\b|\bodd angle\b|\bunreadable\b|\bnot used to\b|\bcatches? .* off guard\b|\bunexpected\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "novelty_advantage");
    }

    if (/\bprepared\b|\bbraced\b|\bready\b|\bset stance\b|\bsteady\b|\baimed\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "prepared");
    }

    if (/\boutnumbered\b|\bsurrounded\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "outnumbered");
    }

    if (/\bopening\b|\bvulnerable\b|\bexposed\b|\breeling\b|\bstaggered\b|\boff balance\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "target_exposed");
    }

    if (/\boverwhelming\b|\bno opening\b|\bcan.?t keep up\b|\boutclassed\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "overwhelmed");
    }

    if (/\bambush\b|\bfrom behind\b|\bsurprise\b/.test(lower)) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "surprise");
    }

    return out;
  },

  isCombatRelevant(rawText, targetTags, situationTags) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();

    if (Array.isArray(situationTags) && situationTags.length) return true;
    if (Array.isArray(targetTags) && targetTags.length && /\battack\b|\bfight\b|\bstrike\b|\bweapon\b|\bspell\b|\bcombat\b|\bduel\b|\bbattle\b|\bhostile\b|\bdefend\b|\bbrace\b|\bpressure\b|\bthreat\b/.test(lower)) {
      return true;
    }

    if (/\battack\b|\bfight\b|\bstrike\b|\bslash\b|\bstab\b|\bshoot\b|\bblast\b|\bspell\b|\bduel\b|\bbattle\b|\bhostile\b|\bthreat\b/.test(lower)) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  hasPlayerPressureFlags(root = state.aidrpg) {
