    const player = root && root.player ? root.player : {};
    const stats = player.stats || {};
    const conditions = player.conditions || {};

    const hp = Number(stats.hp || 0);
    const maxHp = Math.max(1, Number(stats.maxHp || 1));
    const mp = Number(stats.mp || 0);
    const maxMp = Math.max(1, Number(stats.maxMp || 1));
    const ep = Number(stats.ep || 0);
    const maxEp = Math.max(1, Number(stats.maxEp || 1));

    if (hp / maxHp <= 0.35) return true;
    if (mp / maxMp <= 0.25) return true;
    if (ep / maxEp <= 0.25) return true;

    const active = Array.isArray(conditions.active) ? conditions.active : [];
    for (let i = 0; i < active.length; i += 1) {
      const lower = String(active[i] || "").toLowerCase();
      if (/burned|bleeding|poisoned|infected|fatigued|chilled|soaked/.test(lower)) {
        return true;
      }
    }

    return false;
  },

  assessMatchup(profile, targetTags, envTags, situationTags, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const lower = String(rawText || "").toLowerCase();
    const profileTags = Array.isArray(profile && profile.tags) ? profile.tags : [];
    const hasTag = (tag) => profileTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const targetHas = (tag) => Array.isArray(targetTags) && targetTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const envHas = (tag) => Array.isArray(envTags) && envTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const situHas = (tag) => Array.isArray(situationTags) && situationTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    let edgeScore = 0;
    let pressureScore = 0;

    if (this.hasPlayerPressureFlags(root)) {
      pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (/\bopening\b|\bvulnerable\b|\bexposed\b|\brecoils\b|\bstaggers?\b|\boff balance\b|\bcan.?t track\b|\bhesitates?\b/.test(lower)) {
      edgeScore += 2;
    }

    if (/\boverwhelming\b|\bshrugs off\b|\boutclassed\b|\bno opening\b|\btoo fast\b|\btoo strong\b|\bcan.?t keep up\b|\bpressure mounts\b/.test(lower)) {
      pressureScore += 2;
    }

    if (targetHas("armored")) {
      if (hasTag("magic") || hasTag("lightning") || hasTag("pierce")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("undead")) {
      if (hasTag("light") || hasTag("fire")) edgeScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("draconic") || targetHas("large")) {
      if (hasTag("ranged") || hasTag("magic") || hasTag("mobility")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("pack")) {
      if (hasTag("fire") || hasTag("lightning") || hasTag("large_frame")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("hidden")) {
      if (hasTag("detection") || hasTag("fire") || hasTag("lightning")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("ranged")) {
      if (hasTag("mobility") || hasTag("stealth")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("caster")) {
      if (hasTag("mobility") || hasTag("detection")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("flying")) {
      if (hasTag("ranged") || hasTag("magic") || hasTag("lightning")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("aquatic") && envHas("water")) {
      if (hasTag("water") || hasTag("ranged") || hasTag("magic")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (targetHas("fast")) {
      if (hasTag("detection") || hasTag("mobility") || hasTag("defense")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("cramped") && hasTag("large_frame")) {
      pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("open") && (hasTag("ranged") || hasTag("mobility"))) {
      edgeScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("darkness")) {
      if (hasTag("detection") || hasTag("shadow")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("fire")) {
      if (hasTag("fire")) edgeScore += 1;
      else pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("cold")) {
      if (hasTag("cold")) edgeScore += 1;
      else if (this.hasPlayerPressureFlags(root)) pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (envHas("water")) {
      if (hasTag("water")) edgeScore += 1;
      else if (this.hasPlayerPressureFlags(root)) pressureScore += 1;
    }

    if (situHas("novelty_advantage")) edgeScore += 1;
    if (situHas("target_exposed")) edgeScore += 1;
    if (situHas("prepared")) edgeScore += 1;
    if (situHas("outnumbered")) pressureScore += 1;
    if (situHas("overwhelmed")) pressureScore += 2;

    const diff = edgeScore - pressureScore;

    let edge = "even";
    if (diff >= 2) edge = "favorable";
    else if (diff === 1) edge = "slight_favor";
    else if (diff === -1) edge = "pressured";
    else if (diff <= -2) edge = "unfavorable";

    let pressure = "low";
    if (pressureScore >= 4) pressure = "overwhelming";
    else if (pressureScore >= 2) pressure = "high";
    else if (pressureScore >= 1) pressure = "moderate";

    const noveltyActive = situHas("novelty_advantage");
    const parts = [
      "Matchup: " + edge,
      "Pressure: " + pressure
    ];

    if (Array.isArray(targetTags) && targetTags.length) {
      parts.push("Target: " + targetTags.slice(0, 2).join(", "));
    }

    return {
      edge: edge,
      pressure: pressure,
      noveltyActive: noveltyActive,
      summary: parts.join(" | ")
    };
  },

  applyAssessmentToProfile(assessment, matchupProfile, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!assessment || !matchupProfile || typeof matchupProfile !== "object") return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    const spec = root && root.build ? root.build.specializationState || {} : {};
    const targetTags = Array.isArray(spec.currentTargetTags) ? spec.currentTargetTags : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < targetTags.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = String(targetTags[i] || "").trim();
      if (!tag) continue;

      if (assessment.edge === "favorable" || assessment.edge === "slight_favor") {
        changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(matchupProfile.strongAgainst, tag);
      }

      if (assessment.edge === "unfavorable" || assessment.pressure === "high" || assessment.pressure === "overwhelming") {
        changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(matchupProfile.weakAgainst, tag);
      }

      if (assessment.noveltyActive) {
        changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(matchupProfile.unfamiliarAgainst, tag);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  getRelevantContextHint(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const build = root.build || {};
    const spec = build.specializationState || {};
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    if (!spec.currentSummary) return "";
    if (typeof spec.lastAssessmentTurn === "number" && turn - spec.lastAssessmentTurn > 1) {
      return "";
    }

    const parts = [
      "Matchup: " + String(spec.currentEdge || "none"),
      "Pressure: " + String(spec.currentPressure || "none")
    ];

    if (Array.isArray(spec.currentTargetTags) && spec.currentTargetTags.length) {
      parts.push("Target: " + spec.currentTargetTags.slice(0, 2).join(", "));
    }

    if (mode === "wide" && Array.isArray(spec.currentEnvironmentTags) && spec.currentEnvironmentTags.length) {
      parts.push("Terrain: " + spec.currentEnvironmentTags.slice(0, 2).join(", "));
    }

    if (spec.noveltyActive === true) {
      parts.push("Novelty edge active");
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(target, nextValues) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target)) return 0;
    const next = Array.isArray(nextValues) ? nextValues.slice() : [];

    let changed = 0;
    if (target.length !== next.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i += 1) {
        if (target[i] !== next[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    target.splice(0, target.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i += 1) {
      target.push(next[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushUniqueLocal(list, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;
    list.push(text);
    return 1;
  },

  pushRecentAssessment(list, record) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !record || typeof record !== "object") return 0;

    const last = list.length ? list[list.length - 1] : null;
    const nextSummary = String(record.summary || "");

    if (last && String(last.summary || "") === nextSummary) {
      return 0;
    }

    list.push(record);

    while (list.length > 8) {
      list.shift();
    }

    return 1;
  }
},

    // === ACTOR PROFILE GENERATION SYSTEM ===
ActorProfileSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.actors || typeof root.actors !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.actors)) {
      root.actors = {};
    }

    const actors = root.actors;

    if (!actors.byId || typeof actors.byId !== "object" || Array.isArray(actors.byId)) {
      actors.byId = {};
    }
    if (!actors.bySceneKey || typeof actors.bySceneKey !== "object" || Array.isArray(actors.bySceneKey)) {
      actors.bySceneKey = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.allIds)) actors.allIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.localIds)) actors.localIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.pendingGeneration)) actors.pendingGeneration = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(actors.recentUpdates)) actors.recentUpdates = [];
    if (!actors.sightingsByKey || typeof actors.sightingsByKey !== "object" || Array.isArray(actors.sightingsByKey)) {
      actors.sightingsByKey = {};
    }

    return actors;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    let changes = 0;

    changes += this.syncSceneActors(root);
    changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
        const actors = root.actors || {};
        const localIds = Array.isArray(actors.localIds) ? actors.localIds : [];
        for (let i = 0; i < localIds.length; i += 1) {
          U.markDirty("actors", localIds[i], root);
        }
      }

      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
        U.invalidatePackets(root);
      }

      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
        U.logEvent("Actor profiles updated.");
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  syncSceneActors(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : null;
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    if (!scene || !Array.isArray(scene.localActorIds) || !scene.localActorsById || typeof scene.localActorsById !== "object") {
      return this.replaceSimpleArray(actors.localIds, []);
    }

    const nextLocalIds = [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < scene.localActorIds.length; i += 1) {
      const sceneActorId = String(scene.localActorIds[i] || "").trim();
      if (!sceneActorId) continue;

      const rawActor = scene.localActorsById[sceneActorId];
      const candidate = this.buildCandidateFromScene(sceneActorId, rawActor, root);
      if (!candidate) continue;

      const record = this.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
      if (!record) continue;

      changes += this.applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, record, root);
      nextLocalIds.push(record.identity.id);
    }

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(actors.localIds, nextLocalIds);
    return changes;
  },

  buildCandidateFromScene(sceneActorId, rawActor, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;

    let displayName = "";
    if (rawActor && typeof rawActor === "object") {
      displayName =
        String(rawActor.displayName || rawActor.name || rawActor.label || rawActor.id || sceneActorId || "").trim();
    } else {
      displayName = String(sceneActorId || "").trim();
    }

    if (!displayName) return null;

    const roleTags = this.inferRoleTags(displayName);
    const rankTags = this.inferRankTags(displayName);
    const category = this.inferCategory(displayName);
    const sceneKey = U.toKey(displayName || sceneActorId);

    if (!sceneKey) return null;

    const importanceScore = this.computeImportanceScore(displayName, roleTags, rankTags, root);

    return {
      sceneKey: sceneKey,
      displayName: displayName,
      roleTags: roleTags,
      rankTags: rankTags,
      category: category,
      importanceScore: importanceScore,
      persistent: importanceScore >= 2,
      major: importanceScore >= 4
    };
  },

  findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const sceneKey = String(candidate.sceneKey || "").trim();
    if (!sceneKey) return null;

    const existingId = actors.bySceneKey && actors.bySceneKey[sceneKey] ? actors.bySceneKey[sceneKey] : "";
    if (existingId && actors.byId && actors.byId[existingId]) {
      return actors.byId[existingId];
    }

    const actorId = U.makeId("actor");
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    const record = {
      identity: {
        id: actorId,
        name: String(candidate.displayName || ""),
        sceneKey: sceneKey,
        category: String(candidate.category || "humanoid")
      },
      roleTags: [],
      rankTags: [],
      factionRefs: [],
      levelProfile: {
        resolvedLevel: 1,
        bandMin: 1,
        bandMax: 3
      },
      stats: {
        hp: 0,
        maxHp: 0,
        atk: 0,
        def: 0,
        spd: 0,
        intl: 0
      },
      buildTags: [],
      abilities: [],
      equipment: [],
      relationshipToPlayer: 0,
      knownState: {
        alive: true,
        injured: [],
        mood: "",
        locationRef: "",
        lastSeenTurn: turn
      },
      importance: {
        persistent: false,
        major: false,
        score: 0,
        sightings: 0
      }
    };

    actors.byId[actorId] = record;
    actors.bySceneKey[sceneKey] = actorId;
    actors.allIds.push(actorId);

    return record;
  },

  applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, record, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!candidate || !record) return 0;

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (record.identity.name !== candidate.displayName) {
      record.identity.name = candidate.displayName;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.identity.category !== candidate.category) {
      record.identity.category = candidate.category;
      changes += 1;
    }

    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.roleTags, candidate.roleTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.rankTags, candidate.rankTags);

    const levelProfile = this.resolveLevelBand(candidate.roleTags, candidate.rankTags, candidate.category, root);
    if (record.levelProfile.bandMin !== levelProfile.bandMin) {
      record.levelProfile.bandMin = levelProfile.bandMin;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (record.levelProfile.bandMax !== levelProfile.bandMax) {
      record.levelProfile.bandMax = levelProfile.bandMax;
      changes += 1;
    }
    if (record.levelProfile.resolvedLevel !== levelProfile.resolvedLevel) {
      record.levelProfile.resolvedLevel = levelProfile.resolvedLevel;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const nextBuildTags = this.inferBuildTags(candidate.roleTags, candidate.rankTags, candidate.category);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.buildTags, nextBuildTags);

    const nextAbilities = this.inferLikelyAbilities(nextBuildTags, candidate.roleTags, candidate.rankTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.abilities, nextAbilities);

    const nextEquipment = this.inferLikelyEquipment(candidate.roleTags, nextBuildTags);
    changes += this.replaceSimpleArray(record.equipment, nextEquipment);

    const nextStats = this.deriveStats(levelProfile.resolvedLevel, nextBuildTags, candidate.category);
    changes += this.applyStatsObject(record.stats, nextStats);

    if (record.knownState.lastSeenTurn !== turn) {
      record.knownState.lastSeenTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const player = root.player || {};
    const locationRef = String(player.locationRef || "").trim();
    if (record.knownState.locationRef !== locationRef) {
      record.knownState.locationRef = locationRef;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const actors = root.actors || {};
    if (!actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey] || typeof actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey] !== "number") {
      actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey] = 0;
    }

    actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey] += 1;
    const sightings = actors.sightingsByKey[candidate.sceneKey];

    const nextScore = Math.max(Number(candidate.importanceScore || 0), sightings >= 3 ? 2 : 0);
    if (record.importance.score !== nextScore) {
      record.importance.score = nextScore;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (record.importance.sightings !== sightings) {
      record.importance.sightings = sightings;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const nextPersistent = candidate.persistent || sightings >= 3;
    if (record.importance.persistent !== nextPersistent) {
      record.importance.persistent = nextPersistent;
      changes += 1;
    }

    const nextMajor = candidate.major || sightings >= 5;
    if (record.importance.major !== nextMajor) {
      record.importance.major = nextMajor;
      changes += 1;
    }

    changes += this.pushRecentUpdate(root.actors.recentUpdates, {
      turn: turn,
      actorId: record.identity.id,
      name: record.identity.name
    });

    return changes;
  },

  applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.pending || {};
    const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = validated[i];
      if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "actor" || candidate._actorApplied === true) {
        continue;
      }

      const payload = candidate.payload || {};
      const actorLabel = String(payload.actorLabel || "").trim();
      if (!actorLabel) {
        candidate._actorApplied = true;
        continue;
      }

      const matchedIds = this.findMatchingActorIds(actorLabel, root);
      for (let j = 0; j < matchedIds.length; j += 1) {
        const record = root.actors && root.actors.byId ? root.actors.byId[matchedIds[j]] : null;
        if (!record) continue;

        if (candidate.type === "actor_state_change") {
          const nextState = String(payload.state || "").trim().toLowerCase();

          if (nextState === "dead" && record.knownState.alive !== false) {
            record.knownState.alive = false;
            changes += 1;
          }

          if (nextState === "injured") {
            changes += this.pushUniqueLocal(record.knownState.injured, "injured");
          }

          if (nextState === "fled" && record.knownState.mood !== "fled") {
            record.knownState.mood = "fled";
            changes += 1;
          }
        }

        if (candidate.type === "relationship_shift") {
          const relationTag = String(payload.relationTag || "").toLowerCase();
          let delta = 0;
          if (relationTag.indexOf("trusts") !== -1 || relationTag.indexOf("respects") !== -1) delta = 1;
          if (relationTag.indexOf("fears") !== -1 || relationTag.indexOf("hates") !== -1) delta = -1;

          if (delta !== 0) {
            const nextRel = Number(record.relationshipToPlayer || 0) + delta;
            if (record.relationshipToPlayer !== nextRel) {
              record.relationshipToPlayer = nextRel;
              changes += 1;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      candidate._actorApplied = true;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  findMatchingActorIds(actorLabel, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const byId = actors.byId || {};
    const label = String(actorLabel || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!label) return [];

    const out = [];
    const ids = Array.isArray(actors.allIds) ? actors.allIds : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
      const record = byId[ids[i]];
      if (!record) continue;

      const name = String(record.identity && record.identity.name || "").toLowerCase();
      if (name === label || name.indexOf(label) !== -1 || label.indexOf(name) !== -1) {
        out.push(ids[i]);
        continue;
      }

      const roleTags = Array.isArray(record.roleTags) ? record.roleTags : [];
      for (let j = 0; j < roleTags.length; j += 1) {
        if (String(roleTags[j] || "").toLowerCase() === label) {
          out.push(ids[i]);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  computeImportanceScore(displayName, roleTags, rankTags, root = state.aidrpg) {
    let score = 0;
    const name = String(displayName || "");

    if (/\b[A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)+\b/.test(name)) {
      score += 2;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(rankTags) && rankTags.length) {
      score += 2;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(roleTags)) {
      const importantRoles = new Set([
        "guard",
        "captain",
        "commander",
        "guildmaster",
        "adventurer",
        "hunter",
        "mage",
        "priest",
        "merchant",
        "barkeep",
        "blacksmith",
        "smith",
        "knight",
        "noble",
        "bandit",
        "soldier"
      ]);

      for (let i = 0; i < roleTags.length; i += 1) {
        if (importantRoles.has(String(roleTags[i] || "").toLowerCase())) {
          score += 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    return score;
  },

  inferRoleTags(label) {
    const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];
    const checks = [
      "guard",
      "captain",
      "commander",
      "guildmaster",
      "adventurer",
      "hunter",
      "merchant",
      "barkeep",
      "innkeeper",
      "blacksmith",
      "smith",
      "priest",
      "mage",
      "wizard",
      "knight",
      "soldier",
      "bandit",
      "noble",
      "scout",
      "archer",
      "beast",
      "monster",
      "undead",
      "wolf",
      "boar",
      "dragon"
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < checks.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = checks[i];
      if (new RegExp("\\b" + tag.replace(/\s+/g, "\\s+") + "\\b", "i").test(lower)) {
        this.pushUniqueLocal(out, tag);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  inferRankTags(label) {
    const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();
    const out = [];

    if (/\ba-rank\b|\bs-rank\b|\bhigh rank\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "elite");
    if (/\bveteran\b|\belite\b|\bseasoned\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "veteran");
    if (/\bcaptain\b|\bcommander\b|\bguildmaster\b|\blord\b|\blady\b|\bnoble\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "leader");
    if (/\bapprentice\b|\bnovice\b|\binitiate\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "novice");
    if (/\bchild\b|\bboy\b|\bgirl\b/.test(lower)) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "child");

    return out;
  },

  inferCategory(label) {
    const lower = String(label || "").toLowerCase();

    if (/\bundead\b|\bskeleton\b|\bzombie\b|\bwraith\b/.test(lower)) return "undead";
    if (/\bdragon\b|\bdrake\b|\bwyvern\b/.test(lower)) return "draconic";
    if (/\bwolf\b|\bboar\b|\bbear\b|\bhound\b|\bbeast\b/.test(lower)) return "beast";
    if (/\bmonster\b|\bogre\b|\btroll\b|\bgoblin\b/.test(lower)) return "monster";
    return "humanoid";
  },

  resolveLevelBand(roleTags, rankTags, category, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const playerLevel =
      root && root.player && root.player.levelState && typeof root.player.levelState.level === "number"
        ? root.player.levelState.level
        : 1;

    let bandMin = 1;
    let bandMax = Math.max(3, playerLevel + 2);

    const hasRole = (tag) => Array.isArray(roleTags) && roleTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const hasRank = (tag) => Array.isArray(rankTags) && rankTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasRole("guard") || hasRole("hunter") || hasRole("soldier") || hasRole("adventurer")) {
      bandMin = Math.max(1, playerLevel);
      bandMax = Math.max(bandMin + 2, playerLevel + 5);
    }

    if (hasRole("merchant") || hasRole("barkeep") || hasRole("innkeeper") || hasRole("blacksmith") || hasRole("smith")) {
      bandMin = Math.max(1, Math.max(1, playerLevel - 1));
      bandMax = Math.max(bandMin + 2, playerLevel + 3);
    }

    if (hasRole("mage") || hasRole("priest") || hasRole("knight")) {
      bandMin = Math.max(2, playerLevel + 1);
      bandMax = Math.max(bandMin + 2, playerLevel + 6);
    }

    if (category === "beast" || category === "monster" || category === "undead" || category === "draconic") {
      bandMin = Math.max(1, playerLevel - 1);
      bandMax = Math.max(bandMin + 3, playerLevel + 5);
    }

    if (hasRank("veteran")) {
      bandMin += 2;
      bandMax += 3;
    }

    if (hasRank("leader")) {
      bandMin += 4;
      bandMax += 6;
    }

    if (hasRank("elite")) {
      bandMin += 5;
      bandMax += 8;
    }

    if (hasRank("novice")) {
      bandMax = Math.max(2, bandMax - 2);
      bandMin = Math.min(bandMin, Math.max(1, bandMax - 1));
    }

    if (hasRank("child")) {
      bandMin = 1;
      bandMax = Math.min(2, bandMax);
    }

    if (bandMin > bandMax) bandMax = bandMin;
    const resolvedLevel = Math.max(bandMin, Math.floor((bandMin + bandMax) / 2));

    return {
      bandMin: bandMin,
      bandMax: bandMax,
      resolvedLevel: resolvedLevel
    };
  },

  inferBuildTags(roleTags, rankTags, category) {
    const out = [];
    const hasRole = (tag) => Array.isArray(roleTags) && roleTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasRole("guard") || hasRole("knight") || hasRole("soldier")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "martial");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "defense");
    }

    if (hasRole("hunter") || hasRole("archer") || hasRole("scout")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "ranged");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "detection");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "mobility");
    }

    if (hasRole("mage") || hasRole("wizard") || hasRole("priest")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "magic");
    }

    if (hasRole("merchant") || hasRole("barkeep") || hasRole("innkeeper") || hasRole("noble")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "social");
    }

    if (hasRole("blacksmith") || hasRole("smith")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "durable");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "martial");
    }

    if (category === "beast") {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "mobility");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "brute");
    }

    if (category === "monster" || category === "undead" || category === "draconic") {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "brute");
    }

    if (Array.isArray(rankTags) && rankTags.indexOf("leader") !== -1) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "command");
    }

    if (!out.length) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "generalist");
    }

    return out;
  },

  inferLikelyAbilities(buildTags, roleTags, rankTags) {
    const out = [];
    const hasBuild = (tag) => Array.isArray(buildTags) && buildTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasBuild("martial")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "weapon discipline");
    if (hasBuild("defense")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "guarded stance");
    if (hasBuild("ranged")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "aimed shot");
    if (hasBuild("detection")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "tracking sense");
    if (hasBuild("mobility")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "quick reposition");
    if (hasBuild("magic")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "basic spellcasting");
    if (hasBuild("command")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "command presence");
    if (hasBuild("social")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "social leverage");
    if (hasBuild("brute")) this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "overwhelming force");

    if (!out.length) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "practical action");
    }

    return out.slice(0, 4);
  },

  inferLikelyEquipment(roleTags, buildTags) {
    const out = [];
    const hasRole = (tag) => Array.isArray(roleTags) && roleTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;
    const hasBuild = (tag) => Array.isArray(buildTags) && buildTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasRole("guard") || hasRole("soldier")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "shield");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "sidearm");
    }
    if (hasRole("hunter") || hasRole("archer")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "bow");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "knife");
    }
    if (hasRole("blacksmith") || hasRole("smith")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "hammer");
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "heavy apron");
    }
    if (hasRole("barkeep") || hasRole("innkeeper")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "work clothes");
    }
    if (hasRole("merchant")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "coin purse");
    }
    if (hasBuild("magic")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "focus");
    }
    if (hasBuild("brute")) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "heavy weapon");
    }

    if (!out.length) {
      this.pushUniqueLocal(out, "ordinary gear");
    }

    return out.slice(0, 4);
  },

  deriveStats(level, buildTags, category) {
    const lvl = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(level || 1)));
    const stats = {
      hp: 70 + (lvl * 8),
      maxHp: 70 + (lvl * 8),
      atk: 4 + lvl,
      def: 4 + lvl,
      spd: 4 + lvl,
      intl: 4 + lvl
    };

    const hasBuild = (tag) => Array.isArray(buildTags) && buildTags.indexOf(tag) !== -1;

    if (hasBuild("martial")) stats.atk += 2;
    if (hasBuild("defense")) stats.def += 2;
    if (hasBuild("mobility")) stats.spd += 2;
    if (hasBuild("magic")) stats.intl += 3;
    if (hasBuild("durable") || hasBuild("brute")) {
      stats.hp += 16;
      stats.maxHp += 16;
    }

    if (category === "draconic" || category === "monster") {
      stats.hp += 10;
      stats.maxHp += 10;
      stats.atk += 1;
    }

    if (category === "undead") {
      stats.def += 1;
    }

    return stats;
  },

  applyStatsObject(target, nextStats) {
    if (!target || typeof target !== "object") return 0;

    let changes = 0;
    const keys = Object.keys(nextStats || {});
    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
      const key = keys[i];
      const next = Number(nextStats[key] || 0);
      if (target[key] !== next) {
        target[key] = next;
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  getContextSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    this.init(root);

    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const byId = actors.byId || {};
    const localIds = Array.isArray(actors.localIds) ? actors.localIds : [];
    if (!localIds.length) return "";

    const maxActors = mode === "tiny" ? 2 : (mode === "wide" ? 4 : 3);
    const parts = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < localIds.length && parts.length < maxActors; i += 1) {
      const record = byId[localIds[i]];
      if (!record || !record.identity) continue;

      const name = String(record.identity.name || "").trim();
      if (!name) continue;

      const role = Array.isArray(record.roleTags) && record.roleTags.length
        ? String(record.roleTags[0] || "")
        : String(record.identity.category || "actor");

      const band = record.levelProfile
        ? "lvl " + String(record.levelProfile.bandMin || 1) + "-" + String(record.levelProfile.bandMax || 1)
        : "";

      let summary = name;
      if (role) summary += " [" + role + "]";
      if (mode !== "tiny" && band) summary += " " + band;

      parts.push(summary);
    }

    if (!parts.length) return "";
    return "Nearby actors: " + parts.join(", ");
  },

  replaceSimpleArray(target, nextValues) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target)) return 0;
    const next = Array.isArray(nextValues) ? nextValues.slice() : [];

    let changed = 0;
    if (target.length !== next.length) {
      changed = 1;
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i += 1) {
        if (target[i] !== next[i]) {
          changed = 1;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    target.splice(0, target.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i += 1) {
      target.push(next[i]);
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushUniqueLocal(list, value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const text = String(value || "").trim();
    if (!text) return 0;
    if (list.indexOf(text) !== -1) return 0;
    list.push(text);
    return 1;
  },

  pushRecentUpdate(list, entry) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || !entry || typeof entry !== "object") return 0;

    const last = list.length ? list[list.length - 1] : null;
    if (last && last.actorId === entry.actorId && last.turn === entry.turn) {
      return 0;
    }

    list.push(entry);
    while (list.length > 10) {
      list.shift();
    }

    return 1;
  }
},

    // === REPUTATION SYSTEM ===
    ReputationSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.reputation || typeof root.reputation !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.reputation)) {
      root.reputation = {};
    }

    const reputation = root.reputation;

    if (!reputation.bySubject || typeof reputation.bySubject !== "object" || Array.isArray(reputation.bySubject)) {
      reputation.bySubject = {};
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(reputation.recentChanges)) reputation.recentChanges = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(reputation.rumors)) reputation.rumors = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(reputation.titles)) reputation.titles = [];
    if (!reputation.tracking || typeof reputation.tracking !== "object" || Array.isArray(reputation.tracking)) {
      reputation.tracking = {};
    }

    if (typeof reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId !== "string") reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId = "";
    if (typeof reputation.tracking.summaryDirty !== "boolean") reputation.tracking.summaryDirty = false;
    if (typeof reputation.lastChangedTurn !== "number") reputation.lastChangedTurn = 0;

    return reputation;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    let changes = 0;
    changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

    if (changes > 0) {
      root.reputation.tracking.summaryDirty = true;
      root.reputation.lastChangedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
        U.markDirty("reputation", true, root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
        U.invalidatePackets(root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
        U.logEvent("Reputation updates: " + changes);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
    const pending = root.pending || {};
    const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = validated[i];
      if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "reputation" || candidate._reputationApplied === true) {
        continue;
      }

      const payload = candidate.payload || {};
      const subjectInfo = this.resolveSubject(payload.subject || "local_group", root);
      const direction = this.normalizeDirection(payload.direction || "");

      if (!this.shouldApplyReputationCandidate(direction, candidate.evidence || "", payload, root)) {
        candidate._reputationApplied = true;
        continue;
      }

      const target = subjectInfo.id || "reputation";

      const commit = () => {
        if (candidate.type === "reputation_shift") {
          return this.applyReputationShift(subjectInfo, direction, candidate.evidence || "", root);
        }
        return 0;
      };

      const localChanges =
        Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
          ? Guard.applyOnce({
              kind: "reputation_" + String(candidate.type || "change"),
              target: target,
              payload: {
                subject: subjectInfo.id,
                direction: direction,
                evidence: candidate.evidence || ""
              },
              sourceId: candidate.id || ""
            }, commit, root)
          : commit();

      changes += localChanges;
      candidate._reputationApplied = true;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  applyReputationShift(subjectInfo, direction, evidence, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const reputation = this.init(root);
    if (!reputation || !subjectInfo || !subjectInfo.id) return 0;

    const record = this.ensureSubjectRecord(subjectInfo, root);
    if (!record || !record.mutable) return 0;

    const delta = this.deltaForDirection(direction);
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (direction === "rumor_activity") {
      const rumorLabel = this.cleanShortText(evidence) || "rumor activity";
      const rumor = {
        turn: turn,
        subjectId: subjectInfo.id,
        label: rumorLabel,
        text: rumorLabel
      };

      reputation.rumors.push(rumor);
      while (reputation.rumors.length > 20) reputation.rumors.shift();
      changes += 1;

      if (record.mutable.tags.indexOf("rumors_active") === -1) {
        record.mutable.tags.push("rumors_active");
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    if (delta !== 0) {
      const current = typeof record.mutable.score === "number" ? record.mutable.score : 0;
      const next = this.clampScore(current + delta);
      if (record.mutable.score !== next) {
        record.mutable.score = next;
        changes += 1;
      }

      const nextStanding = this.standingForScore(next);
      if (record.mutable.standing !== nextStanding) {
        record.mutable.standing = nextStanding;
        changes += 1;
      }

      const tag = delta > 0 ? "positive_recent" : "negative_recent";
      if (record.mutable.tags.indexOf(tag) === -1) {
        record.mutable.tags.push(tag);
        changes += 1;
      }
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      record.mutable.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId = subjectInfo.id;

      reputation.recentChanges.push({
        turn: turn,
        subject: record.identity.displayName,
        subjectId: subjectInfo.id,
        direction: direction,
        delta: delta,
        evidence: this.cleanShortText(evidence)
      });

      while (reputation.recentChanges.length > 16) reputation.recentChanges.shift();
    }

    return changes;
  },

  ensureSubjectRecord(subjectInfo, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const reputation = this.init(root);
    if (!reputation || !subjectInfo || !subjectInfo.id) return null;

    const id = subjectInfo.id;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    if (!reputation.bySubject[id] || typeof reputation.bySubject[id] !== "object" || Array.isArray(reputation.bySubject[id])) {
      reputation.bySubject[id] = {
        identity: {
          id: id,
          displayName: subjectInfo.displayName || id,
          scope: subjectInfo.scope || "local",
          createdTurn: turn
        },
        mutable: {
          score: 0,
          standing: "neutral",
          tags: [],
          notes: [],
          lastChangedTurn: turn
        }
      };
    }

    const record = reputation.bySubject[id];
    if (!record.identity || typeof record.identity !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.identity)) record.identity = {};
    if (!record.mutable || typeof record.mutable !== "object" || Array.isArray(record.mutable)) record.mutable = {};

    if (typeof record.identity.id !== "string") record.identity.id = id;
    if (typeof record.identity.displayName !== "string" || !record.identity.displayName) {
      record.identity.displayName = subjectInfo.displayName || id;
    }
    if (typeof record.identity.scope !== "string") record.identity.scope = subjectInfo.scope || "local";
    if (typeof record.identity.createdTurn !== "number") record.identity.createdTurn = turn;

    if (typeof record.mutable.score !== "number") record.mutable.score = 0;
    if (typeof record.mutable.standing !== "string") record.mutable.standing = this.standingForScore(record.mutable.score);
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.tags)) record.mutable.tags = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(record.mutable.notes)) record.mutable.notes = [];
    if (typeof record.mutable.lastChangedTurn !== "number") record.mutable.lastChangedTurn = turn;

    record.mutable.score = this.clampScore(record.mutable.score);
    record.mutable.standing = this.standingForScore(record.mutable.score);

    return record;
  },

  resolveSubject(subject, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const raw = String(subject || "local_group").trim().toLowerCase();
    const scene = root && root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : {};
    const sceneLabel = String(scene.displayName || "").trim();
    const parentRef = String(scene.parentLocationRef || (root.player && root.player.locationRef) || "").trim();

    if (raw === "public") {
      return { id: "public", displayName: "public rumor", scope: "public" };
    }

    if (raw === "local_group" || raw === "locals" || raw === "local") {
      const label = sceneLabel || parentRef || "local area";
      const key = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(label) : label.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_");
      return {
        id: "local_" + (key || "area"),
        displayName: label + " locals",
        scope: "local"
      };
    }

    const clean = this.cleanShortText(raw) || "unknown group";
    const key = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(clean) : clean.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_");
    return { id: "subject_" + (key || "group"), displayName: clean, scope: "subject" };
  },

  shouldApplyReputationCandidate(direction, evidence, payload, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cleanDirection = this.normalizeDirection(direction);
    const raw = String(evidence || "");

    if (!raw.trim()) return false;

    if (cleanDirection === "rumor_activity") {
      return this.isStrongPlayerRumorEvidence(raw);
    }

    if (cleanDirection !== "positive" && cleanDirection !== "negative") {
      return false;
    }

    if (cleanDirection === "negative" && this.hasNegatedReputationEvidence(raw)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (this.hasExternalEmotionTarget(raw)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (this.hasPlayerReputationAnchor(raw)) {
      return true;
    }

    // Phase 7u2: allow very clear positive local social reactions when no
    // external target exists. This preserves the rain/storm/bandits blockers
    // above while allowing lines such as "The villagers are respectful and grateful."
    if (cleanDirection === "positive" && this.hasClearLocalPositiveReception(raw)) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  hasNegatedReputationEvidence(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    return (
      /\b(?:not|no longer|not anymore|no more)\s+(?:suspicious|hostile|afraid|wary|fearful|resentful|angry)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:less|no longer)\s+(?:wary|suspicious|hostile|afraid|fearful)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:suspicion|hostility|fear|wariness)\s+(?:fades|ends|lessens|eases)\b/.test(lower)
    );
  },

  hasExternalEmotionTarget(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    const match = lower.match(/\b(?:wary|afraid|hostile|suspicious|grateful|thankful|angry|fearful|respectful|trusting)\s+(?:of|to|toward|towards|at|for)\s+([^.!?;,]+)/);
    if (!match || !match[1]) return false;

    const target = String(match[1] || "").trim();
    if (!target) return false;

    if (/^(?:you|your)\b/.test(target)) return false;

    return true;
  },

  hasPlayerReputationAnchor(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    const actionAnchorNouns = "reputation|name|deed|deeds|action|actions|help|defense|defence|rescue|return|threat|intimidation|violence|mercy|courage|bravery|kindness|cruelty|theft|honesty|dishonesty|warning|sacrifice|aid|service";

    return (
      /\b(?:of|toward|towards|at|to|for)\s+you\b/.test(lower) ||
      new RegExp("\\byour\\s+(?:" + actionAnchorNouns + ")\\b").test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:after|because of|following)\s+your\s+[a-z][a-z'-]*(?:\s+[a-z][a-z'-]*){0,3}\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\b(?:respect|respects|trust|trusts|praise|praises|thank|thanks|cheer|cheers|hail|hails|fear|fears|hate|hates|suspect|suspects|watch|watches|avoid|avoids|welcome|welcomes)\s+you\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\byou\s+(?:are|become|became|seem|seemed|look|looked)\s+(?:trusted|respected|praised|feared|hated|welcomed|liked|known|notorious|infamous)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bpeople\s+(?:see|saw|view|viewed)\s+you\s+as\b/.test(lower)
    );
  },

  hasClearLocalPositiveReception(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    if (/\b(?:of|for|toward|towards|at|to)\s+(?!you\b|your\b)[^.!?;,]+/.test(lower)) {
      return false;
    }

    return /\b(?:locals|townsfolk|villagers|guards|patrons|crowd|people|citizens|merchants)\s+(?:are|seem|become|became)\s+(?:[^.!?;,]{0,40}\b)?(?:grateful|thankful|respectful|trusting|cheering|welcoming|approving)\b/.test(lower);
  },

  isStrongPlayerRumorEvidence(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    if (!/\b(?:rumors?|rumours?|whispers?|whispered|word spreads|news spreads|people are saying|talk spreads)\b/.test(lower)) {
      return false;
    }

    return (
      /\b(?:rumors?|rumours?)\s+(?:spread|circulate|begin|start)\s+(?:about|of)\s+(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bword\s+spreads\s+(?:that\s+)?(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bword\s+spreads\s+(?:about|of)\s+your\s+(?:name|deed|deeds|action|actions|reputation|help|rescue|defense|defence|threat|return)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bnews\s+(?:spreads\s+)?(?:of|about)\s+your\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bwhispers?\s+(?:spread|circulate|rise|begin)?\s*(?:about|of)\s+(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\bpeople\s+are\s+saying\s+(?:you|your)\b/.test(lower) ||
      /\byour\s+(?:name|deed|deeds|reputation|actions)\s+(?:spreads|is whispered|is discussed|is mentioned)\b/.test(lower)
    );
  },

  normalizeDirection(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (lower === "positive" || lower === "negative" || lower === "rumor_activity") return lower;
    if (/trust|respect|grateful|cheer|help|praise/.test(lower)) return "positive";
    if (/wary|afraid|hostile|suspicious|hate|fear/.test(lower)) return "negative";
    if (/rumor|whisper|word/.test(lower)) return "rumor_activity";
    return "rumor_activity";
  },

  deltaForDirection(direction) {
    const clean = this.normalizeDirection(direction);
    if (clean === "positive") return 8;
    if (clean === "negative") return -8;
    return 0;
  },

  clampScore(value) {
    const n = Number(value || 0);
    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
    return Math.max(-100, Math.min(100, Math.round(n)));
  },

  standingForScore(score) {
    const n = this.clampScore(score);
    if (n >= 60) return "beloved";
    if (n >= 30) return "trusted";
    if (n >= 10) return "liked";
    if (n <= -60) return "hated";
    if (n <= -30) return "hostile";
    if (n <= -10) return "wary";
    return "neutral";
  },

  cleanShortText(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[<>\[\]{}]/g, "")
      .replace(/[.!?;:]+$/g, "")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 120);
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    const reputation = this.init(root);
    if (!reputation || !reputation.bySubject) return "";

    const primaryId = reputation.tracking && reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId ? reputation.tracking.primarySubjectId : "";
    let record = primaryId && reputation.bySubject[primaryId] ? reputation.bySubject[primaryId] : null;

    if (!record) {
      const keys = Object.keys(reputation.bySubject);
      if (keys.length) record = reputation.bySubject[keys[0]];
    }

    if (!record || !record.identity || !record.mutable) return "";

    const name = record.identity.displayName || "local reputation";
    const standing = record.mutable.standing || this.standingForScore(record.mutable.score || 0);
    const score = typeof record.mutable.score === "number" ? record.mutable.score : 0;
    const parts = ["Reputation: " + name + " " + standing + " (" + score + ")"];

    if (mode === "wide" && Array.isArray(record.mutable.tags) && record.mutable.tags.length) {
      parts.push("Tags: " + record.mutable.tags.slice(0, 3).join(", "));
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === QUEST LOG SYSTEM ===
    QuestLogSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return null;

    if (!root.quests || typeof root.quests !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.quests)) {
      root.quests = {};
    }

    const quests = root.quests;

    if (!Array.isArray(quests.active)) quests.active = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(quests.archived)) quests.archived = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(quests.recentUpdates)) quests.recentUpdates = [];

    if (!quests.tracking || typeof quests.tracking !== "object" || Array.isArray(quests.tracking)) {
      quests.tracking = {};
    }

    if (typeof quests.tracking.primaryQuestId !== "string") quests.tracking.primaryQuestId = "";
    if (typeof quests.tracking.summaryDirty !== "boolean") quests.tracking.summaryDirty = false;
    if (typeof quests.tracking.lastChangedTurn !== "number") quests.tracking.lastChangedTurn = 0;

    this.repairQuestList(quests.active, root);
    this.repairQuestList(quests.archived, root);

    return quests;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const U = A.Utils;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    let changes = 0;

    changes += this.applyValidatedConsequences(root);

    const events = this.extractDirectQuestEvents(raw, root);
    for (let i = 0; i < events.length; i += 1) {
      changes += this.applyQuestEvent(events[i], root);
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      root.quests.tracking.summaryDirty = true;
      root.quests.tracking.lastChangedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

      if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
        U.markDirty("quests", true, root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") {
        U.invalidatePackets(root);
      }
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") {
        U.logEvent("Quest updates: " + changes);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  applyValidatedConsequences(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const pending = root.pending || {};
    const validated = Array.isArray(pending.validatedConsequences) ? pending.validatedConsequences : [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < validated.length; i += 1) {
      const candidate = validated[i];
      if (!candidate || candidate.category !== "quest" || candidate._questApplied === true) {
        continue;
      }

      const payload = candidate.payload || {};
      const status = this.normalizeStatus(payload.status || "");
      if (!status) {
        candidate._questApplied = true;
        continue;
      }

      const evidence = String(candidate.evidence || "");
      if (this.hasQuestNegation(evidence, status)) {
        candidate._questApplied = true;
        continue;
      }

      const event = {
        kind: "status",
        status: status,
        title: this.cleanQuestTitle(payload.title || this.inferQuestTitleFromEvidence(evidence, status)),
        objective: this.cleanQuestObjective(payload.objective || this.inferQuestObjectiveFromEvidence(evidence, status)),
        progress: this.cleanShortText(payload.progress || ""),
        rewardSummary: this.cleanShortText(payload.rewardSummary || this.extractRewardSummary(evidence)),
        evidence: this.cleanShortText(evidence),
        confidence: Number(candidate.confidence || 0),
        sourceType: "validated_quest"
      };

      changes += this.applyQuestEvent(event, root);
      candidate._questApplied = true;
    }

    return changes;
  },

  extractDirectQuestEvents(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const out = [];

    const add = (status, title, objective, evidence, sourceType, confidence) => {
      const cleanStatus = this.normalizeStatus(status);
      if (!cleanStatus) return;

      const ev = String(evidence || raw);
      if (this.hasQuestNegation(ev, cleanStatus)) return;

      const cleanTitle = this.cleanQuestTitle(title || "");
      const cleanObjective = this.cleanQuestObjective(objective || title || "");
      const cleanProgress = cleanStatus === "progress" ? this.cleanShortText(objective || title || ev) : "";

      if ((cleanStatus === "accepted" || cleanStatus === "completed" || cleanStatus === "failed" || cleanStatus === "abandoned") && !cleanTitle && !this.getPrimaryQuest(root)) {
        return;
      }

      out.push({
        kind: cleanStatus === "progress" ? "progress" : "status",
        status: cleanStatus,
        title: cleanTitle,
        objective: cleanObjective,
        progress: cleanProgress,
        rewardSummary: this.cleanShortText(this.extractRewardSummary(ev)),
        evidence: this.cleanShortText(ev),
        confidence: typeof confidence === "number" ? confidence : 0.86,
        sourceType: String(sourceType || "direct_quest")
      });
    };

    const runPatterns = (patterns, status, sourceType) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
        let match;
        const re = patterns[i];
        while ((match = re.exec(raw))) {
          const title = match[1] || "";
          add(status, title, title, match[0], sourceType, 0.88);
        }
      }
    };

    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:accepted|started|added)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\bnew\s+(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:accept|accepted|take on|took on)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\s+(?:to|of|called|named)?\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:agree|agreed)\s+to\s+([^.!?;\n]{3,100})\s+(?:as\s+)?(?:a\s+)?(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\b/gi,
      /\b(?:the\s+)?(?:innkeeper|guard captain|guard|captain|merchant|elder|villager|mayor|guildmaster|guild master|patron)\s+(?:asks|asked|hires|hired|tasks|tasked)\s+you\s+(?:to|with)\s+([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi
    ], "accepted", "direct_acceptance");

    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:objective|goal)\s+(?:updated|changed|set)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\bnext\s+(?:objective|goal)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi,
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:progress|updated|update)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/gi
    ], "progress", "direct_progress");

    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:complete|completed|finished|fulfilled)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:complete|completed|finish|finished|fulfill|fulfilled)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi
    ], "completed", "direct_completion");

    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:failed|lost)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:fail|failed)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi
    ], "failed", "direct_failure");

    // Phase 8 u1:
    // Abandoned is terminal like failed, but it must keep its own status.
    // Do not group abandoned under failed or /questlog archives the quest incorrectly.
    runPatterns([
      /\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:abandoned)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi,
      /\byou\s+(?:abandon|abandoned)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/gi
    ], "abandoned", "direct_abandonment");

    return this.dedupeEvents(out);
  },

  applyQuestEvent(event, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    if (!event || typeof event !== "object") return 0;

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG;
    const Guard = A.OperationGuard;
    const status = this.normalizeStatus(event.status || "");
    if (!status) return 0;

    const cleanEvent = {
      kind: String(event.kind || "status"),
      status: status,
      title: this.cleanQuestTitle(event.title || ""),
      objective: this.cleanQuestObjective(event.objective || ""),
      progress: this.cleanShortText(event.progress || ""),
      rewardSummary: this.cleanShortText(event.rewardSummary || ""),
      evidence: this.cleanShortText(event.evidence || ""),
      confidence: Number(event.confidence || 0),
      sourceType: String(event.sourceType || "quest_event")
    };

    if (this.shouldIgnoreQuestEvent(cleanEvent, root)) {
      return 0;
    }

    const target = cleanEvent.title || cleanEvent.objective || cleanEvent.status;
    const commit = () => this.commitQuestEvent(cleanEvent, root);

    return Guard && typeof Guard.applyOnce === "function"
      ? Guard.applyOnce({
          kind: "quest_" + cleanEvent.status,
          target: target,
          payload: cleanEvent,
          sourceId: cleanEvent.evidence
        }, commit, root)
      : commit();
  },

  commitQuestEvent(event, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (event.status === "accepted") {
      const title = event.title || event.objective;
      if (!title) return 0;

      const quest = this.ensureQuestRecord(title, event.objective || title, event, root);
      if (!quest) return 0;

      if (quest.status !== "accepted" && quest.status !== "active") {
        quest.status = "accepted";
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (event.objective && quest.objective !== event.objective) {
        quest.objective = event.objective;
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (event.rewardSummary && quest.rewardSummary !== event.rewardSummary) {
        quest.rewardSummary = event.rewardSummary;
        changes += 1;
      }

      changes += this.pushProgressNote(quest, "accepted", event.evidence || event.objective || title, turn);
      changes += this.setPrimaryQuest(quest.id, root);
    } else if (event.status === "progress") {
      const quest = this.findMatchingQuest(event.title || event.objective, root) || this.getPrimaryQuest(root);
      if (!quest) return 0;

      if (event.objective && event.sourceType !== "direct_progress") {
        if (quest.objective !== event.objective) {
          quest.objective = event.objective;
          changes += 1;
        }
      } else if (event.objective && /objective|goal/i.test(event.evidence || "")) {
        if (quest.objective !== event.objective) {
          quest.objective = event.objective;
          changes += 1;
        }
      }

      changes += this.pushProgressNote(quest, "progress", event.progress || event.objective || event.evidence, turn);
    } else if (event.status === "completed" || event.status === "failed" || event.status === "abandoned") {
      let quest = this.findMatchingQuest(event.title, root) || this.getPrimaryQuest(root);

      if (!quest && event.title) {
        quest = this.ensureQuestRecord(event.title, event.objective || event.title, event, root);
      }

      if (!quest) return 0;

      if (quest.status !== event.status) {
        quest.status = event.status;
        changes += 1;
      }

      if (event.rewardSummary && quest.rewardSummary !== event.rewardSummary) {
        quest.rewardSummary = event.rewardSummary;
        changes += 1;
      }

      changes += this.pushProgressNote(quest, event.status, event.evidence || event.title, turn);
      changes += this.archiveQuestIfTerminal(quest.id, root);
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      this.pushRecentUpdate({
        turn: turn,
        status: event.status,
        title: event.title || event.objective || "quest",
        evidence: event.evidence || ""
      }, root);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  shouldIgnoreQuestEvent(event, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!event || typeof event !== "object") return true;

    const status = this.normalizeStatus(event.status || "");
    if (!status) return true;

    const evidence = String(event.evidence || "");
    if (this.hasQuestNegation(evidence, status)) return true;

    if (status === "accepted") {
      const title = event.title || event.objective;
      if (!title || this.isBadQuestTitle(title)) return true;

      // Do not create quests from pure discussion or future intent.
      if (/\b(?:consider|considering|might|maybe|could|should|talk about|talks about|mentions|mention|heard about)\b/i.test(evidence) && !/\b(?:accepted|agree|agreed|take on|took on|quest accepted|new quest|tasks you|hires you|asks you)\b/i.test(evidence)) {
        return true;
      }
    }

    if ((status === "completed" || status === "failed" || status === "abandoned") && !event.title && !this.getPrimaryQuest(root)) {
      return true;
    }

    if (status === "progress" && !this.getPrimaryQuest(root) && !event.title) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  ensureQuestRecord(title, objective, event, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const cleanTitle = this.cleanQuestTitle(title);
    if (!cleanTitle || this.isBadQuestTitle(cleanTitle)) return null;

    const existing = this.findMatchingQuest(cleanTitle, root, true);
    if (existing) {
      return existing;
    }

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const id = "quest_" + (U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(cleanTitle) : this.fallbackKey(cleanTitle));

    const quest = {
      id: id,
      title: cleanTitle,
      objective: this.cleanQuestObjective(objective || cleanTitle),
      status: "accepted",
      rewardSummary: event && event.rewardSummary ? event.rewardSummary : "",
      tags: [],
      progressNotes: [],
      createdTurn: turn,
      lastChangedTurn: turn,
      sourceEvidence: event && event.evidence ? this.cleanShortText(event.evidence) : ""
    };

    quests.active.push(quest);
    return quest;
  },

  findMatchingQuest(query, root = state.aidrpg, activeOnly = false) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const key = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(query || "") : this.fallbackKey(query || "");
    if (!key) return null;

    const lists = activeOnly ? [quests.active] : [quests.active, quests.archived];
    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -1;

    for (let l = 0; l < lists.length; l += 1) {
      const list = Array.isArray(lists[l]) ? lists[l] : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
        const quest = list[i];
        if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") continue;

        const titleKey = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(quest.title || "") : this.fallbackKey(quest.title || "");
        const objectiveKey = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(quest.objective || "") : this.fallbackKey(quest.objective || "");
        let score = 0;

        if (titleKey === key) score = 100;
        else if (objectiveKey === key) score = 95;
        else if (titleKey && titleKey.indexOf(key) !== -1 && key.length >= 5) score = 82;
        else if (titleKey && key.indexOf(titleKey) !== -1 && titleKey.length >= 5) score = 78;
        else if (objectiveKey && objectiveKey.indexOf(key) !== -1 && key.length >= 5) score = 72;
        else if (objectiveKey && key.indexOf(objectiveKey) !== -1 && objectiveKey.length >= 5) score = 70;

        if (score > bestScore) {
          bestScore = score;
          best = quest;
        }
      }
    }

    return bestScore >= 70 ? best : null;
  },

  getPrimaryQuest(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const primaryId = quests.tracking && quests.tracking.primaryQuestId ? quests.tracking.primaryQuestId : "";

    if (primaryId) {
      for (let i = 0; i < quests.active.length; i += 1) {
        if (quests.active[i] && quests.active[i].id === primaryId) {
          return quests.active[i];
        }
      }
    }

    return quests.active.length && quests.active[0] ? quests.active[0] : null;
  },

  setPrimaryQuest(questId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const id = String(questId || "").trim();
    if (!id) return 0;
    if (quests.tracking.primaryQuestId === id) return 0;
    quests.tracking.primaryQuestId = id;
    return 1;
  },

  archiveQuestIfTerminal(questId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    const id = String(questId || "").trim();
    if (!id) return 0;

    let index = -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < quests.active.length; i += 1) {
      if (quests.active[i] && quests.active[i].id === id) {
        index = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (index === -1) return 0;

    const quest = quests.active.splice(index, 1)[0];
    quest.lastChangedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : quest.lastChangedTurn || 0;

    let archivedIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < quests.archived.length; i += 1) {
      if (quests.archived[i] && quests.archived[i].id === id) {
        archivedIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (archivedIndex === -1) {
      quests.archived.push(quest);
      while (quests.archived.length > 30) {
        quests.archived.shift();
      }
    } else {
      quests.archived[archivedIndex] = quest;
    }

    if (quests.tracking.primaryQuestId === id) {
      quests.tracking.primaryQuestId = quests.active.length && quests.active[0] ? quests.active[0].id : "";
    }

    return 1;
  },

  pushProgressNote(quest, status, note, turn) {
    if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") return 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(quest.progressNotes)) quest.progressNotes = [];

    const text = this.cleanShortText(note || "");
    if (!text) return 0;

    const last = quest.progressNotes.length ? quest.progressNotes[quest.progressNotes.length - 1] : null;
    if (last && last.status === status && last.text === text) {
      return 0;
    }

    quest.progressNotes.push({
      turn: typeof turn === "number" ? turn : 0,
      status: String(status || "progress"),
      text: text
    });

    while (quest.progressNotes.length > 10) {
      quest.progressNotes.shift();
    }

    quest.lastChangedTurn = typeof turn === "number" ? turn : quest.lastChangedTurn || 0;
    return 1;
  },

  pushRecentUpdate(entry, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const quests = this.init(root);
    if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return 0;

    const clean = {
      turn: typeof entry.turn === "number" ? entry.turn : 0,
      status: this.normalizeStatus(entry.status || "progress") || "progress",
      title: this.cleanShortText(entry.title || "quest"),
      evidence: this.cleanShortText(entry.evidence || "")
    };

    const last = quests.recentUpdates.length ? quests.recentUpdates[quests.recentUpdates.length - 1] : null;
    if (last && last.turn === clean.turn && last.status === clean.status && last.title === clean.title) {
      return 0;
    }

    quests.recentUpdates.push(clean);
    while (quests.recentUpdates.length > 12) {
      quests.recentUpdates.shift();
    }
    return 1;
  },

  repairQuestList(list, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const turn = root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = list.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
      const quest = list[i];
      if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") {
        list.splice(i, 1);
        changes += 1;
        continue;
      }

      if (typeof quest.id !== "string" || !quest.id) {
        quest.id = "quest_" + this.fallbackKey(quest.title || quest.objective || "quest_" + i);
        changes += 1;
      }
      if (typeof quest.title !== "string") quest.title = "";
      if (typeof quest.objective !== "string") quest.objective = quest.title || "";
      if (typeof quest.status !== "string") quest.status = "accepted";
      if (typeof quest.rewardSummary !== "string") quest.rewardSummary = "";
      if (!Array.isArray(quest.tags)) quest.tags = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(quest.progressNotes)) quest.progressNotes = [];
      if (typeof quest.createdTurn !== "number") quest.createdTurn = turn;
      if (typeof quest.lastChangedTurn !== "number") quest.lastChangedTurn = turn;
      if (typeof quest.sourceEvidence !== "string") quest.sourceEvidence = "";
    }

    return changes;
  },

  dedupeEvents(events) {
    const out = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    for (let i = 0; i < events.length; i += 1) {
      const ev = events[i] || {};
      const key = [ev.status || "", this.cleanQuestTitle(ev.title || ""), this.cleanQuestObjective(ev.objective || "")].join("|").toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      out.push(ev);
    }

    return out;
  },

  inferQuestTitleFromEvidence(evidence, status) {
    const raw = String(evidence || "");
    const cleanStatus = this.normalizeStatus(status || "");
    const patterns = [];

    if (cleanStatus === "accepted") {
      patterns.push(/\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:accepted|started|added)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/i);
      patterns.push(/\bnew\s+(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/i);
      patterns.push(/\byou\s+(?:accept|accepted|take on|took on)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|job|bounty|contract|task)\s+(?:to|of|called|named)?\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/i);
    } else if (cleanStatus === "completed") {
      patterns.push(/\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:complete|completed|finished|fulfilled)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/i);
    } else if (cleanStatus === "failed" || cleanStatus === "abandoned") {
      patterns.push(/\b(?:quest|task|job|contract|bounty)\s+(?:failed|abandoned|lost)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{0,100})/i);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < patterns.length; i += 1) {
      const match = raw.match(patterns[i]);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        return this.cleanQuestTitle(match[1]);
      }
    }

    return "";
  },

  inferQuestObjectiveFromEvidence(evidence, status) {
    const title = this.inferQuestTitleFromEvidence(evidence, status);
    if (title) return title;

    const raw = String(evidence || "");
    const match = raw.match(/\b(?:objective|goal)\s+(?:updated|changed|set)?\s*[:\-—]\s*([^.!?;\n]{3,100})/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return this.cleanQuestObjective(match[1]);
    }

    return "";
  },

  extractRewardSummary(evidence) {
    const raw = String(evidence || "");
    const match = raw.match(/\b(?:reward|payment)\s*[:\-—]?\s*([^.!?;\n]{2,80})/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return this.cleanShortText(match[1]);
    }
    return "";
  },

  hasQuestNegation(value, status) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!lower) return false;

    if (/\b(?:refuse|refused|decline|declined|turn down|turned down|reject|rejected)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:not|never|no longer)\b[^.!?]{0,24}\b(?:accept|accepted|take on|took on|agree|agreed|complete|completed|finish|finished)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:do not|don't|does not|doesn't|did not|didn't)\b[^.!?]{0,24}\b(?:accept|take on|complete|finish)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\bnot\s+(?:a|your|the)\s+(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s+(?:is|was)\s+not\s+(?:accepted|complete|completed|finished)\b/.test(lower)) return true;

    return false;
  },

  normalizeStatus(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!lower) return "";
    if (/^(accepted|accept|started|start|active|added)$/.test(lower)) return "accepted";
    if (/^(completed|complete|finished|finish|fulfilled|fulfill|done)$/.test(lower)) return "completed";
    if (/^(failed|fail|lost)$/.test(lower)) return "failed";
    if (/^(abandoned|abandon)$/.test(lower)) return "abandoned";
    if (/^(progress|updated|update|objective|goal)$/.test(lower)) return "progress";
    return "";
  },

  cleanQuestTitle(value) {
    let text = this.cleanShortText(value)
      .replace(/^(?:to|of|called|named)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s+(?:to|of)?\s*/i, "")
      .replace(/^(?:quest|job|task|contract|bounty)\s+/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+reward\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+payment\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (text.length > 96) text = text.slice(0, 96).trim();
    return text;
  },

  cleanQuestObjective(value) {
    let text = this.cleanShortText(value)
      .replace(/^(?:objective|goal)\s+(?:updated|changed|set)?\s*[:\-—]?\s*/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+reward\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+payment\b[\s\S]*$/i, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

    if (text.length > 120) text = text.slice(0, 120).trim();
    return text;
  },

  cleanShortText(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[<>\[\]{}]/g, "")
      .replace(/[.!?;:]+$/g, "")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 140);
  },

  isBadQuestTitle(value) {
    const lower = this.cleanShortText(value).toLowerCase();
    if (!lower) return true;
    if (lower.length < 3) return true;
    if (lower.split(/\s+/).length > 14) return true;

    const banned = new Set([
      "quest", "task", "job", "contract", "bounty", "the quest", "the task",
      "it", "that", "this", "nothing", "none", "unknown", "accepted", "complete",
      "completed", "failed", "abandoned"
    ]);

    if (banned.has(lower)) return true;
    if (/\b(?:maybe|perhaps|might|could|consider|considering|rumor|rumour|story|tale)\b/.test(lower)) return true;

    return false;
  },

  fallbackKey(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_")
      .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "")
      .replace(/_+/g, "_");
  },

  getCompactSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";
    const quests = this.init(root);
    if (!quests || !Array.isArray(quests.active) || !quests.active.length) return "";

    const quest = this.getPrimaryQuest(root) || quests.active[0];
    if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") return "";

    const parts = ["Quest: " + (quest.title || "active task")];
    if (quest.status) parts.push("Status: " + quest.status);
    if (mode !== "tiny" && quest.objective) parts.push("Goal: " + quest.objective);

    if (mode === "wide" && Array.isArray(quest.progressNotes) && quest.progressNotes.length) {
      const last = quest.progressNotes[quest.progressNotes.length - 1];
      if (last && last.text) parts.push("Latest: " + last.text);
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  }
},

    // === CARD SYNC SYSTEM ===
    CardSyncSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return null;

    if (!root.cards || typeof root.cards !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cards)) {
      root.cards = {};
    }

    const cards = root.cards;

    if (!cards.config || typeof cards.config !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.config)) {
      cards.config = {};
    }

    const config = cards.config;
    if (typeof config.namespacePrefix !== "string" || !config.namespacePrefix) config.namespacePrefix = "SYS:AIDRPG";
    if (typeof config.liveWrites !== "boolean") config.liveWrites = false;
    if (typeof config.strictCardSync !== "boolean") config.strictCardSync = true;
    if (typeof config.cardSafeMode !== "boolean") config.cardSafeMode = true;
    if (typeof config.maxWritesPerTurn !== "number") config.maxWritesPerTurn = 2;
    if (typeof config.maxCardTextChars !== "number") config.maxCardTextChars = 1200;
    if (typeof config.maxQueueSize !== "number") config.maxQueueSize = 80;

    if (!cards.index || typeof cards.index !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index)) {
      cards.index = {};
    }
    if (!cards.index.byNamespace || typeof cards.index.byNamespace !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index.byNamespace)) cards.index.byNamespace = {};
    if (!cards.index.byCardId || typeof cards.index.byCardId !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.index.byCardId)) cards.index.byCardId = {};
    if (typeof cards.index.lastRefreshedTurn !== "number") cards.index.lastRefreshedTurn = 0;

    if (!Array.isArray(cards.dirtyQueue)) cards.dirtyQueue = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(cards.pendingWrites)) cards.pendingWrites = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(cards.recentOperations)) cards.recentOperations = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(cards.dryRunLog)) cards.dryRunLog = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(cards.duplicateRefs)) cards.duplicateRefs = [];
    if (!cards.lastWriteHashes || typeof cards.lastWriteHashes !== "object" || Array.isArray(cards.lastWriteHashes)) cards.lastWriteHashes = {};
    if (typeof cards.lastSyncTurn !== "number") cards.lastSyncTurn = 0;

    return cards;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    // Chunk 24 serializes dirty canon state into dry-run specs by default.
    // Live Story Card writes remain double-gated behind /cardsync live on and /cardsync safe off.
    this.refreshIndex(root);
    const queued = this.queueDirtyFromMeta(root);
    const scheduled = this.processDirtyQueue(root);
    return queued + scheduled;
  },

  getStoryCardApi() {
    try {
      if (typeof storyCards !== "undefined" && storyCards) return storyCards;
    } catch (_error) {}

    const g = globalThis || {};
    if (g.storyCards) return g.storyCards;

    try {
      if (typeof info === "object" && info && info.storyCards) return info.storyCards;
    } catch (_error2) {}

    return null;
  },

  getAllCards() {
    const api = this.getStoryCardApi();
    if (!api) return [];

    try {
      if (Array.isArray(api)) return api.slice();
