      if (Array.isArray(api.cards)) return api.cards.slice();
      if (typeof api.getCards === "function") {
        const value = api.getCards();
        return Array.isArray(value) ? value.slice() : [];
      }
      if (typeof api.getAll === "function") {
        const value = api.getAll();
        return Array.isArray(value) ? value.slice() : [];
      }
    } catch (_error) {
      return [];
    }

    const out = [];
    try {
      const keys = Object.keys(api);
      for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 1) {
        const row = api[keys[i]];
        if (row && typeof row === "object") out.push(row);
      }
    } catch (_error2) {}

    return out;
  },

  makeNamespace(family, id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root) || {};
    const prefix = cards.config && cards.config.namespacePrefix ? cards.config.namespacePrefix : "SYS:AIDRPG";
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const cleanFamily = U.toKey(String(family || "misc")).toUpperCase();
    const cleanId = U.toKey(String(id || "summary")).toUpperCase();
    if (!cleanFamily || !cleanId) return "";
    return prefix + ":" + cleanFamily + ":" + cleanId;
  },

  makeTitle(namespace, displayName) {
    const ns = String(namespace || "").trim();
    const label = this.cleanCardText(displayName || "", 80);
    if (!ns) return label;
    return label ? ns + " | " + label : ns;
  },

  extractNamespaceFromText(value, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root) || {};
    const prefix = cards.config && cards.config.namespacePrefix ? cards.config.namespacePrefix : "SYS:AIDRPG";
    const text = String(value || "");
    const escaped = prefix.replace(/[-\/\\^$*+?.()|[\]{}]/g, "\\$&");
    const re = new RegExp("\\b" + escaped + ":[A-Z0-9_]+:[A-Z0-9_]+\\b", "i");
    const match = text.match(re);
    return match && match[0] ? match[0].toUpperCase() : "";
  },

  extractNamespaceFromCard(card, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!card || typeof card !== "object") return "";

    const fields = [
      card.title,
      card.name,
      card.entry,
      card.text,
      card.description,
      Array.isArray(card.keys) ? card.keys.join(" ") : card.keys,
      Array.isArray(card.keywords) ? card.keywords.join(" ") : card.keywords
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < fields.length; i += 1) {
      const ns = this.extractNamespaceFromText(fields[i], root);
      if (ns) return ns;
    }

    return "";
  },

  isManagedCard(card, root = state.aidrpg) {
    return !!this.extractNamespaceFromCard(card, root);
  },

  normalizeCardRecord(card, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = card && typeof card === "object" ? card : {};
    const id = String(raw.id || raw.cardId || raw.uuid || raw._id || "").trim();
    const title = String(raw.title || raw.name || "").trim();
    const entry = String(raw.entry || raw.text || raw.description || "").trim();
    const keysValue = Array.isArray(raw.keys) ? raw.keys : Array.isArray(raw.keywords) ? raw.keywords : [];
    const keys = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < keysValue.length; i += 1) {
      const key = String(keysValue[i] || "").trim();
      if (key) keys.push(key);
    }

    return {
      id: id,
      title: title,
      entry: entry,
      keys: keys,
      namespace: this.extractNamespaceFromCard(raw, root),
      raw: raw
    };
  },

  refreshIndex(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards) return 0;

    const all = this.getAllCards();
    const byNamespace = {};
    const byCardId = {};
    const duplicateRefs = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i += 1) {
      const normalized = this.normalizeCardRecord(all[i], root);
      if (!normalized.namespace) continue;

      const key = normalized.namespace;
      if (!byNamespace[key]) byNamespace[key] = [];
      byNamespace[key].push({
        id: normalized.id,
        title: normalized.title,
        keys: normalized.keys.slice(0, 8),
        hash: this.stableHash(normalized.entry || normalized.title || key)
      });

      if (normalized.id) {
        byCardId[normalized.id] = key;
      }
    }

    const namespaces = Object.keys(byNamespace);
    for (let i = 0; i < namespaces.length; i += 1) {
      const ns = namespaces[i];
      if (byNamespace[ns].length > 1) duplicateRefs.push(ns);
    }

    cards.index.byNamespace = byNamespace;
    cards.index.byCardId = byCardId;
    cards.index.lastRefreshedTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    cards.duplicateRefs = duplicateRefs;

    return namespaces.length;
  },

  cleanCardText(value, maxChars) {
    const limit = Math.max(40, Math.floor(Number(maxChars || 1200)));
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+/g, " ")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[<>]/g, "")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, limit);
  },

  stableHash(value) {
    if (globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine.stableHash === "function") {
      return globalThis.AIDRPG.TurnEngine.stableHash(value);
    }

    const str = String(value || "");
    let hash = 5381;
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i += 1) {
      hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) ^ str.charCodeAt(i);
      hash = hash >>> 0;
    }
    return String(hash);
  },

  makeCardSpec(family, id, displayName, body, keys, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root) || {};
    const namespace = this.makeNamespace(family, id, root);
    const cleanName = this.cleanCardText(displayName || id || family, 80);
    const cleanBody = this.cleanCardText(body || "", cards.config ? cards.config.maxCardTextChars : 1200);
    const cleanKeys = [namespace];

    if (Array.isArray(keys)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < keys.length && cleanKeys.length < 8; i += 1) {
        const key = this.cleanCardText(keys[i], 40);
        if (key && cleanKeys.indexOf(key) === -1) cleanKeys.push(key);
      }
    }

    return {
      namespace: namespace,
      family: String(family || "misc"),
      id: String(id || "summary"),
      title: this.makeTitle(namespace, cleanName),
      entry: namespace + "\n" + cleanBody,
      keys: cleanKeys,
      hash: this.stableHash(cleanBody + "|" + cleanKeys.join(","))
    };
  },

  isLiveWritesEnabled(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !cards.config) return false;
    return cards.config.liveWrites === true && cards.config.cardSafeMode === false;
  },

  setLiveWrites(enabled, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !cards.config) return 0;

    const next = enabled === true;
    if (cards.config.liveWrites === next) return 0;

    cards.config.liveWrites = next;
    this.recordOperation("setLiveWrites", {
      mode: next ? "armed" : "dry_run",
      message: next ? "live writes armed; safe mode must also be off" : "live writes disabled"
    }, root);
    return 1;
  },

  setCardSafeMode(enabled, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !cards.config) return 0;

    const next = enabled !== false;
    if (cards.config.cardSafeMode === next) return 0;

    cards.config.cardSafeMode = next;
    this.recordOperation("setCardSafeMode", {
      mode: next ? "safe" : "armed",
      message: next ? "safe mode enabled" : "safe mode disabled; live writes require liveWrites on"
    }, root);
    return 1;
  },

  buildStoryCardPayload(spec, existingCard) {
    const cleanSpec = spec && typeof spec === "object" ? spec : {};
    const existing = existingCard && typeof existingCard === "object" ? existingCard : {};
    const raw = existing.raw && typeof existing.raw === "object" ? existing.raw : existing;
    const id = String(existing.id || raw.id || raw.cardId || raw.uuid || raw._id || "").trim();
    const title = String(cleanSpec.title || existing.title || raw.title || raw.name || cleanSpec.namespace || "").trim();
    const entry = String(cleanSpec.entry || existing.entry || raw.entry || raw.text || raw.description || "").trim();
    const keys = Array.isArray(cleanSpec.keys) ? cleanSpec.keys.slice(0, 8) : [];

    const payload = {
      title: title,
      name: title,
      entry: entry,
      text: entry,
      description: entry,
      keys: keys,
      keywords: keys.slice(),
      namespace: String(cleanSpec.namespace || existing.namespace || ""),
      aidrpgManaged: true
    };

    if (id) {
      payload.id = id;
      payload.cardId = id;
    }

    return payload;
  },

  callStoryCardMethod(methodNames, argSets, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const sources = [];
    const api = this.getStoryCardApi();
    if (api && typeof api === "object") sources.push({ label: "storyCards", api: api });

    try {
      if (typeof globalThis === "object" && globalThis) sources.push({ label: "globalThis", api: globalThis });
    } catch (_error) {}

    try {
      if (typeof info === "object" && info && info.storyCards && info.storyCards !== api) {
        sources.push({ label: "info.storyCards", api: info.storyCards });
      }
    } catch (_error2) {}

    const names = Array.isArray(methodNames) ? methodNames : [];
    const calls = Array.isArray(argSets) ? argSets : [];
    const errors = [];

    for (let s = 0; s < sources.length; s += 1) {
      const source = sources[s];
      for (let n = 0; n < names.length; n += 1) {
        const name = names[n];
        const fn = source.api && source.api[name];
        if (typeof fn !== "function") continue;

        for (let a = 0; a < calls.length; a += 1) {
          const args = Array.isArray(calls[a]) ? calls[a] : [calls[a]];
          try {
            const result = fn.apply(source.api, args);
            return {
              ok: true,
              result: result,
              method: String(name || ""),
              source: source.label
            };
          } catch (error) {
            errors.push(String(name || "method") + ": " + (error && error.message ? error.message : String(error)));
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      ok: false,
      error: errors.length ? errors.slice(-2).join(" | ") : "no compatible Story Card write method found"
    };
  },

  tryLiveAddCard(spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = this.buildStoryCardPayload(spec, null);
    return this.callStoryCardMethod(
      ["addStoryCard", "addCard", "createStoryCard", "createCard", "create", "add"],
      [[payload]],
      root
    );
  },

  tryLiveUpdateCard(card, spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const payload = this.buildStoryCardPayload(spec, card);
    const id = String(payload.id || payload.cardId || "").trim();
    const argSets = id ? [[id, payload], [payload]] : [[payload]];

    return this.callStoryCardMethod(
      ["updateStoryCard", "updateCard", "editStoryCard", "editCard", "update", "set"],
      argSets,
      root
    );
  },

  tryLiveRemoveCard(card, reason, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const normalized = this.normalizeCardRecord(card, root);
    const raw = normalized.raw || card;
    const id = String(normalized.id || "").trim();
    const argSets = id ? [[id], [raw]] : [[raw]];

    return this.callStoryCardMethod(
      ["removeStoryCard", "removeCard", "deleteStoryCard", "deleteCard", "remove"],
      argSets,
      root
    );
  },

  recordOperation(action, detail, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards) return 0;

    const row = detail && typeof detail === "object" ? detail : {};
    cards.recentOperations.push({
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      action: String(action || "operation"),
      namespace: String(row.namespace || ""),
      cardId: String(row.cardId || row.id || ""),
      mode: String(row.mode || (this.isLiveWritesEnabled(root) ? "live" : "dry_run")),
      message: this.cleanCardText(row.message || "", 140)
    });

    while (cards.recentOperations.length > 24) cards.recentOperations.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  pushPendingWrite(kind, spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !spec || typeof spec !== "object" || !spec.namespace) return 0;

    const hash = spec.hash || this.stableHash((spec.entry || "") + "|" + (spec.title || ""));
    for (let i = 0; i < cards.pendingWrites.length; i += 1) {
      const row = cards.pendingWrites[i];
      if (row && row.kind === kind && row.namespace === spec.namespace && row.hash === hash) {
        return 0;
      }
    }

    const row = {
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      kind: String(kind || "write"),
      namespace: spec.namespace,
      title: spec.title || "",
      hash: hash
    };

    cards.pendingWrites.push(row);

    cards.dryRunLog.push({
      turn: row.turn,
      kind: row.kind,
      namespace: row.namespace,
      title: row.title,
      hash: row.hash,
      mode: "dry_run"
    });

    while (cards.pendingWrites.length > 80) cards.pendingWrites.shift();
    while (cards.dryRunLog.length > 80) cards.dryRunLog.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  safeFindCardsByNamespace(namespace, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const ns = String(namespace || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!ns) return [];

    const all = this.getAllCards();
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i += 1) {
      const normalized = this.normalizeCardRecord(all[i], root);
      if (normalized.namespace === ns) out.push(normalized);
    }
    return out;
  },

  safeFindCard(namespace, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const matches = this.safeFindCardsByNamespace(namespace, root);
    if (!matches.length) return null;
    return matches[0];
  },

  safeAddCard(spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cleanSpec = spec && typeof spec === "object" ? spec : null;
    if (!cleanSpec || !cleanSpec.namespace) return null;

    if (!this.isLiveWritesEnabled(root)) {
      this.pushPendingWrite("add", cleanSpec, root);
      this.recordOperation("safeAddCard", { namespace: cleanSpec.namespace, mode: "dry_run", message: "live writes disabled" }, root);
      return null;
    }

    const liveResult = this.tryLiveAddCard(cleanSpec, root);
    if (liveResult && liveResult.ok) {
      this.recordOperation("safeAddCard", {
        namespace: cleanSpec.namespace,
        mode: "live",
        message: "created via " + liveResult.source + "." + liveResult.method
      }, root);
      this.refreshIndex(root);
      return liveResult.result || null;
    }

    this.pushPendingWrite("add", cleanSpec, root);
    this.recordOperation("safeAddCard", {
      namespace: cleanSpec.namespace,
      mode: "blocked",
      message: liveResult && liveResult.error ? liveResult.error : "live add failed"
    }, root);
    return null;
  },

  safeUpdateCard(card, spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cleanSpec = spec && typeof spec === "object" ? spec : null;
    if (!cleanSpec || !cleanSpec.namespace) return null;

    if (!this.isLiveWritesEnabled(root)) {
      this.pushPendingWrite("update", cleanSpec, root);
      this.recordOperation("safeUpdateCard", { namespace: cleanSpec.namespace, cardId: card && card.id, mode: "dry_run", message: "live writes disabled" }, root);
      return null;
    }

    const liveResult = this.tryLiveUpdateCard(card, cleanSpec, root);
    if (liveResult && liveResult.ok) {
      this.recordOperation("safeUpdateCard", {
        namespace: cleanSpec.namespace,
        cardId: card && card.id,
        mode: "live",
        message: "updated via " + liveResult.source + "." + liveResult.method
      }, root);
      this.refreshIndex(root);
      return liveResult.result || card || null;
    }

    this.pushPendingWrite("update", cleanSpec, root);
    this.recordOperation("safeUpdateCard", {
      namespace: cleanSpec.namespace,
      cardId: card && card.id,
      mode: "blocked",
      message: liveResult && liveResult.error ? liveResult.error : "live update failed"
    }, root);
    return null;
  },

  safeRemoveCard(card, reason, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const normalized = this.normalizeCardRecord(card, root);
    if (!normalized.namespace && !normalized.id) return 0;

    if (!this.isLiveWritesEnabled(root)) {
      const spec = { namespace: normalized.namespace || String(normalized.id || ""), title: normalized.title || "", hash: this.stableHash(reason || "remove") };
      this.pushPendingWrite("remove", spec, root);
      this.recordOperation("safeRemoveCard", { namespace: normalized.namespace, cardId: normalized.id, mode: "dry_run", message: reason || "remove requested" }, root);
      return 0;
    }

    const liveResult = this.tryLiveRemoveCard(card, reason, root);
    if (liveResult && liveResult.ok) {
      this.recordOperation("safeRemoveCard", {
        namespace: normalized.namespace,
        cardId: normalized.id,
        mode: "live",
        message: "removed via " + liveResult.source + "." + liveResult.method
      }, root);
      this.refreshIndex(root);
      return 1;
    }

    const spec = { namespace: normalized.namespace || String(normalized.id || ""), title: normalized.title || "", hash: this.stableHash(reason || "remove") };
    this.pushPendingWrite("remove", spec, root);
    this.recordOperation("safeRemoveCard", {
      namespace: normalized.namespace,
      cardId: normalized.id,
      mode: "blocked",
      message: liveResult && liveResult.error ? liveResult.error : "live remove failed"
    }, root);
    return 0;
  },

  safeRenameDuplicateCard(card, namespace, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const ns = String(namespace || this.extractNamespaceFromCard(card, root) || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!ns) return 0;

    this.recordOperation("safeRenameDuplicateCard", { namespace: ns, cardId: card && (card.id || card.cardId || ""), mode: "dry_run", message: "duplicate managed card detected" }, root);
    const spec = { namespace: ns, title: ns + " | duplicate pending manual review", hash: this.stableHash("duplicate:" + ns) };
    return this.pushPendingWrite("rename_duplicate", spec, root);
  },

  safeEnsureSingletonCard(spec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cleanSpec = spec && typeof spec === "object" ? spec : null;
    if (!cleanSpec || !cleanSpec.namespace) return null;

    const matches = this.safeFindCardsByNamespace(cleanSpec.namespace, root);
    if (!matches.length) {
      this.safeAddCard(cleanSpec, root);
      return null;
    }

    const primary = matches[0];
    for (let i = 1; i < matches.length; i += 1) {
      this.safeRenameDuplicateCard(matches[i].raw || matches[i], cleanSpec.namespace, root);
    }

    const currentHash = this.stableHash(primary.entry || "");
    if (currentHash !== cleanSpec.hash) {
      this.safeUpdateCard(primary, cleanSpec, root);
    }

    return primary;
  },

  queueDirty(family, id, reason, payload, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards) return 0;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    const cleanFamily = U.toKey(String(family || "misc"));
    const cleanId = U.toKey(String(id || "summary"));
    if (!cleanFamily || !cleanId) return 0;

    const key = cleanFamily + ":" + cleanId;
    for (let i = 0; i < cards.dirtyQueue.length; i += 1) {
      const row = cards.dirtyQueue[i];
      if (row && row.key === key) {
        row.reason = String(reason || row.reason || "dirty");
        row.turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
        return 0;
      }
    }

    cards.dirtyQueue.push({
      key: key,
      family: cleanFamily,
      id: cleanId,
      namespace: this.makeNamespace(cleanFamily, cleanId, root),
      reason: String(reason || "dirty"),
      turn: root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      payloadHash: this.stableHash(payload == null ? "" : JSON.stringify(payload))
    });

    while (cards.dirtyQueue.length > cards.config.maxQueueSize) cards.dirtyQueue.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  queueDirtyFromMeta(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const dirty = root.meta && root.meta.dirty && typeof root.meta.dirty === "object" ? root.meta.dirty : {};
    let changes = 0;

    if (dirty.player === true) changes += this.queueDirty("player", "summary", "player_dirty", null, root);
    if (dirty.reputation === true) changes += this.queueDirty("reputation", "summary", "reputation_dirty", null, root);
    if (dirty.quests === true) changes += this.queueDirty("quest", "summary", "quests_dirty", null, root);
    if (dirty.time === true) changes += this.queueDirty("time", "summary", "time_dirty", null, root);

    const arrayMappings = [
      { key: "abilities", family: "ability" },
      { key: "items", family: "item" },
      { key: "actors", family: "actor" },
      { key: "entities", family: "entity" },
      { key: "routes", family: "route" }
    ];

    for (let m = 0; m < arrayMappings.length; m += 1) {
      const map = arrayMappings[m];
      const list = Array.isArray(dirty[map.key]) ? dirty[map.key] : [];
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length && i < 24; i += 1) {
        changes += this.queueDirty(map.family, list[i], map.key + "_dirty", null, root);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },


  processDirtyQueue(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards || !Array.isArray(cards.dirtyQueue) || !cards.dirtyQueue.length) return 0;

    const max = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(cards.config && cards.config.maxWritesPerTurn ? cards.config.maxWritesPerTurn : 2)));
    let processed = 0;
    let scheduled = 0;
    const remaining = [];

    while (cards.dirtyQueue.length) {
      const row = cards.dirtyQueue.shift();
      if (!row || typeof row !== "object") continue;

      if (processed >= max) {
        remaining.push(row);
        continue;
      }

      const spec = this.buildCardSpecForDirtyTarget(row, root);
      processed += 1;

      if (spec && spec.namespace) {
        this.safeEnsureSingletonCard(spec, root);
        this.clearDirtySourceForRow(row, root);
        scheduled += 1;
      } else {
        this.recordOperation("processDirtyQueue", {
          namespace: String(row.namespace || ""),
          mode: "skipped",
          message: "no serializable canon state for " + String(row.family || "unknown") + ":" + String(row.id || "unknown")
        }, root);
      }
    }

    while (remaining.length) {
      cards.dirtyQueue.unshift(remaining.pop());
    }

    if (scheduled > 0) {
      cards.lastSyncTurn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    }

    return scheduled;
  },

  clearDirtySourceForRow(row, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || !row || typeof row !== "object") return 0;

    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils;
    if (U && typeof U.ensureDirtyShape === "function") {
      U.ensureDirtyShape(root);
    }

    const dirty = root.meta && root.meta.dirty && typeof root.meta.dirty === "object" ? root.meta.dirty : null;
    if (!dirty) return 0;

    const family = String(row.family || "").toLowerCase();
    const id = String(row.id || "").trim();
    const cleanId = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(id) : id;
    let changes = 0;

    const clearBoolean = (key) => {
      if (dirty[key] === true) {
        dirty[key] = false;
        changes += 1;
      }
    };

    const clearArrayValue = (key, value) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(dirty[key])) return;

      const before = dirty[key].length;
      const target = String(value || "");
      const targetKey = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(target) : target;

      dirty[key] = dirty[key].filter(function (entry) {
        const entryText = String(entry || "");
        const entryKey = U && typeof U.toKey === "function" ? U.toKey(entryText) : entryText;
        return entryText !== target && entryKey !== targetKey;
      });

      if (dirty[key].length !== before) {
        changes += 1;
      }
    };

    if (family === "player") {
      clearBoolean("player");
    } else if (family === "reputation") {
      clearBoolean("reputation");
    } else if (family === "quest") {
      clearBoolean("quests");
    } else if (family === "time") {
      clearBoolean("time");
    } else if (family === "ability") {
      clearArrayValue("abilities", cleanId || id);
    } else if (family === "item") {
      clearArrayValue("items", cleanId || id);
    } else if (family === "actor") {
      clearArrayValue("actors", cleanId || id);
    } else if (family === "entity") {
      clearArrayValue("entities", cleanId || id);
    } else if (family === "route") {
      clearArrayValue("routes", cleanId || id);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  buildCardSpecForDirtyTarget(row, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const target = row && typeof row === "object" ? row : {};
    const family = String(target.family || "").toLowerCase();
    const id = String(target.id || "summary");

    if (!family) return null;

    if (family === "player") return this.serializePlayerCard(root);
    if (family === "reputation") return this.serializeReputationCard(root);
    if (family === "quest") return this.serializeQuestCard(root);
    if (family === "time") return this.serializeTimeCard(root);
    if (family === "ability") return this.serializeAbilityCard(id, root);
    if (family === "item") return this.serializeItemCard(id, root);
    if (family === "actor") return this.serializeActorCard(id, root);
    if (family === "entity") return this.serializeEntityCard(id, root);
    if (family === "route") return this.serializeRouteCard(id, root);

    return null;
  },

  serializePlayerCard(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const lines = [];

    if (A.PlayerSystem && typeof A.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      lines.push(A.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary(root.player));
    }
    if (A.BuildIdentitySystem && typeof A.BuildIdentitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      lines.push(A.BuildIdentitySystem.getCompactSummary(root.build));
    }
    if (A.AbilitySystem && typeof A.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const abilityLine = A.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary(root, 3);
      if (abilityLine) lines.push(abilityLine);
    }
    if (A.PersistentTraitSystem && typeof A.PersistentTraitSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const traitLine = A.PersistentTraitSystem.getCompactSummary(root, 3);
      if (traitLine) lines.push(traitLine);
    }
    if (A.InventorySystem && typeof A.InventorySystem.getVisibleCarrySummary === "function") {
      lines.push("Visible Carry: " + A.InventorySystem.getVisibleCarrySummary(root));
    }
    if (A.SceneStateSystem && typeof A.SceneStateSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      lines.push(A.SceneStateSystem.getCompactSummary(root));
    }

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("player", "summary", "Player Summary", lines, root);
  },

  serializeReputationCard(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const lines = [];

    if (A.ReputationSystem && typeof A.ReputationSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const summary = A.ReputationSystem.getCompactSummary(root, "wide");
      if (summary) lines.push(summary);
    }

    const reputation = root.reputation || {};
    const rumors = Array.isArray(reputation.rumors) ? reputation.rumors : [];
    if (rumors.length) {
      const recent = rumors.slice(Math.max(0, rumors.length - 3)).map(function (r) {
        return r && typeof r === "object" ? (r.label || r.text || "") : String(r || "");
      }).filter(Boolean);
      if (recent.length) lines.push("Recent Rumors: " + recent.join(" | "));
    }

    if (!lines.length) return null;
    return this.serializeSummaryCard("reputation", "summary", "Reputation Summary", lines, root);
  },

  serializeQuestCard(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const lines = [];

    if (A.QuestLogSystem && typeof A.QuestLogSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const summary = A.QuestLogSystem.getCompactSummary(root, "wide");
      if (summary) lines.push(summary);
    }

    const quests = root.quests || {};
    const archived = Array.isArray(quests.archived) ? quests.archived : [];
    if (archived.length) {
      const last = archived[archived.length - 1] || {};
      if (last.title) lines.push("Last Archived: " + last.title + " (" + (last.status || "archived") + ")");
    }

    if (!lines.length) return null;
    return this.serializeSummaryCard("quest", "summary", "Quest Summary", lines, root);
  },

  serializeTimeCard(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const lines = [];

    if (A.TimeTravelSystem && typeof A.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const summary = A.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary(root);
      if (summary) lines.push(summary);
    }

    if (!lines.length) return null;
    return this.serializeSummaryCard("time", "summary", "Time Summary", lines, root);
  },

  serializeAbilityCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const abilities = root.abilities || {};
    const byId = abilities.byId || {};
    let record = byId[id];

    if ((!record || typeof record !== "object") && id === "summary") {
      const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
      const summary = A.AbilitySystem && typeof A.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
        ? A.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary(root, 5)
        : "";
      return summary ? this.serializeSummaryCard("ability", "summary", "Ability Summary", [summary], root) : null;
    }

    if (!record || typeof record !== "object") return null;

    const identity = record.identity || {};
    const meaning = record.meaning || {};
    const progression = record.progression || {};
    const observation = record.observation || {};
    const name = identity.name || id;
    const lines = [
      "Name: " + name,
      "Type: " + (identity.type || identity.kind || "ability"),
      "Mastery: " + (progression.masteryState || "novice")
    ];

    if (meaning.intendedFunction) lines.push("Function: " + meaning.intendedFunction);
    if (Array.isArray(meaning.keyPhrases) && meaning.keyPhrases.length) lines.push("Phrases: " + meaning.keyPhrases.slice(0, 5).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(observation.semanticTags) && observation.semanticTags.length) lines.push("Observed: " + observation.semanticTags.slice(0, 5).join(", "));

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("ability", id, name, lines, root);
  },

  serializeItemCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const items = root.items || {};
    const byId = items.byId || {};

    if (id === "summary" || id === "currency") {
      const owned = items.ownership && Array.isArray(items.ownership.player) ? items.ownership.player : [];
      const names = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < owned.length && names.length < 8; i += 1) {
        const item = byId[owned[i]];
        if (!item || typeof item !== "object") continue;
        names.push(item.displayName || item.baseType || owned[i]);
      }
      const currency = items.currency && items.currency.byType ? items.currency.byType : {};
      const lines = [
        "Owned Items: " + (names.length ? names.join(", ") : "none"),
        "Currency: copper " + (currency.copper || 0) + ", silver " + (currency.silver || 0) + ", gold " + (currency.gold || 0)
      ];
      return this.serializeSummaryCard("item", "summary", "Inventory Summary", lines, root);
    }

    const item = byId[id];
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return null;

    const display = item.displayName || item.baseType || id;
    const condition = item.condition || {};
    const ownership = item.ownership || {};
    const lines = [
      "Item: " + display,
      "Base: " + (item.baseType || "item"),
      "Quantity: " + (item.quantity || 1),
      "Holder: " + (ownership.holder || "player"),
      "Visibility: " + (ownership.visibility || "packed")
    ];

    if (item.quality) lines.push("Quality: " + item.quality);
    if (Array.isArray(item.descriptors) && item.descriptors.length) lines.push("Descriptors: " + item.descriptors.slice(0, 5).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(condition.stateTags) && condition.stateTags.length) lines.push("Condition: " + condition.stateTags.slice(0, 5).join(", "));

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("item", id, display, lines, root);
  },

  serializeActorCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const byId = actors.byId || {};
    const actor = byId[id];
    if (!actor || typeof actor !== "object") return null;

    const identity = actor.identity || actor;
    const mutable = actor.mutable || {};
    const name = identity.displayName || identity.name || id;
    const lines = ["Actor: " + name];
    if (identity.kind) lines.push("Kind: " + identity.kind);
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.tags) && mutable.tags.length) lines.push("Tags: " + mutable.tags.slice(0, 8).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.relationshipTags) && mutable.relationshipTags.length) lines.push("Relations: " + mutable.relationshipTags.slice(0, 6).join(", "));

    if (globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem.getMemorySummaryForActor === "function") {
      const memoryLine = globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem.getMemorySummaryForActor(id, root, "card");
      if (memoryLine) lines.push(memoryLine);
    }

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("actor", id, name, lines, root);
  },

  serializeEntityCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const world = root.world || {};
    const entities = world.entitiesById || {};
    const locations = world.locationsById || {};
    const record = entities[id] || locations[id];
    if (!record || typeof record !== "object") return null;

    const identity = record.identity || {};
    const mutable = record.mutable || {};
    const links = record.links || {};
    const name = identity.displayName || id;
    const lines = [
      "Entity: " + name,
      "Kind: " + (identity.kind || "entity")
    ];

    if (links.parentLocationRef) lines.push("Parent: " + links.parentLocationRef);
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.conditionTags) && mutable.conditionTags.length) lines.push("State: " + mutable.conditionTags.slice(0, 8).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.hazardTags) && mutable.hazardTags.length) lines.push("Hazards: " + mutable.hazardTags.slice(0, 8).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.exitHints) && mutable.exitHints.length) lines.push("Exits: " + mutable.exitHints.slice(0, 8).join(", "));

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("entity", id, name, lines, root);
  },

  serializeRouteCard(id, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;
    const world = root.world || {};
    const routes = world.routesById || {};
    const route = routes[id];
    if (!route || typeof route !== "object") return null;

    const identity = route.identity || {};
    const mutable = route.mutable || {};
    const name = identity.id || id;
    const lines = [
      "Route: " + name,
      "From: " + (mutable.fromRef || "unknown"),
      "To: " + (mutable.toRef || "unknown")
    ];
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.travelHints) && mutable.travelHints.length) lines.push("Travel: " + mutable.travelHints.slice(0, 5).join(", "));
    if (Array.isArray(mutable.hazardTags) && mutable.hazardTags.length) lines.push("Hazards: " + mutable.hazardTags.slice(0, 5).join(", "));

    return this.serializeSummaryCard("route", id, name, lines, root);
  },

  clearDirtyQueue(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const cards = this.init(root);
    if (!cards) return 0;
    const count = cards.dirtyQueue.length;
    cards.dirtyQueue = [];
    return count;
  },

  serializeSummaryCard(family, id, displayName, lines, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const safeLines = [];
    if (Array.isArray(lines)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < lines.length && i < 12; i += 1) {
        const line = this.cleanCardText(lines[i], 180);
        if (line) safeLines.push(line);
      }
    }

    return this.makeCardSpec(family, id, displayName, safeLines.join("\n"), [displayName], root);
  }
},

    // === CONTEXT PACKET SYSTEM ===
ContextPacketSystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return null;

    if (!root.cache || typeof root.cache !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.cache)) {
      root.cache = {};
    }

    if (!root.meta || typeof root.meta !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.meta)) {
      root.meta = {};
    }

    if (typeof root.meta.mode !== "string" || !root.meta.mode) {
      root.meta.mode = "normal";
    }

    if (typeof root.cache.scenePacket !== "string") root.cache.scenePacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.playerPacket !== "string") root.cache.playerPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.timePacket !== "string") root.cache.timePacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.actorPacket !== "string") root.cache.actorPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.reputationPacket !== "string") root.cache.reputationPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.questPacket !== "string") root.cache.questPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.worldPacket !== "string") root.cache.worldPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.contextPacket !== "string") root.cache.contextPacket = "";
    if (typeof root.cache.packetMode !== "string") root.cache.packetMode = "normal";

    return root.cache;
  },

  buildContext(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return String(rawText || "");
    this.init(root);

    const mode = this.resolveMode(rawText, root);
    root.meta.mode = mode;
    root.cache.packetMode = mode;

    this.refreshCache(mode, root);

    const packetText = this.composePacketText(mode, rawText, root);
    if (!packetText) {
      return String(rawText || "");
    }

    return packetText + "\n\n" + String(rawText || "");
  },

  resolveMode(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const preferred = root.meta && typeof root.meta.mode === "string"
      ? root.meta.mode.trim().toLowerCase()
      : "";

    if (preferred === "tiny" || preferred === "normal" || preferred === "wide") {
      return preferred;
    }

    const maxChars = this.getMaxChars();
    if (maxChars <= 1400) return "tiny";
    if (maxChars >= 6000) return "wide";
    return "normal";
  },

  getMaxChars() {
    if (typeof info === "object" && info && typeof info.maxChars === "number" && Number.isFinite(info.maxChars)) {
      return Math.max(800, Math.floor(Math.abs(info.maxChars)));
    }
    return 2000;
  },

  getBudget(mode, rawText) {
    const maxChars = this.getMaxChars();
    const baseLen = String(rawText || "").length;
    const freeSpace = Math.max(120, maxChars - baseLen);

    let ratio = 0.14;
    let hardMin = 160;
    let hardMax = 420;

    if (mode === "tiny") {
      ratio = 0.09;
      hardMin = 120;
      hardMax = 220;
    } else if (mode === "wide") {
      ratio = 0.24;
      hardMin = 260;
      hardMax = 760;
    }

    let budget = Math.floor(maxChars * ratio);
    if (budget < hardMin) budget = hardMin;
    if (budget > hardMax) budget = hardMax;

    const freeCap = Math.max(120, Math.floor(freeSpace * 0.5));
    if (budget > freeCap) budget = freeCap;

    return budget;
  },

  refreshCache(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return;
    this.init(root);

    root.cache.scenePacket = this.makeScenePacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.playerPacket = this.makePlayerPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.timePacket = this.makeTimePacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.actorPacket = this.makeActorPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.reputationPacket = this.makeReputationPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.questPacket = this.makeQuestPacket(mode, root);
    root.cache.worldPacket = this.makeWorldPacket(mode, root);
  },

  composePacketText(mode, rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return "";

    const budget = this.getBudget(mode, rawText);
    const packets = this.getPacketPriorityList(mode, root);

    const lines = ["Current truth:"];
    let used = lines[0].length;

    for (let i = 0; i < packets.length; i += 1) {
      const text = String(packets[i] || "").trim();
      if (!text) continue;

      const nextLen = used + 1 + text.length;
      if (nextLen > budget) {
        continue;
      }

      lines.push(text);
      used = nextLen;
    }

    if (lines.length === 1) {
      return "";
    }

    const out = lines.join("\n");
    root.cache.contextPacket = out;
    return out;
  },

  getPacketPriorityList(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const packets = [];
    const pushIf = (value) => {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (text) packets.push(text);
    };

    const actorPacket = root && root.cache ? String(root.cache.actorPacket || "").trim() : "";
    const hasMajorActorMemory = /\bMajor actor memory\b/i.test(actorPacket);

    pushIf(root.cache.scenePacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.playerPacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.timePacket);

    // C25u1 safety: major actor memory is current-turn guidance, not just flavor.
    // Put it ahead of reputation/quest packets when it exists so it is not
    // dropped by the normal context budget after the older packets fill space.
    if (hasMajorActorMemory) {
      pushIf(actorPacket);
    }

    pushIf(root.cache.reputationPacket);
    pushIf(root.cache.questPacket);

    if (!hasMajorActorMemory) {
      pushIf(actorPacket);
    }

    if (mode === "wide") {
      pushIf(root.cache.worldPacket);
    }

    return packets;
  },

  makeScenePacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : null;
  if (!scene || typeof scene !== "object") {
    return "";
  }

  const world = root.world || {};
  const locationsById = world.locationsById || {};

  const parts = [];
  const name = scene.displayName && String(scene.displayName).trim()
    ? String(scene.displayName).trim()
    : "unknown place";

  parts.push("Scene: " + name);

  if (scene.parentLocationRef && locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef] && locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef].identity) {
    const locationName = locationsById[scene.parentLocationRef].identity.displayName || scene.parentLocationRef;
    parts.push("Location: " + locationName);
  }

  if (scene.sceneType) {
    parts.push("Type: " + scene.sceneType);
  }

  const factLimit = mode === "tiny" ? 3 : (mode === "wide" ? 6 : 4);
  const facts = [];

  facts.push.apply(facts, this.takeDistinct(scene.conditionTags, factLimit));
  facts.push.apply(facts, this.takeDistinct(scene.localHazards, factLimit));
  facts.push.apply(facts, this.takeDistinct(scene.localExits, factLimit));

  const trimmedFacts = this.takeDistinct(facts, factLimit);
  if (trimmedFacts.length) {
    parts.push("Facts: " + trimmedFacts.join(", "));
  }

  if (mode !== "tiny" && scene.localEntityIds && scene.localEntityIds.length && scene.localEntitiesById) {
    const names = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < scene.localEntityIds.length && names.length < 3; i += 1) {
      const ref = scene.localEntityIds[i];
      if (scene.localEntitiesById[ref]) {
        names.push(scene.localEntitiesById[ref]);
      }
    }
    if (names.length) {
      parts.push("Objects: " + names.join(", "));
    }
  }

  return parts.join(" | ");
},

  makePlayerPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const playerSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.PlayerSystem.getCompactSummary(root.player)
    : "";

  const visibleCarry = globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem.getVisibleCarrySummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.InventorySystem.getVisibleCarrySummary(root)
    : "";

  const buildSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.BuildIdentitySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.BuildIdentitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.BuildIdentitySystem.getCompactSummary(root.build)
    : "";

  const abilityFocus = globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getRelevantContextHint === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getRelevantContextHint(root)
    : "";

  const abilitySummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.AbilitySystem.getCompactSummary(root, mode === "wide" ? 3 : 1)
    : "";

  const traitSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.PersistentTraitSystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.PersistentTraitSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.PersistentTraitSystem.getCompactSummary(root, mode === "wide" ? 3 : 1)
    : "";

  const specializationHint = globalThis.AIDRPG.SpecializationSystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.SpecializationSystem.getRelevantContextHint === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.SpecializationSystem.getRelevantContextHint(root, mode)
    : "";

  const parts = [];

  if (playerSummary) {
    parts.push("Player: " + playerSummary);
  }

  if (visibleCarry && visibleCarry !== "nothing notable visible") {
    parts.push("Visible gear: " + visibleCarry);
  }

  if (abilityFocus) {
    parts.push(abilityFocus);
  } else if (mode === "wide" && abilitySummary) {
    parts.push(abilitySummary);
  }

  if (specializationHint) {
    parts.push(specializationHint);
  }

  if (traitSummary) {
    parts.push("Traits: " + traitSummary);
  }

  if (mode === "wide" && buildSummary && buildSummary !== "Build summary unavailable.") {
    parts.push(buildSummary);
  }

  return parts.join(" | ");
},

  makeTimePacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const summary = globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem &&
      typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
      ? globalThis.AIDRPG.TimeTravelSystem.getCompactSummary(root)
      : "";

    if (!summary || summary === "Time unknown.") {
      return "";
    }

    return "Time: " + summary;
  },

  makeActorPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
  const actorSummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.ActorProfileSystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.ActorProfileSystem.getContextSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.ActorProfileSystem.getContextSummary(root, mode)
    : "";

  const memorySummary = globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem &&
    typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem.getContextSummary === "function"
    ? globalThis.AIDRPG.MajorActorMemorySystem.getContextSummary(root, mode)
    : "";

  const parts = [];
  if (actorSummary) parts.push(actorSummary);
  if (memorySummary) parts.push(memorySummary);

  return parts.join(" | ");
},

  makeReputationPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const summary = globalThis.AIDRPG.ReputationSystem &&
      typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.ReputationSystem.getCompactSummary === "function"
      ? globalThis.AIDRPG.ReputationSystem.getCompactSummary(root, mode)
      : "";

    return summary || "";
  },

  makeQuestPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (globalThis.AIDRPG.QuestLogSystem && typeof globalThis.AIDRPG.QuestLogSystem.getCompactSummary === "function") {
      const summary = globalThis.AIDRPG.QuestLogSystem.getCompactSummary(root, mode);
      if (summary) return summary;
    }

    const quests = root.quests || {};
    const active = Array.isArray(quests.active) ? quests.active : [];
    const tracking = quests.tracking && typeof quests.tracking === "object" ? quests.tracking : {};

    let quest = null;

    if (tracking.primaryQuestId) {
      for (let i = 0; i < active.length; i += 1) {
        if (active[i] && active[i].id === tracking.primaryQuestId) {
          quest = active[i];
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!quest && active.length) {
      quest = active[0];
    }

    if (!quest || typeof quest !== "object") {
      return "";
    }

    const title = quest.title ? String(quest.title).trim() : "active task";
    const status = quest.status ? String(quest.status).trim() : "";
    const objective = quest.objective ? String(quest.objective).trim() : "";

    const parts = ["Quest: " + title];

    if (status) {
      parts.push("Status: " + status);
    }

    if (mode !== "tiny" && objective) {
      parts.push("Goal: " + objective);
    }

    return parts.join(" | ");
  },

  makeWorldPacket(mode, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (mode !== "wide") return "";

    const scene = root.world && root.world.currentScene ? root.world.currentScene : null;
    const world = root.world || {};
    if (!scene || !world.entitiesById) return "";

    const entityRef = scene.entityRef || "";
    const entity = entityRef && world.entitiesById[entityRef] ? world.entitiesById[entityRef] : null;
    if (!entity || typeof entity !== "object") {
      return "";
    }

    const parts = [];

    if (entity.mutable && Array.isArray(entity.mutable.materialHints) && entity.mutable.materialHints.length) {
      parts.push("Materials: " + entity.mutable.materialHints.join(", "));
    }

    if (entity.mutable && Array.isArray(entity.mutable.ownerHints) && entity.mutable.ownerHints.length) {
      parts.push("Owners: " + entity.mutable.ownerHints.join(", "));
    }

    if (entity.mutable && Array.isArray(entity.mutable.districtHints) && entity.mutable.districtHints.length) {
      parts.push("District: " + entity.mutable.districtHints.join(", "));
    }

    return parts.length ? ("Linked detail: " + parts.join(" | ")) : "";
  },

  takeDistinct(values, maxCount) {
    const out = [];
    const list = Array.isArray(values) ? values : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < list.length && out.length < (maxCount || 4); i += 1) {
      const clean = String(list[i] || "").trim();
      if (!clean) continue;
      if (out.indexOf(clean) === -1) {
        out.push(clean);
      }
    }

    return out;
  }
},

    // === MAJOR ACTOR MEMORY SYSTEM ===
    MajorActorMemorySystem: {
  init(root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object" || Array.isArray(root)) return null;

    if (!root.actors || typeof root.actors !== "object" || Array.isArray(root.actors)) {
      root.actors = {};
    }

    const actors = root.actors;

    if (!actors.majorMemory || typeof actors.majorMemory !== "object" || Array.isArray(actors.majorMemory)) {
      actors.majorMemory = {};
    }

    const memory = actors.majorMemory;

    if (!memory.byActorId || typeof memory.byActorId !== "object" || Array.isArray(memory.byActorId)) memory.byActorId = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(memory.activeIds)) memory.activeIds = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(memory.recentEvents)) memory.recentEvents = [];
    if (!memory.config || typeof memory.config !== "object" || Array.isArray(memory.config)) memory.config = {};

    const config = memory.config;
    if (typeof config.maxMajorActors !== "number") config.maxMajorActors = 12;
    if (typeof config.maxGoals !== "number") config.maxGoals = 6;
    if (typeof config.maxGrudges !== "number") config.maxGrudges = 6;
    if (typeof config.maxNotes !== "number") config.maxNotes = 8;
    if (typeof config.maxEvents !== "number") config.maxEvents = 40;
    if (typeof config.activationSightings !== "number") config.activationSightings = 5;

    return memory;
  },

  processOutput(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== "object") return 0;
    this.init(root);

    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const U = A.Utils;
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw.trim()) return 0;

    if (A.TurnEngine && typeof A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput === "function") {
      if (A.TurnEngine.isDuplicateCurrentOutput()) return 0;
    }

    let changes = 0;
    changes += this.discoverNamedMajorActors(raw, root);
    changes += this.syncMajorActors(root);
    changes += this.applyMemoryFromText(raw, root);
    changes += this.rebuildActiveIds(raw, root);
    changes += this.pruneMajorMemory(root);

    if (changes > 0) {
      if (U && typeof U.invalidatePackets === "function") U.invalidatePackets(root);
      if (U && typeof U.logEvent === "function") U.logEvent("Major actor memory updated.");
    }

    return changes;
  },

  discoverNamedMajorActors(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const A = globalThis.AIDRPG || {};
    const ActorSystem = A.ActorProfileSystem;
    if (!ActorSystem || typeof ActorSystem.findOrCreateRecord !== "function") return 0;

    const patterns = [
      /\b(Captain|Commander|Guildmaster|Guild Master|Lord|Lady|Sir|Dame|Priest|Priestess|Mage|Wizard|Knight|Hunter|Merchant|Barkeep|Innkeeper|Blacksmith|Smith)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30})(?:\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z'’-]{1,30}))?\b/g
    ];

    let changes = 0;
    for (let p = 0; p < patterns.length; p += 1) {
      let match;
      while ((match = patterns[p].exec(raw))) {
        const title = this.cleanShortText(match[1] || "");
        const first = this.cleanShortText(match[2] || "");
        const last = this.cleanShortText(match[3] || "");
        const name = this.cleanShortText([title, first, last].filter(Boolean).join(" "));
        if (!name || this.isBannedActorLabel(name)) continue;

        const sceneKey = A.Utils && typeof A.Utils.toKey === "function" ? A.Utils.toKey(name) : name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "_");
        if (!sceneKey) continue;

        const roleTags = typeof ActorSystem.inferRoleTags === "function" ? ActorSystem.inferRoleTags(name) : [];
        const rankTags = typeof ActorSystem.inferRankTags === "function" ? ActorSystem.inferRankTags(name) : [];
        const category = typeof ActorSystem.inferCategory === "function" ? ActorSystem.inferCategory(name) : "humanoid";

        const candidate = {
          sceneKey: sceneKey,
          displayName: name,
          roleTags: roleTags,
          rankTags: rankTags.length ? rankTags : ["leader"],
          category: category,
          importanceScore: 5,
          persistent: true,
          major: true
        };

        const record = ActorSystem.findOrCreateRecord(candidate, root);
        if (!record || !record.identity || !record.identity.id) continue;

        if (typeof ActorSystem.applyCandidateToRecord === "function") {
          changes += ActorSystem.applyCandidateToRecord(candidate, record, root) || 0;
        }

        changes += this.ensureMemoryForActor(record.identity.id, "named_major_actor", root) ? 1 : 0;
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  syncMajorActors(root = state.aidrpg) {
    this.init(root);

    const actors = root.actors || {};
    const byId = actors.byId || {};
    const allIds = Array.isArray(actors.allIds) ? actors.allIds : [];
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < allIds.length; i += 1) {
      const actorId = allIds[i];
      const actor = byId[actorId];
      if (!actor || typeof actor !== "object") continue;

      if (this.shouldActorHaveMemory(actor, root)) {
        changes += this.ensureMemoryForActor(actorId, "profile_major_actor", root) ? 1 : 0;
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  shouldActorHaveMemory(actor, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!actor || typeof actor !== "object") return false;

    const identity = actor.identity || {};
    const name = this.getActorName(actor);
    if (!name || this.isBannedActorLabel(name)) return false;

    const importance = actor.importance || {};
    const score = Number(importance.score || 0);
    const sightings = Number(importance.sightings || 0);
    const rankTags = Array.isArray(actor.rankTags) ? actor.rankTags : [];
    const roleTags = Array.isArray(actor.roleTags) ? actor.roleTags : [];

    if (importance.major === true) return true;
    if (score >= 4) return true;
    if (sightings >= this.getConfig(root).activationSightings) return true;

    for (let i = 0; i < rankTags.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = String(rankTags[i] || "").toLowerCase();
      if (tag === "leader" || tag === "elite" || tag === "veteran") return true;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < roleTags.length; i += 1) {
      const tag = String(roleTags[i] || "").toLowerCase();
      if (tag === "captain" || tag === "commander" || tag === "guildmaster") return true;
    }

    if (/\b(Captain|Commander|Guildmaster|Lord|Lady|Sir|Dame)\b/.test(String(identity.name || identity.displayName || ""))) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  ensureMemoryForActor(actorId, reason, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return false;

    const id = String(actorId || "").trim();
    if (!id) return false;

    const actor = root.actors && root.actors.byId ? root.actors.byId[id] : null;
    const name = actor ? this.getActorName(actor) : id;
    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;

    if (!memory.byActorId[id] || typeof memory.byActorId[id] !== "object") {
      memory.byActorId[id] = {
        actorId: id,
        name: name,
        createdTurn: turn,
        lastSeenTurn: turn,
        lastUpdatedTurn: turn,
        relationshipStance: "neutral",
        goals: [],
        grudges: [],
        attitudeNotes: [],
        longTermNotes: [],
        recentEvents: [],
        activation: {
          reason: String(reason || "major_actor"),
          activationCount: 0
        }
      };

      this.pushRecentEvent("activated", name, id, String(reason || "major_actor"), root);
      this.markActorDirty(id, root);
      return true;
    }

    const rec = memory.byActorId[id];
    let changed = false;
    if (rec.name !== name && name) {
      rec.name = name;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!rec.activation || typeof rec.activation !== "object") {
      rec.activation = { reason: String(reason || "major_actor"), activationCount: 0 };
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.goals)) { rec.goals = []; changed = true; }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.grudges)) { rec.grudges = []; changed = true; }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.attitudeNotes)) { rec.attitudeNotes = []; changed = true; }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.longTermNotes)) { rec.longTermNotes = []; changed = true; }
    if (!Array.isArray(rec.recentEvents)) { rec.recentEvents = []; changed = true; }
    if (typeof rec.relationshipStance !== "string") { rec.relationshipStance = "neutral"; changed = true; }

    if (changed) {
      rec.lastUpdatedTurn = turn;
      this.markActorDirty(id, root);
    }

    return changed;
  },

  applyMemoryFromText(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return 0;

    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const actorIds = Object.keys(memory.byActorId || {});
    let changes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < actorIds.length; i += 1) {
      const actorId = actorIds[i];
      const actor = root.actors && root.actors.byId ? root.actors.byId[actorId] : null;
      const rec = memory.byActorId[actorId];
      if (!rec || typeof rec !== "object") continue;

      const referenced = this.isActorReferencedInText(actor, rec, raw);
      const local = this.isActorCurrentlyLocal(actorId, root);
      if (!referenced && !local) continue;

      changes += this.touchMemory(rec, actorId, referenced ? "mentioned" : "local", root);
      if (!referenced) continue;

      const candidates = this.extractMemoryCandidatesForActor(raw, actor, rec, root);
      for (let c = 0; c < candidates.length; c += 1) {
        changes += this.applyMemoryCandidate(actorId, candidates[c], root);
      }
    }

    return changes;
  },

  extractMemoryCandidatesForActor(rawText, actor, rec, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const labels = this.getActorReferenceLabels(actor, rec);
    const out = [];

    const add = (kind, text, confidence) => {
      const clean = this.cleanShortText(text);
      if (!kind || !clean) return;
      out.push({ kind: kind, text: clean, confidence: Number(confidence || 0.75) });
    };

    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 1) {
      const label = labels[i];
      const reLabel = this.escapeRegExp(label);
      if (!reLabel) continue;

      let match;
      const goalPatterns = [
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b(?:wants|intends|plans|hopes|needs|tries|is determined)\\s+to\\s+([^.!?;,]{3,100})", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,24}\\b(?:goal|aim|plan)\\s+(?:is|becomes)\\s+to\\s+([^.!?;,]{3,100})", "i")
      ];

      for (let g = 0; g < goalPatterns.length; g += 1) {
        match = raw.match(goalPatterns[g]);
        if (match && match[1]) add("goal", match[1], 0.82);
      }

      const grudgePatterns = [
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b(?:swears|vows|promises)\\s+(?:revenge|to make you pay|to remember this)", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b(?:holds|bears|keeps)\\s+(?:a\\s+)?grudge", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b(?:will not forget|won't forget)\\s+(?:what you did|this)", "i")
      ];

      for (let gr = 0; gr < grudgePatterns.length; gr += 1) {
        match = raw.match(grudgePatterns[gr]);
        if (match) add("grudge", match[0], 0.84);
      }

      const positiveRe = new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b(?:trusts|respects|thanks|supports|protects|is grateful to|is loyal to)\\s+you\\b", "i");
      const negativeRe = new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,36}\\b(?:fears|hates|distrusts|resents|suspects|is wary of|is suspicious of|is hostile to)\\s+you\\b", "i");
      const playerEarnedRe = new RegExp("\\byou\\s+(?:earn|earned|gain|gained)\\s+" + reLabel + "'?s?\\s+(trust|respect|gratitude|fear|hatred)\\b", "i");

      match = raw.match(positiveRe);
      if (match) add("relationship", "friendly: " + match[0], 0.86);

      match = raw.match(negativeRe);
      if (match) add("relationship", "hostile: " + match[0], 0.86);

      match = raw.match(playerEarnedRe);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        const value = String(match[1] || "").toLowerCase();
        const stance = value === "fear" || value === "hatred" ? "hostile" : "friendly";
        add("relationship", stance + ": " + match[0], 0.84);
      }

      const notePatterns = [
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:remembers|recognizes|owes|promises|warns|forgives|blames)\\b[^.!?]{0,80}", "i"),
        new RegExp("\\b" + reLabel + "\\b[^.!?]{0,48}\\b(?:asks you to return|offers to help|refuses to help|keeps your secret)\\b[^.!?]{0,80}", "i")
      ];

      for (let n = 0; n < notePatterns.length; n += 1) {
        match = raw.match(notePatterns[n]);
        if (match) add("note", match[0], 0.76);
      }
    }

    return this.dedupeCandidates(out);
  },

  applyMemoryCandidate(actorId, candidate, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory || !candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return 0;

    const rec = memory.byActorId[String(actorId || "")];
    if (!rec) return 0;

    const kind = String(candidate.kind || "note").toLowerCase();
    const text = this.cleanShortText(candidate.text || "");
    if (!text) return 0;

    let changes = 0;

    if (kind === "goal") {
      changes += this.pushCappedUnique(rec.goals, text, this.getConfig(root).maxGoals);
    } else if (kind === "grudge") {
      changes += this.pushCappedUnique(rec.grudges, text, this.getConfig(root).maxGrudges);
      if (rec.relationshipStance !== "hostile") {
        rec.relationshipStance = "hostile";
        changes += 1;
      }
    } else if (kind === "relationship") {
      const stance = text.indexOf("hostile:") === 0 ? "hostile" : text.indexOf("friendly:") === 0 ? "friendly" : "mixed";
      if (rec.relationshipStance !== stance) {
        rec.relationshipStance = stance;
        changes += 1;
      }
      changes += this.pushCappedUnique(rec.attitudeNotes, text.replace(/^(?:friendly|hostile|mixed):\s*/i, ""), this.getConfig(root).maxNotes);
    } else {
      changes += this.pushCappedUnique(rec.attitudeNotes, text, this.getConfig(root).maxNotes);
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
      rec.lastUpdatedTurn = turn;
      this.pushCappedUnique(rec.recentEvents, kind + ": " + text, 8);
      this.pushRecentEvent(kind, rec.name || actorId, actorId, text, root);
      this.markActorDirty(actorId, root);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  rebuildActiveIds(rawText, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return 0;

    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    const byActorId = memory.byActorId || {};
    const ids = Object.keys(byActorId);
    const active = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
      const id = ids[i];
      const actor = root.actors && root.actors.byId ? root.actors.byId[id] : null;
      const rec = byActorId[id];
      if (!rec) continue;

      if (this.isActorCurrentlyLocal(id, root) || this.isActorReferencedInText(actor, rec, raw)) {
        active.push(id);
      }
    }

    active.sort((a, b) => {
      const left = byActorId[a] || {};
      const right = byActorId[b] || {};
      return Number(right.lastSeenTurn || 0) - Number(left.lastSeenTurn || 0);
    });

    return this.replaceArray(memory.activeIds, active.slice(0, 4));
  },

  touchMemory(rec, actorId, reason, root = state.aidrpg) {
    if (!rec || typeof rec !== "object") return 0;

    const turn = root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0;
    let changes = 0;

    if (rec.lastSeenTurn !== turn) {
      rec.lastSeenTurn = turn;
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (!rec.activation || typeof rec.activation !== "object") {
      rec.activation = { reason: String(reason || "seen"), activationCount: 0 };
      changes += 1;
    }

    rec.activation.activationCount = Math.max(0, Number(rec.activation.activationCount || 0)) + 1;

    if (changes > 0) {
      this.markActorDirty(actorId, root);
    }

    return changes;
  },

  pruneMajorMemory(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return 0;

    const max = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(this.getConfig(root).maxMajorActors || 12)));
    const byActorId = memory.byActorId || {};
    const ids = Object.keys(byActorId);
    if (ids.length <= max) return 0;

    ids.sort((a, b) => {
      const left = byActorId[a] || {};
      const right = byActorId[b] || {};
      return Number(right.lastUpdatedTurn || right.lastSeenTurn || 0) - Number(left.lastUpdatedTurn || left.lastSeenTurn || 0);
    });

    let changes = 0;
    for (let i = max; i < ids.length; i += 1) {
      delete byActorId[ids[i]];
      changes += 1;
    }

    if (changes > 0) {
      const keep = ids.slice(0, max);
      memory.activeIds = Array.isArray(memory.activeIds) ? memory.activeIds.filter((id) => keep.indexOf(id) !== -1) : [];
    }

    return changes;
  },

  getContextSummary(root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return "";

    const byActorId = memory.byActorId || {};
    const activeIds = Array.isArray(memory.activeIds) ? memory.activeIds : [];
    const ids = activeIds.length ? activeIds : Object.keys(byActorId).sort((a, b) => {
      const left = byActorId[a] || {};
      const right = byActorId[b] || {};
      return Number(right.lastSeenTurn || 0) - Number(left.lastSeenTurn || 0);
    }).slice(0, mode === "wide" ? 3 : 1);

    if (!ids.length) return "";

    const maxActors = mode === "wide" ? 3 : 2;
    const parts = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length && parts.length < maxActors; i += 1) {
      const summary = this.getMemorySummaryForActor(ids[i], root, mode);
      if (summary) parts.push(summary);
    }

    return parts.length ? "Major actor memory: " + parts.join(" || ") : "";
  },

  getMemorySummaryForActor(actorId, root = state.aidrpg, mode = "normal") {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return "";

    const rec = memory.byActorId[String(actorId || "")];
    if (!rec || typeof rec !== "object") return "";

    const parts = [];
    const name = rec.name || String(actorId || "actor");
    parts.push(name);

    if (rec.relationshipStance && rec.relationshipStance !== "neutral") {
      parts.push("stance: " + rec.relationshipStance);
    }

    if (Array.isArray(rec.goals) && rec.goals.length) {
      parts.push("goal: " + rec.goals[rec.goals.length - 1]);
    }

    if (Array.isArray(rec.grudges) && rec.grudges.length) {
      parts.push("grudge: " + rec.grudges[rec.grudges.length - 1]);
    }

    // C25u1 context safety: normal context has a tight budget, so keep
    // active actor memory concise. Full attitude notes still appear in wide
    // context, /actormemory, and actor card serialization.
    if ((mode === "wide" || mode === "card") && Array.isArray(rec.attitudeNotes) && rec.attitudeNotes.length) {
      parts.push("note: " + rec.attitudeNotes[rec.attitudeNotes.length - 1]);
    }

    return parts.join("; ");
  },

  markActorDirty(actorId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const U = globalThis.AIDRPG && globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils ? globalThis.AIDRPG.Utils : null;
    if (U && typeof U.markDirty === "function") {
      U.markDirty("actors", String(actorId || ""), root);
    }
  },

  pushRecentEvent(kind, name, actorId, text, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    if (!memory) return 0;

    const event = {
      turn: root && root.meta && typeof root.meta.currentTurn === "number" ? root.meta.currentTurn : 0,
      kind: String(kind || "memory"),
      actorId: String(actorId || ""),
      name: this.cleanShortText(name || actorId || "actor"),
      text: this.cleanShortText(text || "")
    };

    memory.recentEvents.push(event);
    const max = Math.max(8, Math.floor(Number(this.getConfig(root).maxEvents || 40)));
    while (memory.recentEvents.length > max) memory.recentEvents.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  getConfig(root = state.aidrpg) {
    const memory = this.init(root);
    return memory && memory.config ? memory.config : {};
  },

  getActorName(actor) {
    if (!actor || typeof actor !== "object") return "";
    const identity = actor.identity || {};
    return this.cleanShortText(identity.displayName || identity.name || actor.name || actor.id || "");
  },

  getActorReferenceLabels(actor, rec) {
    const out = [];
    const add = (value) => {
      const clean = this.cleanShortText(value);
      if (!clean || clean.length < 3) return;
      if (this.isBannedActorLabel(clean)) return;
      if (out.indexOf(clean) === -1) out.push(clean);
    };

    if (rec && rec.name) add(rec.name);
    const actorName = this.getActorName(actor);
    if (actorName) add(actorName);

    if (actor && Array.isArray(actor.roleTags)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < actor.roleTags.length && out.length < 5; i += 1) {
        add(actor.roleTags[i]);
      }
    }

    return out;
  },

  isActorReferencedInText(actor, rec, rawText) {
    const raw = String(rawText || "");
    if (!raw) return false;

    const labels = this.getActorReferenceLabels(actor, rec);
    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i += 1) {
      const label = labels[i];
      const re = new RegExp("\\b" + this.escapeRegExp(label) + "\\b", "i");
      if (re.test(raw)) return true;
    }

    return false;
  },

  isActorCurrentlyLocal(actorId, root = state.aidrpg) {
    const actors = root && root.actors ? root.actors : {};
    const localIds = Array.isArray(actors.localIds) ? actors.localIds : [];
    return localIds.indexOf(String(actorId || "")) !== -1;
  },

  isBannedActorLabel(value) {
    const lower = String(value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!lower) return true;
    if (lower.length > 80) return true;

    return new Set([
      "the", "a", "an", "someone", "somebody", "anyone", "everyone", "people",
      "crowd", "locals", "townsfolk", "patrons", "guards", "merchants", "bandits",
      "you", "your", "player", "storm", "rain", "fire", "market", "street"
    ]).has(lower);
  },

  cleanShortText(value) {
    return String(value || "")
      .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+/g, " ")
      .replace(/[<>]/g, "")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .replace(/^[\s:;,.!?-]+|[\s:;,.!?-]+$/g, "")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 160);
  },

  escapeRegExp(value) {
    return String(value || "").replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
  },

  pushCappedUnique(list, value, maxCount) {
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) return 0;
    const clean = this.cleanShortText(value);
    if (!clean) return 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) {
      if (String(list[i] || "").toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase()) return 0;
    }

    list.push(clean);
    const max = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(maxCount || 8)));
    while (list.length > max) list.shift();
    return 1;
  },

  replaceArray(target, next) {
    if (!Array.isArray(target) || !Array.isArray(next)) return 0;

    let changed = target.length !== next.length;
    if (!changed) {
      for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i += 1) {
        if (target[i] !== next[i]) {
          changed = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!changed) return 0;

    target.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < next.length; i += 1) target.push(next[i]);
    return 1;
  },

  dedupeCandidates(list) {
    const out = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    const input = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];

    for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i += 1) {
      const row = input[i];
      if (!row || typeof row !== "object") continue;
      const key = String(row.kind || "") + "|" + this.cleanShortText(row.text || "").toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      out.push(row);
    }

    return out;
  }
}
  };
})();
